* Fix the memory accounting of the writer of packed data parts after a failed memory allocation, which could make it spill to disk too much or too little. [#127375](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127375) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* This patch changes how `WindowTransform` executes window functions by introducing batched execution over sliding blocks. Also, a general window function interface will be introduced in this patch. [#127389](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127389) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* A lossy codec like `SZ3` is now rejected for `LowCardinality` columns of `MergeTree` tables, also inside `Array`, `Tuple` and `Nested` and as a subcolumn codec. Previously the table was created, but the written column could become unreadable. [#127469](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127469) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Text indexes now use the `pfor` posting list codec by default (MergeTree setting `text_index_posting_list_codec`), which makes them smaller and enables lazy posting list decoding. Parts written with `pfor` cannot be read by servers older than 26.10; set `compatibility` to an older version during a rolling upgrade to keep writing uncompressed posting lists. [#127489](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127489) ([Anton Popov](https://github.com/CurtizJ)).
* Fix huge transient values of `data_files` and `data_compressed_bytes` in `system.distribution_queue` during asynchronous inserts into `Distributed` tables. [#127551](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127551) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* This patch supports `writeFileUsingBlobWritingFunction` for disks with multiple configured locations. [#127568](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127568) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* Jemalloc heap profiles in `system.jemalloc_profile_text`, `system.jemalloc_sampled_allocations` and the Keeper `/jemalloc/profile` endpoint no longer contain runtime memory addresses nor the memory map: addresses inside the binary are offsets, others are 0. `jeprof` still analyzes them. [#127596](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127596) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* `ProfileEvents` in `system.part_log` for merges and mutations now include the counters of all threads of the operation, for example S3 reads and prefetches made by the remote reader thread pool. Time-based events, such as `RealTimeMicroseconds`, now include these threads too, so they can be larger than `duration_ms`. A merge run by `OPTIMIZE` is no longer counted twice in `system.events` and `system.metric_log`. [#127610](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127610) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* A full-definition `ATTACH TABLE` now refuses a column of type `Dynamic` or `JSON` for a table engine that does not support columns with dynamic structure (for example `Log`, `TinyLog`, `StripeLog`), the same way `CREATE TABLE` does. Previously such a table was attached and its first `INSERT` failed with a `LOGICAL_ERROR`. Existing tables still load at server startup and with a short `ATTACH TABLE`. [#127613](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127613) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The MySQL compatibility interface now reports the source database, table and column of each result column, and the display width of its type, in the column metadata. `COM_STMT_PREPARE` now returns the column definitions of the result, and `COM_STMT_RESET` is supported. This lets MySQL clients and connectors that read column metadata from the protocol see the columns of ClickHouse tables. [#127661](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127661) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The `ORC` format is now read and written by a native implementation instead of the Apache ORC C++ library, with the same behavior and the same files on output. The values are read and written directly into and from ClickHouse columns: reading is 1.4 to 2.3 times faster and writing is about 25% faster. The build does not need `protoc` for ORC anymore. Fixed writing `Date` values after 2059-09-18 to ORC, which were read back as dates before 1970. [#127690](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127690) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `text_log.level` can now be changed without a server restart; it is re-applied on configuration reload like the other logger levels. [#127718](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127718) ([Nikita Vaniasin](https://github.com/nikita-vanyasin)).
* Support the `bzip2` and `xz` codecs in the `Avro` format, both for reading and for writing (`output_format_avro_codec`). [#127774](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127774) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* By default, polls of the Paimon incremental read (`paimon_incremental_read = 1`) after the first one now deliver only `APPEND` snapshots, like Paimon's own streaming read, so a `DELETE`, `UPDATE`, `MERGE INTO` or `INSERT OVERWRITE` in Paimon no longer re-delivers the unchanged rows of the files it rewrites. The rows such commits write are not delivered, and a warning is logged for each skipped `OVERWRITE` snapshot; the new table setting `paimon_incremental_read_append_overwrite` delivers them, like Paimon's `streaming-read-append-overwrite`. A query with `paimon_target_snapshot_id` still reads the delta of any snapshot. Closes [#127546](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/127546). [#127808](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127808) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reading an Apache Paimon table no longer fails with a missing-file error when a partition value is a blank string or a `FLOAT`/`DOUBLE` value that Java prints as `2.0` or `1.0E10`, and tables created with `'partition.legacy-name' = 'false'` can now be read. Closes [#127547](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/127547). [#127812](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127812) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `EXPLAIN WHATIF` estimates hypothetical aggregate projections: the marks and rows that a query reads from the projection, and whether the optimizer chooses it. [#127825](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127825) ([Yaroslav Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Introduced server setting `stream_kafka_locks_refresh_polls` to control how often Kafka tables with offsets in Keeper re-check which partitions they own. It was hardcoded at 15 and can now be changed without a restart. [#127833](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127833) ([Aparajita Pandey](https://github.com/aparajita31pandey)).
* When a system log flush (e.g. into `system.query_log` or `system.session_log`) reaches `max_server_memory_usage`, it now asks the memory overcommit tracker to stop the running query that exceeds its soft limit the most and waits up to `flush_overcommit_wait_time_microseconds` (5 s by default, configurable per system log or for all of them in `system_tables`) instead of failing right away and dropping the batch of log entries. Set it to `0` to keep the previous behavior. [#128146](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128146) ([Christoph Wurm](https://github.com/cwurm)).
* Batch-serialized `LowCardinality(String)` `GROUP BY` keys store their string sizes in little-endian byte order, as every reader of that format expects. This is needed for `GROUP BY` over several `LowCardinality(String)` columns on big-endian platforms (s390x). Related to [#128050](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128050). [#128159](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128159) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A merge or mutation that fails on one S3 read now stops its other S3 reads that are still in flight. They used to keep retrying to their retry budget (about 41 minutes per object with default settings) while the failed task held its source parts and the table, so `DROP TABLE` waited for it and an `OPTIMIZE` (also `OPTIMIZE ... DRY RUN PARTS`) that ran the merge did not return. [#128185](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128185) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add the column `used_data_skipping_indices` to `system.query_log` and the ProfileEvents `GranulesDroppedByPartitionKey`, `GranulesDroppedByPrimaryKey` and `GranulesDroppedBySkipIndexes`, which show which data skipping indexes a query applied and how many marks each index layer excluded. [#128223](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128223) ([Yaroslav Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Paimon `DATE` columns are now read as `Date32` instead of `Date`, like Iceberg and Delta Lake, so dates before 1970-01-01 or after 2149-06-06 are read instead of failing with `VALUE_IS_OUT_OF_RANGE_OF_DATA_TYPE`. `DESCRIBE` and `toTypeName` now report `Date32`. Closes [#128306](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128306). [#128329](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128329) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Paimon tables with a `ROW` column can now be read: a `ROW` is read as a named `Tuple`, like an Iceberg or Delta Lake `struct`, and a `NULL` `ROW` as a tuple of its fields' default values (`NULL` for nullable fields). Before, every query on such a table failed with `Unsupported Paimon type: ROW`, even one that did not read the `ROW` column. Closes [#128307](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128307). [#128330](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128330) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The native Parquet reader now ignores column chunk and page statistics whose `min_value` is greater than `max_value` (for example the `UInt32` / `UInt64` statistics that polars 0.20 writes in signed order) instead of failing the query with `INCORRECT_DATA`, so such files can be filtered and aggregated. Closes [#128302](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128302). [#128336](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128336) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Iceberg tables can now be created and altered with `Bool` columns, stored as Iceberg `boolean`, and with `Int8`, `UInt8`, `Int16` and `UInt16` columns, widened to Iceberg `int` and read back as `Int32`. A `Bool` partition value is now written to manifests as an Avro `boolean`, and `DELETE` and `DROP PARTITION` match the boolean partition values written by other tools and by older ClickHouse versions. Closes [#128303](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128303). [#128338](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128338) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A sparse column whose values are truncated in data sent over the native protocol is now rejected with `INCORRECT_DATA` instead of `LOGICAL_ERROR`. [#128363](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128363) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reading a malformed `LowCardinality` column (for example from `Native` input) whose dictionary declares more keys than the data contains now fails with `CANNOT_READ_ALL_DATA` instead of a `LOGICAL_ERROR` or an attempt to allocate terabytes of memory. [#128366](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128366) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The arguments of a table function the server does not know, such as a misspelled `s3Clustr(...)` or `deltaLake(...)` in a build without it, are now hidden in the server log (including the error line's `(in query: ...)`) and in `system.query_log`, like the arguments of an unknown table engine. So is the first list of a call like `s3(...)(1)`, also for known names. A parameterized view call (`pv(n = 15)`) shows only its parameter names there. Closes [#128372](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128372). [#128391](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128391) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Queries reading Iceberg and Delta Lake tables report the total number of rows to read (`total_rows_approx` in `system.processes`, progress in the native and HTTP protocols) from the row counts of the data files in the table metadata, so that the progress of such queries can be shown. [#128401](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128401) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Improved keeper timeout error messages to include the configured threshold values and the relevant `coordination_settings` knobs, and added logging when a client-requested session timeout is adjusted by the server during the handshake — on both the keeper side and the ClickHouse keeper-client side. [#128427](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128427) ([YenchangChan](https://github.com/YenchangChan)).
* `clickhouse compressor --decompress` reports a corrupted block as `CORRUPTED_DATA` naming the codec again, instead of the internal error `The secret arguments finder is not installed`. [#128474](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128474) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reading a `LowCardinality` column from a corrupted data part whose indexes point past the dictionary now throws `INCORRECT_DATA` instead of reading past the end of the keys, which could crash the server. [#128476](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128476) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `parseDateTimeBestEffort` now parses a month name followed by a dash or a slash, such as `'Nov-08-2024'` or `'Nov/08/2024'`, like `'Nov 08 2024'`, instead of reading `-08` as a time zone offset. [#128554](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128554) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Text formats (`TSV`, `CSV`, `JSONEachRow`, `LineAsString`, ...) read from data compressed with `snappy_mode = 'framed'` with `input_format_parallel_parsing = 0` no longer read past the end of the decompression buffer. [#128567](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128567) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Distributed reads of Delta Lake tables (`deltaLakeCluster`, parallel replicas) also report the total number of rows to read from the row counts in the table metadata. [#128571](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128571) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Don't do s3 requests to get metadata in delta tables. [#128604](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128604) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* Initialize parquet reader a bit earlier. [#128607](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128607) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* Paimon tables can now be read when a data file was written before a column was renamed, or before a column was dropped and another one with the same name was added: the columns of each data file are matched to the current schema by Paimon field id, as Spark does. Such reads failed with `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` before. [#128620](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128620) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `CREATE TABLE` in a `DataLakeCatalog` database now registers the Iceberg table location as a full URI (for example `s3://bucket/path/`), so a REST catalog that writes table files through its own S3 client accepts the table without `write_full_path_in_iceberg_metadata`. Closes [#128219](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128219). [#128636](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128636) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `ReplicatedMergeTree` now removes old deduplication blocks from Keeper in batches of `TryRemove` requests instead of one `Remove` request per block. This reduces the number of Raft entries in Keeper for tables with many replicas. [#128645](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128645) ([Antonio Andelic](https://github.com/antonio2368)).
* Apache Paimon primary-key tables can now be read where Paimon reads them without merging: buckets whose data files are all above level 0, are known to have no retract rows and are in one level, and, for tables with `deletion-vectors.enabled` or `'merge-engine' = 'first-row'`, buckets in several levels (their level-0 files are not read until compacted, as in Paimon). Other buckets still throw `NOT_IMPLEMENTED`, since merge-on-read is not implemented; a full compaction makes them readable. Data files that Paimon re-adds under the same name (compaction to another level, Flink's `rewrite_file_index`) are no longer dropped from the read of any Paimon table. [#128686](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128686) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The error message for `DELETE` on a table with projections now says that `lightweight_mutation_projection_mode` is a table-level `MergeTree` setting and shows how to change it with `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY SETTING`. [#128701](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128701) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* This patch improves the performance of the argument preparation phase during window transform calculation. [#128726](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128726) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* This feature will reduce the number of manifest files written when writting across multiple partition. Today ClickHouse writes one file per partition. This might lead to a less optimal metadata layout. ClickHouse will instead write only one manifest files but with a partition breakdown within the file. [#128880](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128880) ([Melvyn Peignon](https://github.com/melvynator)).
* Queries that read a subcolumn or a virtual column of a `MaterializedPostgreSQL` table before any row is replicated (for example `x IS NULL` or `count(x)` rewritten by `optimize_functions_to_subcolumns`, an explicit `x.null`, or `_part`) now work instead of failing with `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK`. [#128953](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128953) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed memory freed by the server on macOS staying reserved by the system, outside the server's own memory usage, until the server restarted. It could grow to tens of GiB under heavy query load. [#129016](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129016) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* `clickhouse-local` and `clickhouse-client` on macOS now load command-line suggestions in a thread with an 8 MiB stack, like their thread-pool threads, so the suggestion query of an interactive `clickhouse-local` can no longer overflow the 512 KiB default thread stack during JIT compilation. [#129026](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129026) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Merges and mutations now respect `filesystem_prefetches_limit` and `filesystem_prefetch_max_memory_usage`, so a merge of a wide table on remote storage no longer takes the whole remote reader thread pool. [#129042](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129042) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Relative paths of local Delta Lake tables (`deltaLakeLocal`, `DeltaLakeLocal`) are supported with the delta-kernel-rs reader, e.g. in `clickhouse-local` they are relative to the current directory. [#129102](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129102) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Introspection functions and `system.symbols` work only with offsets inside the main binary: `addressToSymbol` no longer accepts absolute addresses, `system.symbols` no longer lists symbols of shared libraries, and `flameGraph` requires the `INTROSPECTION` grant. gRPC library logs are no longer written, and the `grpc.verbose_logs` setting is removed (it is ignored if present). [#129112](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129112) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Support reading GeoParquet 1.1 files with geometry columns in the native (GeoArrow) encodings `point`, `linestring`, `polygon`, `multipoint`, `multilinestring` and `multipolygon` (in the `Parquet` and `Arrow` formats). Previously such files could not be read. [#129121](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129121) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix double counting in the network charts on the dashboard.html. [#129201](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129201) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* `SYSTEM SYNC REPLICA` and `OPTIMIZE` of a `ReplicatedMergeTree` table now stop waiting for the replication queue when the query is killed, when the server shuts down, or when `max_execution_time` is exceeded. Before, they kept waiting until `receive_timeout` (300 seconds by default), which also delayed server shutdown when such a query ran on the distributed DDL worker. [#129283](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129283) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Allow jemalloc percpu arenas even if CPU counts are non deterministic. [#129308](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129308) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Added MergeTree setting `empty_parts_lifetime`: the removal of empty parts (left by `TRUNCATE`, `DROP PARTITION`, `DROP PART`, TTL or a mutation) now waits until such a part is at least that many seconds old. The default `0` keeps removing empty parts right away. [#129309](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129309) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Better compatibility with S3-compatible storages that reject `CopyObject` of an empty object (for example, RustFS): copying an empty object, such as removing an empty file from a `plain_rewritable` disk, now falls back to a regular upload. A rejected `CopyObject` is retried as a multipart copy only when multipart copy is allowed (`s3_allow_multipart_copy`) and the source is larger than 5 MB. Closes [#129218](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/129218). [#129316](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129316) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Automatic parallel replicas can now enable parallel replicas for queries that use direct read from a text index. [#129339](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129339) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* `QueryRunner` tables in cluster mode now support `SQL SECURITY` when the cluster uses an interserver secret. [#129344](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129344) ([Miсhael Stetsyuk](https://github.com/mstetsyuk)).
* With `iceberg_use_version_hint`, the metadata file named by `metadata/version-hint.text` is now resolved through the Iceberg metadata cache, so `iceberg_metadata_staleness_ms` applies to it as it already did to the listing-based and catalog-based lookup of the latest metadata file. Within the staleness window a read of such a table performs no object storage request to find its current metadata file. [#129349](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129349) ([Melvyn Peignon](https://github.com/melvynator)).
* With the new setting `use_iceberg_table_statistics_ndv` (on top of `use_iceberg_manifest_statistics`), join reordering and the condition estimates of an Iceberg read take the number of distinct values of a column from the table statistics in `metadata.json` (the `ndv` of an `apache-datasketches-theta-v1` blob, as written by Spark `compute_table_stats` or Trino `ANALYZE`) when the manifest files give no count for it. Only the blobs computed from the snapshot that is read are used. Closes [#129260](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/129260). [#129360](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129360) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Read Parquet footers of bucket splitting through `ParquetMetadataCache`. [#129389](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129389) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* Send the buckets of a file to the same replica. [#129450](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129450) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* With `optimize_aggregation_in_order = 1`, `uniqExact(x)` and `count(DISTINCT x)` without `GROUP BY` over a table sorted by `x` count the values in the order of the table, in parallel over ranges of the key, without keeping them in memory. The order of aggregation is also chosen after the projections, so a projection that reads fewer granules is no longer skipped because of `optimize_aggregation_in_order`. [#129508](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129508) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* A compression level that `gzip`, `deflate`, `xz` or a `BACKUP` to a zip archive does not accept (for example `output_format_compression_level = 18` for a `.gz` file or for gzip-compressed Iceberg metadata) now fails with `Compression level 18 is out of range for gzip` instead of a codec library message such as `deflateInit2 failed: stream error`. A rejected `bz2` level or `zstd_window_log_max` is now named in the error, for example `zstd_stream_decoder init failed for zstd_window_log_max = 9: Parameter is out of bound`. `bz2` and `xz` errors describe the library status (for example `Not a bzip2 stream (BZ_DATA_ERROR_MAGIC)`) instead of a number. Error codes are unchanged. [#129519](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129519) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a rare hang of a query reading a Delta Lake table when listing its data files fails. [#129547](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129547) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Kafka logs no longer contain memory addresses: pointer-valued librdkafka properties are logged as `[HIDDEN]` and hexadecimal numbers in librdkafka messages are hidden. The librdkafka testing properties `test.*` are no longer accepted in the Kafka configuration. [#129556](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129556) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* A namespace listing from an Iceberg REST catalog that contains an empty namespace entry, or a top-level namespace with an empty name, now fails with `DATALAKE_DATABASE_ERROR` on the first response. Previously the empty entry threw `LOGICAL_ERROR`, and the empty top-level name re-listed the root namespace until `TOO_DEEP_RECURSION`. Closes [#129521](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/129521). [#129563](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129563) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An S3 disk or query whose `s3_strict_upload_part_size` is greater than `s3_max_upload_part_size` is now rejected up front with `INVALID_SETTING_VALUE`, as Azure already does. Before, such a configuration was accepted and every write larger than `max(s3_max_single_part_upload_size, s3_max_upload_part_size)` failed with the logical error `Part size exceeded max_upload_part_size`. To keep such a configuration working, lower `s3_strict_upload_part_size` or raise `s3_max_upload_part_size`. [#129578](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129578) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The HDFS client no longer sends a memory address of the server as part of its client name to the HDFS NameNode. [#129607](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129607) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Allow distributed joins to broadcast small aggregated inputs by preserving row-count estimates through `MergingAggregated`. [#129669](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129669) ([zoomxi](https://github.com/zoomxi)).
* Make the `ALP` codec GA. [#129732](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129732) ([Raufs Dunamalijevs](https://github.com/rienath)).
* On macOS, system tables, exception messages and fatal error reports no longer contain runtime memory addresses: stack frames inside the main binary are stored as offsets in it and other frames as 0, as on Linux. `system.symbols` lists only symbols of the main binary, and `addressToSymbol`/`addressToLine` accept only offsets. [#129735](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129735) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Improve "Filesystem cache overview" dashboard in system.dashboards. [#129736](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129736) ([Kseniia Sumarokova](https://github.com/kssenii)).
* Keeper no longer prints memory addresses in its Raft logs. [#129739](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129739) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Reading a malformed `uniqTheta` state no longer allocates the sketch size it declares before the bytes arrive, so a few bytes of state cannot make a query allocate 1 GiB. [#129746](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129746) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* With `s3_strict_upload_part_size` set, an S3 disk, an `s3` query or an S3 backup whose `s3_min_upload_part_size` is zero or greater than `s3_max_upload_part_size` is now rejected up front with `INVALID_SETTING_VALUE`, as Azure already does. Before, such a configuration was accepted and a native copy (for example a `BACKUP` to S3 or a copy between S3 disks) failed later with `INVALID_CONFIG_PARAMETER`. To keep such a configuration working, set `s3_min_upload_part_size` to a non-zero value not greater than `s3_max_upload_part_size`. [#129747](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129747) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A malformed `stochasticLinearRegression` or `stochasticLogisticRegression` state that declares more weights, gradient values or weights-updater values than its type implies is now rejected with `TOO_LARGE_ARRAY_SIZE` before the vector is allocated, instead of allocating up to 8 GiB and then failing with `MEMORY_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` or `CANNOT_READ_ALL_DATA`. [#129751](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129751) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `max_parser_depth` now also limits the nesting of array, tuple and map literals in dictionary arguments (such as `SOURCE(CLICKHOUSE(HOST [[...]]))`), in `SET param_<name>` values, and in literals with alternating brackets (such as `[([(1)])]`), as it already does for other literals. The standalone WebAssembly parser now returns a parse error for such a literal instead of running out of the engine stack. Closes [#123169](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123169). [#129765](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129765) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `arrayJoin` function is planned as an `ARRAY JOIN` step also with `query_plan_enable_optimizations = 0`, so it behaves the same way with and without query plan optimizations. [#129780](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129780) ([Yaroslav Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Add setting `automatic_parallel_replicas_statistics_drift_ratio` (default 1.5, previously a fixed 2): how much the rows a read selects may differ from the ones its statistics were collected on before automatic parallel replicas collects the statistics again. [#129809](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129809) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* The Iceberg writer now supports `lower_bounds`/`upper_bounds` (excluding NaN and ordering `-0.0` before `+0.0`, as the Iceberg spec requires) and `nan_value_counts` for `Float32` and `Float64` columns, so Iceberg readers can prune data files by float columns. [#129812](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129812) ([Daria Zhukova](https://github.com/cherepasshka)).
* A `DataLakeCatalog` database with `catalog_type = 'unity'` now reports `Unsupported DeltaLake type` instead of `std::out_of_range` when the catalog returns a column whose `type_json` is a single `"`, and `system.zookeeper_info` now reports `CANNOT_PARSE_NUMBER` instead of `std::out_of_range` when the `srvr` reply of a ZooKeeper server has a `Zxid` without the `0x` prefix or without digits after it. [#129820](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129820) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A `MOVE PART ... TO SHARD` whose part is removed (for example by `TRUNCATE`) before it is attached on the destination shard now reports `NO_SUCH_DATA_PART` in `system.part_moves_between_shards` and can be cancelled with `KILL PART_MOVE_TO_SHARD`, instead of crashing the server. [#129832](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129832) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A malformed `REPLICATED`-serialized column sent over the native protocol whose element count is implausibly large is now rejected with `TOO_LARGE_ARRAY_SIZE` before any memory is allocated for it, instead of failing with a `LOGICAL_ERROR` from the allocator. [#129932](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129932) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reading Parquet files uses less memory on servers with less than about 4.4 GiB of memory limit, so that `INSERT ... SELECT FROM file('*.parquet')` into a wide table does not fail with `MEMORY_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` on a small server. [#130049](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130049) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Writing wide parts of a table with many columns uses adaptive write buffers when the preallocated buffers would take a significant share of the server memory limit, so that inserts and merges into such tables fit into the memory of a small server. Controlled by the new `MergeTree` setting `max_memory_ratio_to_activate_adaptive_write_buffer`; set it to `0` for the previous behavior. [#130050](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130050) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The cap of `min_insert_block_size_bytes` by the server memory limit is configurable with the new setting `insert_squashing_max_memory_ratio` (default `0.1125`, as before). Lower it so that an `INSERT` into a wide table fits into the memory of a small server, or set it to `0` to disable the cap. [#130052](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130052) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Update `jemalloc_internal_defs.h.in` for s390x to align with other platforms. [#130053](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130053) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Iceberg v3 `variant` columns in Avro data files can now be read: an Avro record of two `bytes` fields, `metadata` and `value`, is decoded with the variant binary encoding when it is read as `Dynamic` or `JSON`, as for Parquet. Closes [#129998](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/129998). [#130078](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130078) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reading an Iceberg table whose schema has no fields now fails with a clear error naming the schema-id (`EMPTY_LIST_OF_COLUMNS_PASSED`) instead of the misleading `Schema with id N is unknown`. [#130121](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130121) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Iceberg tables with columns of the v3 type `unknown` can be read with the `iceberg*` table functions: such a column is read as `Nullable(Nothing)` (always `NULL`), also after its type is promoted, and a required `unknown` field is rejected as a specification violation. Previously reading such a table failed with `Unknown Iceberg type: unknown`. [#130129](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130129) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Iceberg table metadata files are checked against the fields that the specification requires for their format version: a missing or mistyped required field (e.g. a `snapshot-id` of `9876543210123456789`, which does not fit `long`) or a `format-version` below 1 gives `ICEBERG_SPECIFICATION_VIOLATION` naming the field and the file, instead of `POCO_EXCEPTION` or reading the invalid table. A file the JSON parser rejects (e.g. an integer below `-2^63`) gives the same error naming the file. [#130132](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130132) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reserve the size of the spilled table for external GROUP BY, not the query memory. [#130144](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130144) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Iceberg tables whose metadata schemas violate the Iceberg specification are now rejected with `ICEBERG_SPECIFICATION_VIOLATION` naming the field, as the Java implementation does: an `identifier-field-ids` entry that is not a required primitive field outside lists, maps and optional structs, a list or map without `element-id`, `key-id` or `value-id`, a `geometry` or `geography` type with an empty CRS or an unknown edge interpolation algorithm, and a schema whose `type` is not `struct`. `ALTER` refuses to drop an identifier column or make it `Nullable`, and the Iceberg REST catalog server refuses such a schema on create. [#130157](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130157) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A graceful server shutdown no longer waits for the next collection of `system.metric_log`, `system.error_log` and `system.aggregated_zookeeper_log`. Each of them could delay the shutdown by almost its `collect_interval_milliseconds`, about 3 seconds in total with the default configuration. [#130193](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130193) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `Vortex` output format now writes `String` columns as `Utf8` instead of `Binary`, which Vortex compresses much better: the `URL` column of ClickBench's `hits_0.parquet` takes 24.9 MB instead of 47.0 MB. A value that is not valid UTF-8 throws an exception; the new setting `output_format_vortex_string_as_string` (enabled by default) can be disabled to write such data as `Binary`. Closes [#130209](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130209). [#130234](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130234) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An `INSERT` into an Iceberg table now keeps the upper bound of a `Nullable` column in the data file when one block of the insert holds only `NULL` in that column. Closes [#129827](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/129827). [#130296](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130296) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Promote adaptive codec selection to Beta. It is still enabled per table with the `enable_adaptive_codec_selection` setting, and the sampling period is still controlled by `adaptive_codec_selection_period`. [#130321](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130321) ([Raufs Dunamalijevs](https://github.com/rienath)).
* `ALTER TABLE ... DROP PARTITION` and `DETACH PARTITION` on a `MergeTree` table now stop and wait only for the merges and mutations of the dropped partition. Dropping an old partition no longer aborts a long merge of the current partition, and no longer fails with `Timeout while waiting for already running merges` because of merges in other partitions. Background merge selection now skips a partition that is being dropped or replaced, and `DROP`, `DETACH` and `REPLACE PARTITION` wait for a merge of the partition until its transaction is committed. [#130334](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130334) ([mishel-gc](https://github.com/mishel-gc)).
* `IN` subqueries cached with `query_cache_for_subqueries` can now recover when the speculative set build for primary key analysis stops early, instead of failing with "Not-ready Set". [#130375](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130375) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Queries with `query_cache_for_subqueries = 1` no longer fail with `Method serialize is not implemented for StreamInQueryResultCache` under parallel replicas with `serialize_query_plan = 1`. [#130376](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130376) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Settings `grace_hash_join_initial_buckets` and `grace_hash_join_max_buckets` are no longer experimental and can be changed without enabling experimental features. [#130494](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130494) ([János Benjamin Antal](https://github.com/antaljanosbenjamin)).
* The setting `use_iceberg_manifest_statistics`, which estimates Iceberg reads for join reordering from their manifest files, is renamed to `use_datalake_metadata_statistics`, so that it can cover other data lake formats later. The setting was not released under the old name, so the old name is not kept as an alias. [#130535](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130535) ([Antonio Filipovic](https://github.com/antoniofilipovic)).
* Enable jemalloc's safety checks: deallocations with a wrong size, double frees of large allocations and inconsistent allocator metadata now abort at the faulty call instead of corrupting the allocator state. [#130612](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130612) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* A new or recovering replica of a `Replicated` database now restores tables that use the experimental `Elasticsearch` table engine or `elasticsearch` table function, instead of failing its initialization with `SUPPORT_IS_DISABLED`. [#130681](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130681) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `TRUNCATE TABLE`, `ALTER TABLE ... DROP PART` and `ALTER TABLE ... DROP PARTITION` of a `MergeTree` table now stop retrying when the query is killed, when the server shuts down, or when `max_execution_time` is exceeded, while an outdated part with the name of their new empty part waits to be removed. Before, they retried for up to `lock_acquire_timeout`, which also delayed server shutdown when such a query ran on the distributed DDL worker. [#130696](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130696) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An `INNER UUID` clause of a `TimeSeries` table target now rejects the Nil UUID with `BAD_ARGUMENTS`, as `TO INNER UUID` already does, instead of silently ignoring the clause. [#130698](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130698) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The Prometheus HTTP handlers (metrics exposition, remote write, remote read, query API) now accept the OpenTelemetry `traceparent` and `tracestate` headers and record a server span in `system.opentelemetry_span_log`, like the main HTTP interface. [#130782](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130782) ([Sergey Veletskiy](https://github.com/velom)).
* `system.tables` no longer reads the object storage to fill `total_rows` and `total_bytes` of a table that reads a data lake table through another engine (`Alias`, `Merge`, `Buffer`, a lazily loaded table, `CREATE TABLE ... AS icebergS3(...)`). They are reported as `NULL`, as they already are for the data lake table itself, so a `system.tables` scan no longer stalls on an unreachable bucket. [#130797](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130797) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A value of `cluster_function_wait_for_replica_ms` too large for the deadline arithmetic (about 292 years or more) is now honoured as the longest possible wait, instead of overflowing, which skipped the wait. Closes [#126101](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126101). [#130812](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130812) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* ORC files with long string values are smaller and faster to read: string statistics keep at most 1024 bytes, as in the Java ORC writer. [#130923](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130923) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Add ProfileEvents `CompressedWriteBufferBlocks`, `CompressedWriteBufferBytes` and `CompressedWriteBufferCompressMicroseconds`, describing the compression of written data. [#130953](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130953) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The built-in Web UI pages `/binary` and `/jemalloc` no longer load JavaScript and CSS from third-party CDNs: Leaflet and uPlot are embedded into the server, as for the other pages. The CDN links kept for opening the pages as local files have subresource integrity. [#131051](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131051) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Add the `allow_ai_functions` setting, which disables AI functions when set to `0`. [#131120](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131120) ([George Larionov](https://github.com/george-larionov)).
* The Web UI (`/play`) now follows the dark theme in native controls and scrollbars, applies the theme before the page renders, uses native buttons and a popover for the download menu, and enforces Trusted Types to guard against DOM XSS. [#131141](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131141) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* A query parameter such as `{t:Identifier}` in the name of a `TimeSeries` target table (`SAMPLES`, `TAGS`, `METRIC FAMILIES`, ...) is now rejected with a syntax error. The parameter was never substituted there: such a statement failed with a misleading `UNKNOWN_TABLE` error, used the current database for `{d:Identifier}.name`, or with a `UUID` gave a query that could not be formatted back. Closes [#130928](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130928). [#131187](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131187) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A Hive partition value that does not fit the type of its partition column (for example `k0=300` for `k0 UInt8`) now gives a `TYPE_MISMATCH` error that names the value, the path and the column, instead of `BAD_GET` (or a `LOGICAL_ERROR` for `LowCardinality`). Closes [#131087](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131087). [#131202](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131202) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An inline disk definition (`SETTINGS disk = disk(...)`) is now rejected with a clear error message by the `Log`, `TinyLog`, `StripeLog`, `Set` and `Join` table engines, the data lake table engines and table functions, and the `DataLakeCatalog` database engine, instead of the internal `Bad get: has CustomType, requested String` error. Only the `MergeTree` family table engines and the `Atomic` and `Ordinary` database engines support inline disk definitions. Closes [#131082](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131082). [#131205](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131205) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `CREATE TABLE` without engine arguments in a `DataLakeCatalog` database now treats a namespace location that is not a URI, such as the `not_used` placeholder Databricks Unity Catalog reports for a schema of managed tables, as no location, so the catalog chooses the table location. Previously the query failed with `Unexpected location format` (`NOT_IMPLEMENTED`). Closes [#131164](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131164). [#131238](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131238) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Added the `quick_startup` server setting, which skips the optional startup work (`mlock` and remapping of the executable, its integrity check, and the checks of the environment). With a cold page cache, this cuts the server startup time from about 5.5 s to 2.3 s on a cloud VM with a network disk. [#131257](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131257) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `EXPLAIN ANALYZE` now prints the q-error of the estimated rows of each join input next to `rows estimated` and `rows`; for a filtered table read this is the q-error of its filter estimate. [#131313](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131313) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Hints in `clickhouse-client` no longer blink while typing: each redraw of the prompt is sent to the terminal at once, as a synchronized update where the terminal supports it. [#131327](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131327) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* The install script (`curl https://clickhouse.com/ | sh`) downloads the `master` build by default again. `CLICKHOUSE_BRANCH=latest` selects the newest release branch, and `CLICKHOUSE_BRANCH=26.10` selects a specific one. [#131339](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131339) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The `ZXC` compression codec is promoted from experimental to beta. It is still enabled with the `enable_zxc_codec` setting, which servers with `allow_feature_tier` set to 1 or 2 now allow. [#131397](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131397) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add the setting `enable_parallel_single_level_merge_by_index`, enabled by default for the index-based merge of the adaptive aggregator's single-level hash tables. Disabling it makes the parallel merge of `GROUP BY` hash tables with the adaptive aggregator (`enable_adaptive_aggregator`) scan every cell of every table for each partition instead of reading only the cells of each partition through the indexes of the tables, which can be faster and take less memory. [#131549](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131549) ([Nihal Z. Miaji](https://github.com/nihalzp)).
* Inside a transaction, an `INSERT` whose data is sent over the native protocol as blocks (such as `INSERT ... FROM INFILE`) with async inserts enabled now fails with `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` like `INSERT ... VALUES`, instead of being committed outside the transaction. With `throw_on_unsupported_query_inside_transaction = 0` it runs synchronously inside the transaction. [#131703](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131703) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Iceberg `ALTER TABLE ... EXECUTE remove_orphan_files` on a local or HDFS table now keeps the files the table references when the table path, the `location` argument or the metadata spells them with `//` or `./`, or when the table path is relative. [#131781](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131781) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Iceberg `ALTER TABLE ... EXECUTE expire_snapshots` now treats variants of one storage path (`s3a://` vs `s3://`, a `file://` prefix, `//` or `./` on a local or HDFS table) as one file, so it no longer deletes a file a retained snapshot still uses. [#131782](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131782) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `SELECT DISTINCT` no longer copies a constant column to every row of a block when all `DISTINCT` columns are constants, or when the `DISTINCT` is done in order (`optimize_distinct_in_order`). For example, `SELECT DISTINCT toFixedString('a', 1048577) FROM numbers(100000)` no longer requests 64 GiB of memory with `max_block_size = 65409`. [#131870](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131870) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* With `filesystem_isolation`, the working directories, the caches and the TLS certificates of `distributed_cache_server` sections, and the certificate chain of `distributed_cache_client`, stay visible. [#131885](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131885) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Chdig [v26.11.1](https://github.com/azat/chdig/releases/tag/v26.11.1). [#131929](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131929) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* `clickhouse-client` now clears the values of `--jwt`, `--ssh-key-passphrase`, `--one-time-password` and `--oauth-client-secret` from its command line, as it already did for `--password`, so they are no longer visible in `ps` and `/proc/<pid>/cmdline`. `clickhouse-keeper-client` now does the same for `--password` and `--identity`. Closes [#131191](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131191). [#131963](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131963) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* More efficient processed node lookup in S3Queue for ordered partition mode. [#131980](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131980) ([Bharat Nallan](https://github.com/bharatnc)).
* In filesystem cache SLRU eviction policy remove blocking lock in priority increase. Closes [#131932](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131932). [#132008](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132008) ([Kseniia Sumarokova](https://github.com/kssenii)).
* A forged `groupFormat` aggregate function state that declares more than 1e9 rows is now rejected with `TOO_LARGE_ARRAY_SIZE` before anything is allocated for it, instead of failing with `LOGICAL_ERROR` from the allocator. Closes [#130418](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130418). [#132013](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132013) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `FQDN` function (alias `fullHostName`) returns a constant like `hostName`, so a condition on it can use the primary key. [#132018](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132018) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* A deferred asynchronous insert flush that fails because the submitting user was dropped or its authentication method expired (`VALID UNTIL`) is now recorded in `system.asynchronous_insert_log` (`FlushError`) and `system.query_log`, like other failed flushes. Closes [#131654](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131654). [#132084](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132084) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* With `merge_use_batch_sorting_queue` enabled, merges of normal projection parts, including the temporary parts written while a projection is built, now also use the batch sorting queue. With the setting enabled, merged projection parts can be split into blocks differently than on previous versions. The data is the same, but in a `ReplicatedMergeTree` cluster with replicas on different versions this can show up as a harmless "not byte-identical" checksum mismatch, after which the replica downloads the merged part from another replica. [#132085](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132085) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* S3 server-side copies (`UploadPartCopy` and `CopyObject`, used by native `BACKUP`/`RESTORE` and by copies between S3 disks) now wait for their response for `request_timeout_ms` instead of the 3 second first-attempt timeout of `s3_use_adaptive_timeouts`, so a part that takes longer than 3 seconds to copy is no longer sent twice. Closes [#131965](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131965). [#132092](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132092) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Table function `url` and other readers over HTTP no longer retry a response that violates the HTTP protocol or the header limits, which made such queries wait for minutes before failing. Retries of `HEAD` requests are now logged. [#132117](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132117) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Allow writing `Variant` columns to Parquet `VARIANT` when several members share the same Parquet encoding, such as `Variant(UInt32, Int64)`, if one of them can hold the values of the others. [#132321](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132321) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* The `url` table function no longer retries a request when the response header exceeds `http_max_fields`, `http_max_field_name_size` or `http_max_field_value_size`, and the error names these settings. [#132325](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132325) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Errors of `INSERT` and `TRUNCATE` into a read-only `S3`, `AzureBlobStorage` or other object storage table now name the table instead of repeating its path, consistently with the `File` engine. [#132333](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132333) ([Arsen Muk](https://github.com/arsenmuk)).
* Skip S3Queue registry polling for ordered and exclusive modes. [#132339](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132339) ([Bharat Nallan](https://github.com/bharatnc)).
* The watchdog now writes `Child process was terminated by signal ...` (or `exited normally with code ...`) to the server log before it exits. With the asynchronous logger, the default, this line was lost, so a server killed by the OOM killer or by a signal left no trace of it in the log. [#132409](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132409) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `MaterializedPostgreSQL` database engine no longer puts the `INCLUDE` columns of a PostgreSQL primary key or replica identity index into the sorting key of the replicated table, so updates and deletes of such rows replace the old row instead of leaving it visible, and a table with a nullable `INCLUDE` column is replicated instead of failing with `ILLEGAL_COLUMN`. Closes [#128270](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128270). [#132653](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132653) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Backported in [#134128](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/134128): Added the `hostname` column to `system.dead_letter_queue`. [#133849](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/133849) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).

#### Bug Fix (user-visible misbehavior in an official stable release)
* Fixed `max_rows_to_read_leaf` being enforced on local non-distributed queries, causing unexpected `TOO_MANY_ROWS` errors when reading through views. [#99646](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/99646) ([Yash ](https://github.com/Onyx2406)).
* Fix the `remote_url_allow_hosts` check for MySQL dictionary sources validating the wrong port when a `REPLICA(...)` clause has no `PORT` of its own: the check used the default port 3306 while the connection used the top-level `PORT`, so an allow-list entry pinned to a specific port could be bypassed. [#109736](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109736) ([Shaohua Wang](https://github.com/tiandiwonder)).
* Access entities stored on local disk (users, roles, grants, quotas, row policies, settings profiles created via SQL) are now fsync'd when `fsync_metadata` is enabled, so an acknowledged `CREATE`/`ALTER`/`DROP USER` (and `GRANT`/`REVOKE`) is no longer silently lost on power loss. Previously `DiskAccessStorage` wrote and removed the `.sql` entity files without any fsync. [#111346](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111346) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed silent integer corruption when inserting into a `JSON`/`Dynamic` column via the Field-insertion path (e.g. `INSERT ... VALUES` with a `CAST` to `JSON`/`Array(JSON)`). If a dynamic path was first seen with a small integer (e.g. `50`, inferred as `Int8`), a larger later value (e.g. `1944`) was silently wrapped into that narrow type (stored as `-104`) instead of widening it. Text formats such as `JSONEachRow` were not affected. [#111354](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111354) ([tinydaniel](https://github.com/tinydaniel)).
* Fixed projections returning rows removed by a lightweight `DELETE` after `ALTER UPDATE`, `MATERIALIZE PROJECTION` or `CLEAR COLUMN` rebuilt them on a Wide part. A query with `apply_deleted_mask = 0` no longer uses projections when the table has lightweight-deleted rows, since projections do not store them. On release branches this also fixes a normal projection without a `WHERE` keeping the rows removed by a `rebuild`-mode lightweight `DELETE`, which [#118375](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/118375) fixed on master. [#111809](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111809) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `DataLakeCatalog` databases can now read tables whose catalog location is on HDFS (`hdfs://`), including with the default `vended_credentials` setting. Reading requires the same `READ ON HDFS` grant as `icebergHDFS` (filters match the HDFS authority, e.g. `hdfs://namenode:9000`). Such tables are read-only; writes fail with `READONLY`. [#112468](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/112468) ([saad-imc](https://github.com/SaadIMC)).
* Reject `WITH FILL` bounds and generated values that do not fit the type of the `ORDER BY` column, and stop `WITH FILL` at a step that overflows instead of generating wrapped-around, non-monotonic, or non-terminating output. [#112650](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/112650) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a signed integer overflow when computing the next retry time of a refreshable materialized view with a very large `refresh_retry_max_backoff_ms` or `refresh_retry_initial_backoff_ms`. The overflowed instant landed in the past, so the view refreshed on every pass rather than waiting. [#113007](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113007) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `accurateCast`, `accurateCastOrNull` and `accurateCastOrDefault` from an unsigned integer to `IPv4`, and from `UInt128` to `IPv6`: they never converted the value even though the plain `CAST` of it succeeds. Because the MergeTree set index builds its pruning set with `accurateCastOrNull`, an `IPv4` primary key with `IN` over a `UInt32` subquery returned no rows. [#113040](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113040) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong row order for `ORDER BY` (and `GROUP BY` with `optimize_aggregation_in_order`) when reading in order from a sorted object storage table, e.g. an Iceberg table whose files carry its sort order. `ORDER BY ... DESC` on such tables no longer returns ascending rows. [#113095](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113095) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results for comparison chains on a `Variant` expression. At the default settings `v = '7' OR v = '8' OR v = '9'` on a `Variant(String, UInt64)` column was rewritten to `IN` and missed the `UInt64` row `7`, and with `use_variant_default_implementation_for_comparisons = 0` `optimize_redundant_comparisons` could drop or add rows of an `AND` chain such as `v = 'ab'::FixedString(4) AND v = 'ab'::FixedString(2)`. Both optimizations now decline `Variant` expressions. [#113470](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113470) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a `Block structure mismatch` logical error on `INSERT` into a table that several materialized views write to, when the target table's metadata is changed concurrently. Each view's sink is now built from the same metadata snapshot as the header feeding it. [#113518](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113518) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `RESTORE` failing with `READONLY` for a backup containing a table on read-only storage that provides the table's data itself, such as a shared example dataset. Such storage can now be marked with the storage policy option `backup_metadata_only`: `BACKUP` stores only the definitions of its tables, and `RESTORE` recreates them with their UUIDs from the backup without restoring data. [#113574](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113574) ([Julia Kartseva](https://github.com/jkartseva)).
* Fixed `BACKUP` failing with `A concurrent backup writing to the same destination ... (BACKUP_ALREADY_EXISTS)` when no concurrent backup exists. Retrying a backup to a destination whose previous failed attempt had just been cleaned up could read the removed attempt's `.lock` contents from a read cache instead of the freshly written file. Affects local-disk destinations only with a non-default local read cache: the page cache (`page_cache_max_size`, `use_page_cache_for_local_disks`, `local_filesystem_read_method = 'pread'`) or the mmap cache (`use_reader_executor`, `local_filesystem_read_method = 'mmap'`, non-zero `min_bytes_to_use_mmap_io`). [#113617](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113617) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix undefined behavior when a refreshable materialized view uses a very large `RANDOMIZE FOR` window. The random offset was converted from `double` to `Int64` milliseconds and widened to microseconds without range checks, so such a window produced a meaningless next refresh time or aborted a sanitizer build. The offset is now capped at about 95 years, so an absurd window behaves like the widest supported jitter. [#113673](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113673) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed background merges and mutations ignoring cancellation while reading from an unreachable S3 endpoint. Such a merge or mutation kept retrying to its retry budget, so `KILL MUTATION`, `SYSTEM STOP MERGES`, `SYSTEM STOP TTL MERGES` and shutdown had no effect on it, and a following `DROP TABLE` waited for it, blocking further `DROP`/`DETACH` on that database. [#113939](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113939) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a mutated part being written with a checksum entry missing for one of its columns when a mutation's rename map is a permutation, for example the swap `{a.bin -> b.bin, b.bin -> a.bin}` that a chained `RENAME COLUMN` collapses to. Reading such a part raised `LOGICAL_ERROR: Stream <col> for column <col> ... is not found`, and on the next consistency-checking load the part was detached as broken together with the part it covers. [#113945](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113945) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `ORDER BY ... LIMIT n` over a `Float32`/`Float64`/`BFloat16` column with a `minmax` index returning wrong rows when the column holds `NaN`: under `DESC` or `NULLS FIRST`, top-K granule selection could skip the granules holding the answer. `ASC NULLS LAST` keeps using the index. [#114812](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114812) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed silent data loss when concurrent `INSERT`s with `s3_create_new_file_on_insert = 1` (or `azure_create_new_file_on_insert = 1`) picked the same new file name, from one table or from different tables, servers or `s3()` queries on the same path: one `INSERT` overwrote the other's rows with no error. The write is now conditional (`If-None-Match: *`), so the `INSERT` that loses the race fails with a precondition error and can be retried. HDFS is unchanged. Also fixed a data race on the path list of object storage tables. [#114995](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114995) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `clickhouse-keeper-client` and the Keeper HTTP CLI no longer resolve znode paths through the filesystem of the host they run on. Before, a path whose prefix is a symlink on that host (for example `/lib` on Ubuntu) operated on another znode (`rm /lib/x` removed `/usr/lib/x`), and error messages could expose host paths such as the Keeper working directory. [#115016](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115016) ([Young](https://github.com/ylw510)).
* Fixed `IN` with a `Date`/`Date32` constant outside the range of `DateTime`, such as `toDate('2149-06-06')` or `toDate32('0000-01-01')`: the constant was wrapped around and could match an unrelated `DateTime` row. Such a constant now matches nothing, consistent with `=`. [#115047](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115047) ([Yakov Olkhovskiy](https://github.com/yakov-olkhovskiy)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... MATERIALIZE INDEX` on wide `MergeTree` parts producing a skip index that never prunes, when the indexed column had been added by a metadata-only `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN` or had been left absent by a column TTL expiry. The index occupied disk and was refused by the reader on every query. Also fixed wrong results from a skip index after a metadata-only `MODIFY COLUMN` (for example a timezone-only change of a `DateTime`), or from the implicit `_block_number`/`_block_offset` minmax indices of a part adopted by `ATTACH PARTITION ... FROM`, followed by a mutation that rewrites the whole part. [#115055](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115055) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `SELECT count()` returning too many rows for a Delta Lake table whose deletes are stored as deletion vectors: the count came from the `numRecords` file statistics, which still include the removed rows. Closes [#114933](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/114933). [#115872](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115872) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix wrong results when a `MergeTree` table is ordered by `hilbertEncode(x, y)` and a part contains a single distinct point at the origin. With `analyze_index_with_space_filling_curves = 1` (the default), such a part was pruned away and its rows were silently missing from the result. [#115904](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115904) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `executable_pool` dictionaries, functions and the `ExecutablePool` table engine permanently losing a pool slot when a worker process failed to start, after which every call failed with `Could not get process from pool`. Also fixed child processes of executable commands being left unreaped when their startup failed. [#116619](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116619) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed silent wrong results when a text index is created with `dictionary_block_size` close to the maximum of `UInt64`. Rounding the token count up to a number of dictionary blocks overflowed, so the part was written with an empty text index and token predicates such as `hasToken` returned no rows, with no exception and no log line. [#116634](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116634) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a server abort when reading Parquet with a declared structure carrying a `DEFAULT` expression, on a count-only read or with `input_format_defaults_for_omitted_fields` disabled. [#116666](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116666) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the access checks of the `mergeTreeIndex`, `mergeTreeProjection`, `mergeTreeTextIndex`, `mergeTreeAnalyzeIndexes`, `viewIfPermitted` and `loop` table functions. Resolving their structure, for example with `DESCRIBE`, required no privilege on the source table, an `Alias` source was read with no privilege on its target, and a source that did not exist or had another engine was named in the error. `viewIfPermitted` resolved an `ELSE executable(...)` with no `TABLE ENGINE` grant. Reading them was already checked. [#116793](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116793) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix lightweight updates on replicated tables silently skipping rows that arrived on the initiating replica during the `update_sequential_consistency` sync. The update reused a storage snapshot taken before the sync, so the `UPDATE` returned success while those acknowledged rows kept their old values. [#117174](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117174) ([Pratyush Anand](https://github.com/pratyush-anand1444)).
* Fixed the KQL dialect treating a `LowCardinality` argument differently from its unwrapped type in `/`, `bin` and `bin_at`: integer division returned a real number, and `bin` and `bin_at` rejected wrapped numbers, timespans and datetimes they accept unwrapped. [#117190](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117190) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a second-factor bypass in the MySQL protocol: a user configured with `plaintext_password` or `double_sha1_password` plus a time-based one-time password could authenticate with the password alone, because the `mysql_native_password` auth response was verified without checking the TOTP. Such users are now switched to the `sha256_password` plugin and must append the one-time password to the password, like `password+123456`. [#117407](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117407) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Fixed a privilege check that `RESTORE` skipped: restoring a `MATERIALIZED VIEW` with an external `TO` target, or a `TimeSeries` table with external targets, needed no privilege on those targets, so the restored view could write into a table the user has no `INSERT` grant on. Restoring one now requires `SELECT, INSERT` on each external target, the same as `CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW ... TO ...`. Targets of objects the restore skips are exempt, since they are never created. [#117423](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117423) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results when a query called a table function such as `view` twice with arguments that differed only in an `OVER` clause (a window frame offset, `PARTITION BY` or `ORDER BY`) or in a `SAMPLE` clause. A table function's result is cached for the duration of a query under the tree hash of its argument AST, and the query analyzer did not attach the inline window definition, `SAMPLE` size or `SAMPLE` offset to that AST's children, so the hash did not cover them and the second call returned the first call's rows. [#117653](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117653) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `NO_SUCH_COLUMN_IN_TABLE` exception `Unexpected const virtual column: _table` when a mutation (`ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE/DELETE` or lightweight `DELETE`) on a `MergeTree` table referenced the `_table` or `_database` virtual column. Mutations on `Replicated` tables that read a virtual column whose value may differ between replicas (`_table`, `_database`, `_disk_name`) are now rejected unless `allow_nondeterministic_mutations` is enabled, like non-deterministic functions. Closes [#102331](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/102331). [#117724](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117724) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `Arrow`, `ArrowStream` and Arrow Flight output failing with `Capacity error: array cannot contain more than 2147483646 bytes` or `Arrow IPC string offset exceeds 32 bits` when a block held more than 2 GiB of `String` data. Such a block is now written as several Arrow record batches. [#117754](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117754) ([Pavel Kruglov](https://github.com/Avogar)).
* Fix a privilege escalation where a scheduled refresh of a `SQL SECURITY DEFINER` refreshable materialized view reading from a `Distributed` table over a cluster with an interserver `<secret>` was executed on remote shards without authentication, bypassing the definer's access rights. The remote query now runs as the definer. [#117798](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117798) ([Michael Kolupaev](https://github.com/al13n321)).
* Fixed the exception `Join is supported only for pipelines with one output port` when `join_algorithm = 'parallel_full_sorting_merge'` is used with `max_threads` greater than one and the two sides of the join are planned with different thread budgets, for example when one side is a subquery with a trivial `LIMIT` and the other is a `UNION ALL`. The same defect also silently disabled the sharded join when both sides were limited to a single thread. [#117826](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117826) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results when a `GROUP BY` or `LIMIT BY` key, or an `ORDER BY` expression, was a function call whose result type collapses to `Nullable(Nothing)` because an argument is a `NULL` constant, such as `concatAssumeInjective(s, NULL)`. Such a call was still treated as injective and replaced by its argument in the key, so `GROUP BY` and `LIMIT BY` produced one group or row per distinct argument value, and `optimize_truncate_order_by_after_group_by_keys` dropped a still-needed `ORDER BY` tiebreaker. [#117894](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117894) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed under-reporting of read rows for `SELECT ... ORDER BY <sorting key prefix> DESC ... LIMIT n` served by `optimize_read_in_order`. Reading a range in reverse buffers the whole read task before emitting any of it, and rows still buffered when the `LIMIT` stopped the query were never reported, so `SelectedRows`, `SelectedBytes`, `system.query_log.read_rows`, read quotas and `max_rows_to_read` all missed those rows. Such a query may now correctly fail with `TOO_MANY_ROWS` where it previously succeeded. [#118192](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118192) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `JOIN` on `FixedString` and `String` key pairs, also as elements of tuple keys: values that differ only in trailing zero bytes now match and sort the same way as with the comparison operators. `isNotDistinctFrom` and `isDistinctFrom` now agree with `equals` for `Nullable` values of these types. Optimizations that derive predicates from join keys (transitive predicates, filter pushdown to the other side) no longer apply to such key pairs. [#118561](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118561) ([Vladimir Cherkasov](https://github.com/vdimir)).
* Fix column names of `untuple` used as the body of a SQL user-defined function: with the analyzer, `SELECT f(...) AS alias` produced `tupleElement(...)` names instead of `alias.field`. The alias of the call now takes priority over an alias inside the function body, as in the old analyzer. Closes [#102205](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/102205). [#118607](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118607) ([Vladimir Cherkasov](https://github.com/vdimir)).
* Fixed a `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` error ("WITH TOTALS, ROLLUP, CUBE or GROUPING SETS are not supported without aggregation") when a query with a JOIN or ARRAY JOIN and a `GROUP BY ... WITH TOTALS`/`ROLLUP`/`CUBE`/`GROUPING SETS` read a `Merge` table that matches a `Distributed` table. The rewritten child query kept the GROUP BY modifier after its `GROUP BY` clause was removed. [#118658](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118658) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed tables in a database with the `lazy_load_tables` setting staying hidden behind a lightweight proxy after they have been loaded. They were missing from `system.parts`, `system.parts_columns`, `system.detached_parts`, `system.mutations` and `system.replicas`, and were not counted in the `AttachedReplicatedTable` metric. [#118699](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118699) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed leftovers of the nested `SETTINGS` constraints fix ([#115397](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/115397)): `use_query_cache` set inside a subquery or a CTE could still enable the query result cache despite a `CONST` or `readonly` constraint, and a nested clause was forwarded to the shards of a `Distributed` query as written, bypassing the initiator's constraints there. An unknown setting name or an uncastable value in a nested `SETTINGS` clause now throws as in a top-level clause instead of being silently ignored. [#118822](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118822) ([Dmitry Novik](https://github.com/novikd)).
* `ALTER TABLE ... FETCH PARTITION`/`FETCH PART` no longer produces a copy that silently lacks the acknowledged lightweight `UPDATE`s of the source: it refuses a source partition with unapplied patch parts and asks for `APPLY PATCHES`, as the other part-relocation commands already do. [#119181](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119181) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
