* `IN` and `NOT IN` no longer fail with an exception when a tuple left-hand column with NULL rows or NULL fields, such as a `Nullable(Tuple)` column, has to be converted to the set's key type, and no longer report false matches when a `DateTime64` value loses sub-second precision or exceeds the `DateTime` range in that conversion; a fractional `DateTime64` value now matches an equal `Float64` value. `accurateCastOrNull` to a tuple type returns NULL instead of throwing when one of the fields cannot be converted, and `toDecimal*OrNull` and `toDecimal*OrZero` accept zero-padded `FixedString` input. A `DateTime64` value matches a floating-point set key only when the key converts back to the same timestamp, and JOIN keys whose tuple elements are `LowCardinality(Nullable)` take the common-subtype fallback instead of failing with `NO_COMMON_TYPE`. [#119183](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119183) ([Nihal Z. Miaji](https://github.com/nihalzp)).
* A nested `SETTINGS profile = ...` clause on a subquery that enables `use_query_cache` through the profile now correctly opts the subquery into the query result cache, matching the existing behavior of an explicit `SETTINGS use_query_cache = 1`. Previously the Planner only recognized a literal `use_query_cache` change in the clause and silently ignored one contributed via a profile. [#119283](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119283) ([MakerYuichi](https://github.com/MakerYuichi)).
* Fix silently missing rows when a query filters on a column whose `ALTER MODIFY COLUMN` or `DROP COLUMN` is still a pending on-the-fly mutation: statistics-based part pruning, skip indexes (including the top-k minmax index) and the query condition cache described the values as they were written, and dropped parts or granules the query had to read. [#119337](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119337) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed reading a `TimeSeries` target table through the `timeSeriesSamples`, `timeSeriesTags` and `timeSeriesMetricFamilies` table functions with parallel replicas, which returned every row once per replica. [#119350](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119350) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Respect `remote_url_allow_hosts` in the Hive metastore catalog. [#119353](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119353) ([Smita Kulkarni](https://github.com/SmitaRKulkarni)).
* The JSONPath wildcard `[*]` in `JSON_QUERY`, `JSON_VALUE`, and `JSON_EXISTS` now treats a non-array value as a one-element array per RFC 9535 lax semantics, instead of silently dropping it. For example, `JSON_QUERY('{"p":3}', '$.p[*]')` returns `[3]` instead of an empty string. This closes [#118755](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/118755). [#119449](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119449) ([Kirill Heitzler](https://github.com/heitzlki)).
* Removing a default property from an `EPHEMERAL` column used to suggest `REMOVE EPHEMERAL`, which is not valid syntax and fails with a syntax error. The error now points at `DROP COLUMN`, which deletes the column, or `MODIFY COLUMN` with a `DEFAULT`, `MATERIALIZED` or `ALIAS` expression, which replaces the ephemeral property. This applies to `REMOVE DEFAULT`, `REMOVE MATERIALIZED` and `REMOVE ALIAS` alike. [#119608](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119608) ([Serhiy Bzhezytskyy](https://github.com/serhiy-bzhezytskyy)).
* Fix RESTORE storage_policy ignored for MV inner tables, not for TimeSeries Engine yet. [#119993](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119993) ([Han Fei](https://github.com/hanfei1991)).
* Fixed a privilege check bypass in text index `preprocessor` and `postprocessor` expressions. They were resolved under an internal full-access context, so functions requiring a grant, such as `demangle` and `addressToLine`, could be used without it. These expressions are now authorized against the user who declares the index. Creating or adding a text index with a `preprocessor` or `postprocessor` using `ON CLUSTER` now requires `distributed_ddl_use_initial_user_and_roles = 1` on every host and `distributed_ddl_entry_format_version >= 8`; otherwise the query is rejected. [#120080](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120080) ([Jimmy Aguilar Mena](https://github.com/Ergus)).
* Fixed an issue where queries could silently drop rows (return incorrect results) when using space-filling curve indexes or primary keys on `Nullable(UInt64)` columns. [#120169](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120169) ([Aditya Kumar](https://github.com/adityaksolves)).
* A query that reads `system.stack_trace` more than once, for example through `merge('system', ...)`, a `UNION ALL` or a self join, deadlocked while its pipeline was built, could not be cancelled or killed, and left every later reader of that table blocked for the lifetime of the server. [#120216](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120216) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a wrong result where a `RIGHT`, `LEFT`, `FULL` or `ANY` outer `JOIN` returned too few rows when the `WHERE` value came from `assumeNotNull` of a `NULL`. The query plan converted such a join to `INNER`, `SEMI` or `ANTI` after evaluating the filter over constants, where `assumeNotNull` reads a different value than it does at runtime. Closes [#120204](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120204). [#120258](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120258) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` for a `SELECT` whose first join is a `FULL JOIN ... USING` on keys of different types, followed by another `USING` join and a `WHERE` over the second join's key. The equi-key filter push-down substituted the coalesced `USING` key by a name the join computes instead of materialising it, so a valid query was rejected. [#120342](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120342) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Mask credentials embedded in HTTP URIs. [#120407](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120407) ([Han Fei](https://github.com/hanfei1991)).
* Fixed the Delta Lake writer committing `add.stats` one level too deep, as `{"stats_json":"{\"numRecords\":N}"}` instead of `{"numRecords":N}`, so no reader saw the per-file row count: `SELECT count()` could not use the row count from the log, external readers such as Spark and delta-rs could not answer `count(*)` from the log either, and an `INSERT` into a table whose protocol supports the `rowTracking` writer feature was rejected outright. Closes [#120522](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120522). [#120635](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120635) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a server abort in `ORDER BY ... WITH FILL ... INTERPOLATE (...)` when the same column is named as an `INTERPOLATE` output more than once, for example `INTERPOLATE (b AS b, b AS b)`. Such a query is now rejected with `INVALID_WITH_FILL_EXPRESSION` during query analysis instead of aborting the server as soon as a row is filled. Closes [#120645](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120645). [#120748](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120748) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a read of uninitialized memory when a `Variant`, `Dynamic` or `JSON` column holding only `NULL` values is compressed, for example on insert into a `Memory` table created with `compress = 1`, or when a block is compressed for a `JOIN` or for the query result cache. The reported size of such a column was also unspecified. [#120776](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120776) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The default partition key of the inner recent samples table of a `TimeSeries` table is now anchored to UTC. Previously it depended on the server timezone, so changing that timezone made range queries silently skip whole parts. [#120960](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120960) ([Valery Petrov](https://github.com/valerypetrov)).
* Bound the total number of nested `-Resample` combinator states by the product of each level's element count, so nested `-Resample` chains with an empty innermost range no longer loop over `2^60` nested states and pin a CPU thread indefinitely. [#121083](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121083) ([Ilya Yatsishin](https://github.com/qoega)).
* Fixed formatting of a `CREATE DICTIONARY` query whose `SETTINGS` name is not an identifier, such as `` SETTINGS(`my setting` = 1) ``. The name was written back unquoted, so `SHOW CREATE DICTIONARY` and the stored definition of such a dictionary did not parse, and a server that had one refused to start. A name ending in a dot, such as `` `a.` ``, additionally lost the dot in `SET`, `CREATE NAMED COLLECTION` and settings profiles, which stored a different setting name. [#121091](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121091) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results when `WHERE` wraps a `Nullable` partition key in a NULL-erasing function: `assumeNotNull`, `isNull`/`isNotNull` compared with a constant (`(c IS NULL) >= true`), or `ifNull`/`coalesce` under `NOT` or with `allow_key_condition_coalesce_rewrite = 0`. Partition pruning discarded the `NULL` partition instead of evaluating the predicate on its rows, so rows disappeared from both a predicate and its negation. [#121154](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121154) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the parallel-replicas row estimate running an `IN (subquery)` while planning, which made an `INSERT ... SELECT` whose filter reads `input()` fail with `INVALID_USAGE_OF_INPUT` when `parallel_replicas_min_number_of_rows_per_replica` was not zero. [#121283](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121283) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `sig` parameter of an Azure shared access signature in a URL (`url_base`, `s3_base`, `format_avro_schema_registry_url`, `s3` and `url` arguments, and error messages) is now replaced with `[HIDDEN]` in `system.query_log`, `system.settings`, `system.processes` and settings profiles, like the AWS and Google presigned parameters. `X-Amz-*` and `X-Goog-*` parameters are now hidden in any letter case, so the lower-case `x-amz-security-token` of a SigV2 presigned URL is no longer shown or kept in the base-backup locator of an incremental backup. [#121348](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121348) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a logical error (`Bad cast from type DB::ColumnReplicated to DB::ColumnNullable`) in an `ASOF JOIN` whose right side has a `Nullable` ASOF key produced through lazy column replication, for example by `arrayJoin` or a nested `JOIN`. `enable_lazy_columns_replication` is on by default, and on a release build the cast was unchecked, so the NULL filter on the ASOF key could keep or drop the wrong rows. [#121385](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121385) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Tables with `partition_strategy = 'hive'` now skip hidden paths when reading: objects with a path segment starting with `_` or `.` below the table root (`_temporary/`, `.spark-staging-<id>/`), which hold the in-progress staging output of Hive-ecosystem writers. Reading them raced the writer's commit and either returned uncommitted rows or failed with `The specified key does not exist`. For plain glob paths the same filtering is available through the new settings `s3_skip_hidden_files`, `azure_skip_hidden_files` and `hdfs_skip_hidden_files`, which are disabled by default, so existing glob reads are unchanged. [#121445](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121445) ([Zach Rippas](https://github.com/zatchet)).
* Fixed StorageKafka2 skipping messages when a consumer is stopped mid-batch, a possible use-after-free when such a table is dropped without a shutdown, and partitions outside the permanent locks being consumed only every other round. [#121478](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121478) ([johnjing](https://github.com/JingYanchao)).
* Requesting SSH-key authentication over the native protocol no longer reveals whether the named user exists, or how it authenticates, before its signature is checked. [#121704](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121704) ([Shaohua Wang](https://github.com/tiandiwonder)).
* Fix an out-of-bounds read in the LSM-based Keeper node storage when reading a corrupted block whose entry claims a path prefix longer than the block's base path. [#122037](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122037) ([sunliqiang](https://github.com/Pyolar)).
* Fixes a server abort when a query with `LIMIT AFTER` or `LIMIT UNTIL` has a lambda in its `SELECT` list that the boundary expression repeats, or once its expressions are JIT-compiled (by default on its fourth run). Such a query over remote shards also no longer fails with `Serialization is not implemented for data type Function`. [#122045](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122045) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` (Code 48) when lazy materialization squashed chunks that still carried a captured-lambda `ColumnFunction` (shared lambda in `WHERE` and `SELECT` with `ORDER BY ... LIMIT`). [#122063](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122063) ([Ria Khatoniar](https://github.com/Ria-K912)).
* Fix a server crash when a `RIGHT JOIN`/`FULL JOIN` is planned for parallel replicas (`parallel_replicas_plan_based=1`) and the join output header declares a column by type only — e.g. the residual column of a constant `NULL` conjunct in the `ON` clause. Type-only header columns are now materialized as an empty column of the declared type instead of being dereferenced as null. [#122128](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122128) ([Ilias](https://github.com/iliasabk)).
* A valid query over a `JOIN` whose `WHERE` guards a conjunct that can throw could be rejected with an error such as `CANNOT_PARSE_DATETIME`. Filter push-down into the `JOIN`, merging a filter into the `JOIN` condition, and an aggregate projection's residual filter each rebuilt the conjuncts they pushed into a side or left behind in an order that was not the written one, so a guarded conjunct could be evaluated ahead of its guard. Reported in [#121909](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121909). [#122160](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122160) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix merges failing with `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` on every retry when a rows TTL reads a column that no part stores yet. [#122164](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122164) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* A string constant compared with a column now compares by value when an integer in it does not fit the column type, both for a scalar and inside a quoted `Array`, `Tuple` or `Map` literal, like the same literal unquoted. Such an integer used to wrap around (`toUInt64(0) = '18446744073709551616'` and `[toUInt8(44)] = '[300]'` were `1`), ordering comparisons answered `0` in both directions (`toInt32(0) < '2147483648'`), a negative constant against an unsigned type threw, and `ALTER TABLE ... DROP PARTITION '18446744073709551616'` dropped partition `0`. `IN` sets, primary key ranges and `values()` no longer accept the wrapped value. [#122245](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122245) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `prettyPrintJSON`, `printf`, `formatQuery`, `formatQuerySingleLine`, `parseQueryToJSON`, `formatQueryFromJSON`, `conv`, `MGRSToGeo`, `ULIDStringToDateTime`, `readWKTPoint`, `readWKBPoint`, `filesystemAvailable`/`Capacity`/`Unreserved`, `base32Decode`, `base58Decode`, `base64Decode`, `base64URLDecode`, the AES `encrypt`/`decrypt` functions, `divideDecimal`, `clamp`, `widthBucket`, `regexpPosition`, the tuple functions, `hop`, `hopStart`, `hopEnd`, `timeSeriesIdToGroup`, `s2ToGeo`, `s2CapUnion`, `s2RectAdd`, `s2RectUnion`, `s2RectIntersection` throwing an exception on a valid `LowCardinality` argument, or on a `Nullable` argument holding a NULL, because they were executed on the argument type's default value, which no row held. The tuple and vector functions (e.g. `dotProduct`), `clamp`, `s2ToGeo`, `filesystem*` and `multiplyDecimal` also no longer throw on a row removed by `ARRAY JOIN`. Closes [#122277](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122277). Closes [#121498](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121498). Closes [#132563](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/132563). [#122356](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122356) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `text` index on `mapValues(m)` dropping rows for a `LIKE`, `ILIKE` or `match` pattern that holds for `m['k']` on a row whose map has no key `k`. `m['k']` then reads the map value type's default, which for `FixedString(N)` is `N` NUL bytes, while the index stores terms only for the elements the map holds. Index analysis now evaluates the predicate on that default and skips the index when it holds. [#122358](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122358) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ssd_cache` and `complex_key_ssd_cache` dictionaries that share a `PATH` returning each other's values from `dictGet`, and `Cache data corrupted` errors in queries that run during `SYSTEM RELOAD DICTIONARY` of an SSD cache dictionary. Each dictionary instance now creates its own cache file instead of truncating the file another instance may still be reading. [#122465](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122465) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results and `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` errors in `ORDER BY ... LIMIT` queries with `use_top_k_dynamic_filtering` (enabled by default) when the query sorts by `_part_offset` and is served by a projection that stores `_part_offset`, and when the table has a column named `__topKFilter(<sort column>)`. [#122486](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122486) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reading an Apache Paimon snapshot whose index manifest lists deletion vectors now throws `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` instead of silently returning rows that later `DELETE` and `UPDATE` statements removed: the reader does not apply deletion vectors. This also covers tables where `deletion-vectors.enabled` was turned off afterwards. Closes [#122431](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122431). [#122506](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122506) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results of `ORDER BY` and `LIMIT BY` over a `Merge` table or the `merge` table function when an underlying table is read from a normal projection with a different `ORDER BY`. [#122520](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122520) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Check access rights before loading a dictionary or reading its definition in the `dictionary` table function, `dictGet`-family functions, `naiveBayesClassifier`, `Dictionary` tables and `CREATE TABLE ... ENGINE = Dictionary`. Queries without the required grants no longer trigger the dictionary load, and queries or DDL that reference a dictionary now require access to it even when they do not read it, e.g. `DESCRIBE`, `CREATE VIEW` or a `DEFAULT dictGet(...)` column. [#122678](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122678) ([Matheus Nerone](https://github.com/Manerone)).
* Fix query parameters in the filter of `CREATE ROW POLICY` and `ALTER ROW POLICY` being stored unsubstituted: a `{db:Identifier}` table qualifier was resolved in the current database of the query reading the table, and a value parameter made every read of the table fail. The parameters are now substituted when the policy is created, and an `Identifier` query parameter that reaches a filter unsubstituted (for example in `additional_table_filters`) fails with `UNKNOWN_QUERY_PARAMETER`. [#122688](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122688) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `AzureBlobStorage` tables created with `PARTITION BY` under `file_like_engine_default_partition_strategy = 'wildcard'` (the default for `compatibility` older than 26.6) that could not be attached again, so the server did not start after a restart. The engine now reads back the `partition_strategy = 'none'` argument that the server stores for such tables, and the engine and the `azureBlobStorage` table function accept `partition_strategy` and `partition_columns_in_data_file` as key-value arguments. [#122707](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122707) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a wrong result when an alias of `sumIf(k, cond)` or `sum(if(cond, 0, k))` with a constant `k` other than 1 is referenced more than once, for example `SELECT sumIf(2, cond) AS r, r + 0, r + 1` or `HAVING r > 3 AND r < 15`: every reference after the first returned `countIf` without the factor `k`. [#122726](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122726) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results for a query whose condition calls a non-deterministic function such as `rand` or `generateUUIDv4` on a table with a `set` skip index, a `text` index on `mapKeys` or `JSONAllPaths`, an aggregate projection or a projection index: the function was computed on index values or projection rows instead of once per row, so `WHERE rand() % 2 = 0` could return about a quarter of the rows instead of half, or keep or drop whole groups. Closes [#89615](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/89615). [#122787](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122787) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Forbid renaming the source of incremental refreshable views closes #[#120082](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120082). [#122826](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122826) ([Smita Kulkarni](https://github.com/SmitaRKulkarni)).
* Fixed a Keeper abort (`Trying to rollback invalid ZXID`) on healthy nodes when a misbehaving node, such as a node restarted with an empty data directory in `force_recovery`, became leader. Followers now refuse to roll back committed log entries. [#122881](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122881) ([Antonio Andelic](https://github.com/antonio2368)).
* Fix computation of the predicted size for MergeTree readers involving `PREWHERE` clause. The columns that are read just to execute the prewhere clause are also considered for the block size, preventing out-of-memory errors. [#122919](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122919) ([Javier G. Sogo](https://github.com/jgsogo)).
* Fix cluster discovery with `secure` enabled: nodes now register with the `secure` flag and advertise `tcp_port_secure`, so peers no longer skip each other or connect with TLS to the plain port. [#122930](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122930) ([Drew Hammond](https://github.com/drewhammond)).
* Fixed cluster table functions (`s3Cluster`, `urlCluster`, `fileCluster`, `azureBlobStorageCluster`, `hdfsCluster`, the data lake `*Cluster` functions, and `url`/`s3` reads distributed by `parallel_replicas_for_cluster_engines`) returning no rows or wrong rows, and `INSERT ... SELECT` from them inserting nothing, when a subquery reads a CTE, e.g. `WITH t AS (...) SELECT ... WHERE x IN (SELECT ... FROM t)`: the replicas received the CTE name without its definition. `GROUP BY ROLLUP`/`CUBE`/`GROUPING SETS` with `grouping()` and `SELECT *, x + 1 AS x` over these functions no longer fail with `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER` and `MULTIPLE_EXPRESSIONS_FOR_ALIAS`. [#122931](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122931) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* PromQL: an `offset` on a range selector or a subquery keeps the timestamps of the samples, as in Prometheus. Previously range-vector results, subquery range vectors and `ts_of_*_over_time` returned timestamps shifted by the offset, and `predict_linear` predicted from the end of the shifted window. [#122977](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122977) ([Valery Petrov](https://github.com/valerypetrov)).
* Fixed the one-argument form of the `merge` table function, `merge('regexp')`, in a subquery, a `JOIN` or a CTE of a query that is sent to other servers (`remote`, `cluster`, a `Distributed` table, `*Cluster` table functions, parallel replicas): those servers matched the regexp against their own default database instead of the current database of the query, which returned wrong results or failed with `CANNOT_EXTRACT_TABLE_STRUCTURE`. [#122985](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122985) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a server termination on `COMMIT` of a MergeTree transaction when the write of the part's version metadata or of a mutation CSN fails (for example on `ENOSPC`, a read-only disk, or an object-storage write error); the write is now retried for a bounded time, in `ROLLBACK` as well. Also fix a server termination when `KILL MUTATION` raced with the commit of a transactional mutation. [#123134](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123134) ([filimonov](https://github.com/filimonov)).
* Fixed a projection that a `ReplicatedMergeTree` replica fetched with a part before applying the `ALTER TABLE ... ADD PROJECTION` that added it: merges dropped the projection, and mutations kept its pre-mutation data, so queries answered from the projection returned wrong results. [#123185](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123185) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `Invalid argument` exceptions when reading `MergeTree` data or merging parts with Direct I/O on kernels that reject unaligned reads at EOF. [#123271](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123271) ([zhao-xiaogang](https://github.com/zhao-xiaogang)).
* Fix possible crash due to use-after-free of memory reservation, and a possible hang of CPU and IO requests, after DROP WORKLOAD. Queries still running in a dropped workload now fail. [#123341](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123341) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* `FileLog` no longer fails with `CANNOT_READ_ALL_DATA` (`Stream is in bad state`) when a file ends with a line that has no newline yet: the complete lines are read, and the last line is read from its start once it is completed. Related to [#123368](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123368). [#123404](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123404) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed secret masking of the Azure table functions and engines: a SAS token, account key or client secret was logged in clear in `system.query_log` for the `azureBlobStorage(url, sas_token)` form, argument lists with a `key = value` argument, `extra_credentials(...)` (now hidden whole), connection strings with a second or repeated secret key or an endpoint url with a query, named-collection overrides with a key Azure does not accept, and `after_processing_move_connection_string`. A SAS is no longer shown in the `remote_url_allow_hosts` error and `system.schema_inference_cache`, nor a SAS or a disk's account key in the backup log. [#123651](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123651) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading a subcolumn of a `Nested` member added by `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN` (`sizeN`, or a `JSON` path below the member's array) from parts written before the `ALTER`. It failed with `There is no subcolumn`, or produced a column of the wrong type, which returned garbage values or crashed the server, also in a `FINAL` merge. [#123690](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123690) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading a `SQL SECURITY DEFINER` or `NONE` view with `enable_parallel_replicas = 1`: the replicas ran the view's query as the cluster user with the invoker's row policies instead of as the definer, so the read could return the rows the definer's row policy hides, or fail with `ACCESS_DENIED` on a cluster with an interserver secret. Queries reading `DEFINER` / `NONE` views no longer use query-tree parallel replicas; plan-based parallel replicas (`parallel_replicas_plan_based = 1`) still apply. [#123729](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123729) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix reading a tuple element whose name contains a dot, like `` s.`a.b` ``, or its subcolumns, from Parquet files and Delta Lake tables when the tuple also has an element named by its prefix, like `a`. Such queries failed with `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK`, or returned the JSON path `b` of `a` when `a` was a `JSON`. [#123836](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123836) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed merges replacing the values of a column with its default in a part that had no TTL info for that column's TTL (for example, a part attached with `ALTER TABLE ... ATTACH PARTITION ... FROM` from a table without the column TTL, or written before `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY COLUMN ... TTL` with `materialize_ttl_after_modify = 0`) when another merged part had every value of that column expired. [#123837](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123837) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM` exception in `if`, `ifNull` and `coalesce` with a `Nullable(Enum)` argument that contains `NULL` and a result type other than this `Enum`, e.g. `ifNull(y, '')`. [#123886](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123886) ([Manuel](https://github.com/raimannma)).
* Fixed data loss on object storage disks with `local` metadata (the default for S3, Azure and HDFS disks): when a failed metadata transaction, such as a part removal, could not be rolled back completely, for example because the rollback hit the memory limit, a hard link count stayed too low and a later removal deleted blobs still used by another part, such as its detached copy, so `ATTACH PARTITION` failed with `The specified key does not exist`. [#123888](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123888) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` for a query whose `SELECT` alias holds an `AND`/`OR`/`=` chain and is reused by `GROUP BY`, `ORDER BY`, `HAVING`, `WHERE` or another alias, for example `SELECT (((s = '1') = 1) = 1) AND (n > 0) AS r FROM t GROUP BY r ORDER BY r`. [#123925](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123925) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `varPop`, `varSamp`, `stddevPop` and `stddevSamp` now return `nan` instead of `0` when the data contains a NaN or an infinity, and `studentTTest`, `welchTTest`, `studentTTestOneSample` and `meanZTest` return a `nan` p-value instead of `0` next to a `nan` statistic. [#123931](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123931) ([Ashish Kumar Choubey](https://github.com/aschoube)).
* Fix rows silently skipped by primary key, partition key and `minmax` skip index analysis when a `DateTime64` column with a fractional scale is compared with an integer or `DateTime` constant by `>` or `<`, for example `WHERE dt > 1577836800` on a `DateTime64(3)` key. The regression appeared in 26.3. [#123999](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123999) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `positionCaseInsensitiveUTF8`, `countSubstringsCaseInsensitiveUTF8`, `ILIKE`, `startsWithCaseInsensitiveUTF8` and `endsWithCaseInsensitiveUTF8` giving different results for constant and column arguments, for short and long strings and for neighbouring rows. `startsWithCaseInsensitiveUTF8` and `endsWithCaseInsensitiveUTF8` now compare by code points, so `startsWithCaseInsensitiveUTF8('ß', 'ẞ')` returns 1. In substring search, the KELVIN, ANGSTROM, OHM and THETA SYMBOL signs now match only themselves. [#124117](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124117) ([Valery Petrov](https://github.com/valerypetrov)).
* Fixed a missing or wrong `WITH TOTALS` row of a `JOIN` whose `ON` condition is constant false when an input that the join does not need to read produces totals. Appeared in 26.8 with `query_plan_short_circuit_constant_false_join`. [#124134](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124134) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A projection `ORDER BY` tuple containing an aliased element is now stored in a form that can be parsed back, so it no longer makes `SHOW CREATE TABLE` or server startup fail. [#124137](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124137) ([Ankit Nakhawa](https://github.com/AnkitNakhawa)).
* Fixed a graceful server shutdown cancelling a `BACKUP ... ON CLUSTER` or `RESTORE ... ON CLUSTER` whose part reached this host while it was shutting down, although `shutdown_wait_backups_and_restores` (enabled by default) makes the shutdown wait for running backups and restores. [#124334](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124334) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` for a query that groups by an alias of an `OR` chain of `LIKE`/`ILIKE` conditions and reuses that alias in `ORDER BY` or `HAVING`, for example `SELECT (s LIKE '%a%' OR s LIKE '%b%' OR s LIKE '%c%' OR s LIKE '%1%') AS r FROM t GROUP BY r ORDER BY r`. [#124480](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124480) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` for a query whose `SELECT` alias is an `extract` call with a pattern ending in more than one `.*$` and is reused by `GROUP BY` together with `ORDER BY`, `HAVING`, `WHERE` or another alias, for example `SELECT extract(s, '(1).*$.*$') AS r FROM t GROUP BY r ORDER BY r`. [#124482](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124482) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix lost position deletes and an extra object metadata request per task when reading Iceberg in a cluster with `cluster_table_function_split_granularity = 'bucket'`. [#124618](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124618) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* Fixed wrong results of comparisons, `has` / `indexOf` and casts of `Decimal128` / `Decimal256` values when bringing a value to a larger scale does not fit the type: `toDecimal128(1, 30) < 1000000000` returned `0`, and `CAST(toDecimal128(1000000000, 0) AS Decimal(38, 30))` returned a wrapped value. A comparison of decimals with different scales is now exact for every width (`toDecimal64(1, 18) < 100` threw `DECIMAL_OVERFLOW`), and such a cast, also from an integer (a `UInt256` turned negative), throws `DECIMAL_OVERFLOW`. [#124680](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124680) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix server startup failure when an Azure Blob Storage disk from the server configuration has an endpoint that is not listed in `remote_url_allow_hosts` (since 26.7). Such disks are no longer checked against this list, like S3 disks, also when a data lake table uses one via `SETTINGS disk = ...`. Disks created by `disk(...)` in a query, URLs of table functions and table engines, and redirects are still checked. An existing table on a `disk(...)` disk whose host is no longer allowed loads with a warning and keeps reading and writing to that host, as for S3 disks; a new `disk(...)` with that host is rejected. Closes [#124431](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124431). [#124721](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124721) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `WHERE x` / `PREWHERE x` over a Parquet file without a page index, where `x` is a `UInt8` column holding values greater than 1: reading the other columns failed with `Unexpected number of rows in column subchunk`, read out of bounds (`BYTE_STREAM_SPLIT`) or returned errors on garbage data (GeoParquet, `Decimal` stored as `BYTE_ARRAY`). [#124815](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124815) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results (unsorted rows, duplicate `DISTINCT` rows) and `Positional argument number N is out of bounds` errors for `ORDER BY ALL` queries whose projection contains an integer constant, when they read from `remote` or `Distributed` inside a view. [#124870](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124870) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reject CR and LF characters in values sent as HTTP headers or in the request line of outgoing HTTP requests (BigQuery, YTsaurus, `url`, DataLake catalogs, GCP OAuth, AI functions, S3 storage class), which could be used to inject headers or additional requests. [#124905](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124905) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fix `CANNOT_RESTORE_TABLE` when restoring a Alias Engine tabel. [#124914](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124914) ([Han Fei](https://github.com/hanfei1991)).
* Fixed configuration overrides given on the command line after `--` (for example `clickhouse-server -- --merge_tree.max_suspicious_broken_parts=7`) being silently ignored when the configuration file has no such section. This affected MergeTree settings, Keeper coordination settings, `remote_url_allow_hosts`, `http_options_response`, system logs, `zookeeper`, `filesystem_caches`, and `macros` in `clickhouse-local`. [#124915](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124915) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A `PostgreSQL` table created with connection pool settings taken from the session, such as `SET postgresql_connection_pool_size = 3`, now stores them in its definition and keeps them after a server restart, instead of falling back to the server's defaults. [#124927](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124927) ([Vadim Lopatin](https://github.com/buggins)).
* Fixed an exception (such as `Division by zero` or `Cannot parse input`) when a `_part_offset` projection used as an index evaluated the query filter on rows the query does not read. [#124971](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124971) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ORDER BY ... LIMIT` and `GROUP BY ... ORDER BY ... LIMIT` on MergeTree tables returning wrong rows with top-K dynamic filtering when the key type contains `Dynamic`, `Variant` or `JSON` (for example `JSON`, `Array(Dynamic)` or `Tuple(Variant(Date, UInt64))`). Such keys no longer use top-K dynamic filtering. Closes [#124960](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124960). [#124984](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124984) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SIZES_OF_COLUMNS_DOESNT_MATCH` and `LOGICAL_ERROR` when a `file`, `url`, `s3`, `azureBlobStorage` or `hdfs` glob matches paths that do not all contain a Hive partition column: a filter on `_path` or `_file` now works, and filtering on or reading such a column fails with `INCORRECT_DATA` naming the path. The Hive partition columns of a table created with `use_hive_partitioning = 1` are now also read when the query runs with `use_hive_partitioning = 0`. [#125024](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125024) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `ALTER UPDATE` never finishing when an update expression has an `IN` subquery nested in another `IN` subquery. Restore `PREWHERE` for `IN (SELECT ... WHERE ...)` subqueries of mutations and lightweight deletes, which have read the subquery table in full since 26.6. Fix a mutation never finishing when two of its `IN` subqueries contain the same `IN` subquery. [#125031](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125031) ([Seva Potapov](https://github.com/seva-potapov)).
* Fixed `file`, `url`, `s3` and the other file-like table functions and engines silently dropping rows when the data has a column named `_idx`, or, for an object storage glob, a column named `_path`: a `WHERE` on that column was evaluated against the position of the file in the list, or turned into the list of object keys. A Hive partition key named `_idx` no longer makes a filtered query fail with `AMBIGUOUS_COLUMN_NAME`. [#125045](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125045) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixes `RabbitMQ` tables inserting most of the already received but unprocessed messages twice after a lost connection or channel, sometimes followed by `unknown delivery tag` channel errors. [#125105](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125105) ([Sven Eliasson](https://github.com/comino)).
* Fixed a query that never finished planning, ignoring `max_execution_time` and `KILL QUERY`, when a filter over expressions that reuse the same subexpression (such as `x + x` across nested subqueries) is pushed down to a `JOIN` or checked for the `ANY` to `SEMI`/`ANTI` join conversion. [#125117](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125117) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a database-qualified `RENAME` or `EXCHANGE` of tables and dictionaries in a `Replicated` database whose replicas mount it under different local names: the other replicas replayed it with the initiator's database name, got stuck, then recovered by moving their tables to `<database>_broken_tables`. The `EXCHANGE` done by a refresh of a refreshable materialized view is now applied on such replicas too. `RENAME TABLE db1.t TO u` or `EXCHANGE TABLES db1.a AND b` issued from another current database is now rejected as a move between databases instead of acting inside `db1`; qualify both names. [#125159](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125159) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Creating an Iceberg table with a `Time` column, adding a `Nullable(Time)` column, or changing a column to `Time` with `ALTER TABLE` now fails with `Unsupported type for iceberg Time`, as it already does for `Time64`. Previously the column was declared as Iceberg `time` (microseconds) but written as whole seconds without the Parquet `TIME` annotation, so other Iceberg readers refused or misread the table. Closes [#118806](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/118806). [#125189](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125189) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading a table created as `CREATE TABLE ... AS generateRandom(...)` without a structure argument, which failed with `NO_SUCH_COLUMN_IN_TABLE` because each read generated another random structure instead of using the table's columns. A table created as `CREATE TABLE t (...) AS null()` without a structure argument can now be read too, instead of failing with `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK`. [#125190](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125190) ([Vadim Lopatin](https://github.com/buggins)).
* Reject `Arrow` and `ArrowStream` inputs with an unsupported metadata version. [#125224](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125224) ([Mohammed Sohail](https://github.com/msohail22)).
* `ASOF JOIN` with the `hash` algorithm now returns the same row when several right rows share the closest `ASOF` value: the last arrived one for `>` and `>=`, the first one for `<` and `<=`. Previously the result changed once the right side exceeded 256 rows and differed for `DateTime64` and `Decimal` columns. [#125346](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125346) ([Irisphora Chen](https://github.com/RagamuffinCat)).
* Fixed `icebergBucket` and `icebergHash` for `DateTime64(9)` values (Iceberg `timestamp_ns` and `timestamptz_ns`) before 1970 that have a sub-microsecond part: nanoseconds are now floored to microseconds as the Iceberg spec requires, so partition pruning no longer skips the files that hold such rows, and `INSERT` writes them into the same bucket as other engines. Closes [#125330](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125330). [#125351](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125351) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reads of a data lake table such as `Iceberg` through the `merge` table function, a `Merge` table or a `Buffer` table (and, in a database with `lazy_load_tables`, through an `Alias` table), which returned the snapshot of the last direct query of that table and silently missed the rows of newer commits. `loop` over such a table now reports its current columns. [#125401](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125401) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `ALTER USER ... ADD IDENTIFIED WITH` no longer duplicates methods the user already has. Does not apply to passwords with a random salt (`sha256_password`, `scram_sha256_password`, `bcrypt_password`). Side effect: TOTP secret changes in the users config now apply on reload. [#125443](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125443) ([Andrey Zvonov](https://github.com/zvonand)).
* Fixed reading wrong data from a Compact part when a column whose substreams use different codecs (for example an `Array` column with `CODEC(Delta, ZSTD)`) is followed by a read that starts inside a shared compressed block: `SELECT a, c.y` could return the values of `c.x`, a mutation reading such a subcolumn could write them into the new part, and with `compress_per_column_in_compact_parts = 0` mutations could fail with `CANNOT_READ_ALL_DATA`. Closes [#118560](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/118560). [#125446](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125446) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a row-policy bypass where an authenticated user could forge the result of a scalar subquery used in a row policy (e.g. `USING has((SELECT ...), col)`) by sending a `Scalar` packet (as with `clickhouse-client --external ... --scalar`) named with the subquery's query-tree hash, and read rows the policy should hide. Client-supplied scalars are no longer accepted by the analyzer's scalar-subquery cache. <!-- CURSOR_AGENT_PR_BODY_END -->. [#125495](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125495) ([Yakov Olkhovskiy](https://github.com/yakov-olkhovskiy)).
* Reject HTTP requests with a header value that contains CR, LF or NUL after RFC 2047 decoding. Previously such a header listed in `forward_headers` of an HTTP authentication server could inject extra headers into the request to that server and allow authenticating as another user. <!-- CURSOR_AGENT_PR_BODY_END -->. [#125497](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125497) ([Yakov Olkhovskiy](https://github.com/yakov-olkhovskiy)).
* Fix batch object deletion fallback on S3-compatible stores like Minio when FIPS mode restricts the MD5 digest. [#125507](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125507) ([Craig Ringer](https://github.com/ringerc)).
* `RESTORE` of a `MySQL` table or database no longer fails with `CANNOT_RESTORE_TABLE` or `CANNOT_RESTORE_DATABASE` when the session sets `mysql_datatypes_support_level` to a value other than the default. [#125518](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125518) ([Vadim Lopatin](https://github.com/buggins)).
* Fixed `INSERT INTO FUNCTION file(...)` and `INSERT` into a `File` table (also with `PARTITION BY` and through `Alias` and `TimeSeries` tables) with `max_execution_time` and `timeout_overflow_mode = 'break'`: the query reported success but left the file unfinalized, so a Parquet file had no footer and could not be read, and a CSV file ended with a truncated row. The file now holds the rows written before the time limit. [#125523](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125523) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `DoGet` over the Arrow Flight interface failing with `Check failed: (chunks_.size()) > (0)` when a SQL query ticket returns no rows. It now returns an empty result with the query's schema. [#125534](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125534) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed formatting of the function composition operator `f | g` inside `CODEC`, `STATISTICS`, backup engine arguments and the `EXPLAIN SYNTAX` output of non-`SELECT` queries: it was written as a call to `__compose`, which could not be run back. [#125567](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125567) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a server crash in `INSERT ... SELECT` with parallel replicas (`parallel_distributed_insert_select = 2`) when the `SELECT`'s own `SETTINGS` rule out the local plan that the explicit `INSERT`-level settings allow, for example `INSERT INTO t SETTINGS parallel_replicas_local_plan = 1 SELECT ... SETTINGS parallel_replicas_local_plan = 0` (likewise `parallel_replicas_prefer_local_replica`, or a `max_execution_time_leaf` that differs between the two). Closes [#122729](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122729). [#125616](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125616) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reject malformed ORC timezone data with an error instead of aborting. [#125651](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125651) ([Sergey Kuznetsov](https://github.com/skuznetsov-clickhouse)).
* Improved logic of matching read steps between single-node and parallel replicas plans. This fixes a potential wrong query result in case of an incorrect matching and also makes the automatic parallel replicas optimization applicable to more queries. [#125687](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125687) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Fixed a bucketed `Map` (`map_serialization_version = 'with_buckets'`) where `m[0.0]` did not find the key `-0.0`, and `m[-0.0]` did not find the key `0.0`. The same applied to a zero inside a `Tuple`, `Array` or `JSON` key. [#125713](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125713) ([Ashish Kumar Choubey](https://github.com/aschoube)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` when the `optimize_and_compare_chain_max_hash_work` budget ran out between two copies of an expression, for example a `SELECT` alias of an `AND` chain of comparisons that is reused in `GROUP BY`. The transitive comparisons of `optimize_and_compare_chain` are now derived for the whole query or not at all. [#125735](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125735) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a client hang after the server closed an idle connection. A process started by the server, for example an `executable_pool` dictionary source, inherited the client connections that were open at that moment and kept them open, so the client did not see the close and its next query or HTTP keep-alive request never got an answer. [#125753](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125753) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `_row_id` and `_last_updated_sequence_number` staying `NULL` for rows written before an Iceberg table was upgraded to format version 3 by another engine, when ClickHouse made the first commit after the upgrade: `INSERT` now assigns row ids to those data manifests and advances `next-row-id` past them, as the Iceberg spec requires. Closes [#125334](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125334). [#125754](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125754) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed queries with `extremes = 1` and `additional_result_filter`: the filter was applied to the extremes rows, so the query failed with `Got 1 in extremes chunk, expected 2`, lost its extremes, or returned wrong extremes for a `UNION ALL`. The result filter now leaves the extremes unchanged, like `LIMIT` does, and no longer evaluates its predicate on them. [#125805](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125805) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the `Function node ... is not resolved` exception for a `JOIN USING` key taken from a projection alias over an `ALIAS` column with `analyzer_compatibility_join_using_top_level_identifier = 1`, a regression in 26.10. Closes [#124842](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124842). [#125818](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125818) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* For users with a time-based one-time password, the error `REQUIRED_SECOND_FACTOR` is now returned whenever the code is missing, regardless of whether the password is correct. Previously a wrong password without a code returned `AUTHENTICATION_FAILED`, which allowed guessing the password without the second factor. The password is verified only together with the code. The error maps to HTTP status 403 instead of 500. `clickhouse-client` prompts for the password before the code when neither is given, so users with `no_password` and TOTP now press Enter at the password prompt. [#125823](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125823) ([Vladimir Cherkasov](https://github.com/vdimir)).
* Fix `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` and wrong results when a `GROUP BY` key is used inside an `if` chain with `optimize_if_chain_to_multiif = 1`, or is a tuple used in `ORDER BY` (for example `if(1, tuple(a), tuple(0)) AS k ... GROUP BY k ORDER BY k`). With `group_by_use_nulls = 1`, such an `if` key returned its value instead of `NULL` in `ROLLUP`, `CUBE` and `GROUPING SETS` subtotal rows. Closes [#125803](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125803). Closes [#125804](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125804). [#125828](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125828) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `LOGICAL_ERROR` `Duplicate announcement received for replica number N` (or `Initiator received more initial requests than there are replicas`) when a view whose body contains a correlated subquery is read by a query with parallel replicas enabled and `correlated_subqueries_use_in_memory_buffer = 0`, through `cluster` or a `Distributed` table, or directly with `parallel_replicas_allow_view_over_mergetree = 1`. [#125849](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125849) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A MergeTree table with `add_minmax_index_for_string_columns` (or the numeric / temporal variant) and an `ALIAS` column that calls `dictGet`, `joinGet` or an executable user-defined function can now be loaded when that dictionary, `Join` table (or its database) or function is missing, as an executable function is at every startup. A table written before 25.12 whose definition or Keeper metadata lists its implicit indices loads too, also with such an index over an `ALIAS` expression, which failed with `Index with name ... already exists`. [#125861](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125861) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed text-search functions (`hasToken`, `hasAnyTokens`, `hasAllTokens`, `hasPhrase`) in the SELECT list of a query with `ORDER BY` over a table with a `text` index. [#125878](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125878) ([Anton Popov](https://github.com/CurtizJ)).
* Fixed a server crash (or missing rows in the view, with a `Null` source table) on `INSERT` into the source table of a materialized view when a row policy or `additional_table_filters` of a table read by the view references the source table, e.g. `USING x IN src`. [#125893](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125893) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The setting `http_zlib_compression_level` now applies only to the `gzip` and `deflate` HTTP response compression. The level of other methods (`zstd`, `lz4`, `br`, `xz`, `bz2`) is controlled by `output_format_compression_level`. Previously, a level suitable for zlib was passed to every method, which could make `lz4` unexpectedly slow or make `xz` fail. [#125899](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125899) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* A query that started while CPU slots were busy, or whose threads were stopped by CPU preemption, now starts threads for the slots granted to it later as soon as they are granted. Previously such slots could stay unused for seconds, and other queries could not use them either. [#125901](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125901) ([Sergei Trifonov](https://github.com/serxa)).
* Fix the `backups.allowed_path` check for relative `File()` backup destinations: the destination is now normalized before the check, so a relative path whose `..` leaves every allowed entry is rejected even when one allowed entry contains another. Closes [#125871](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125871). [#125911](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125911) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `MaterializedPostgreSQL` database that replicates a whole PostgreSQL schema not resuming replication after a server restart if a new table was created in PostgreSQL after the database. [#125962](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125962) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `x + INTERVAL 0 DAY` (also `WEEK`, `MONTH`, `QUARTER`, `YEAR`, `addDays(x, 0)` and the other `add*`/`subtract*` functions) returning a value earlier than `x` by the DST shift when `x` is a `DateTime` or `DateTime64` in the second occurrence of a repeated hour after clocks are turned back. [#125965](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125965) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a crash on `INSERT` when a materialized view reads its source table on the right of `IN` or in a `JOIN` over a `Buffer` of a `Distributed` table. The IN subqueries of the view query now also read the inserted block when a `merge` table with a `Distributed` child plans the query again for the source table. [#125990](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125990) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a crash when reading a 7z archive whose entry has no file name with the `archive :: path` syntax of `file`, `s3`, `s3Cluster` and the other table functions (the same bytes saved with a `.tar` name crashed too). Such an entry is now read as a file with an empty name. Closes [#125940](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125940). [#126006](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126006) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed unsorted or wrongly grouped results of `ORDER BY`, `LIMIT BY` and `GROUP BY` in order when a `Decimal` or `DateTime64` column is compared with a float, an `Array` column with an array of an incompatible type, or a `Tuple` column with a `Variant`/`Dynamic` element with a string literal. [#126019](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126019) ([Samuel Krempaský](https://github.com/NIKTONIKTO717)).
* Fixed `least` and `greatest` with two floating-point arguments returning a different result for a `NaN` argument depending on the argument order and on whether the expression was JIT-compiled, so the same query could change its result from its 4th execution at default settings. A `NaN` argument now loses against any number, as in the variadic form. Closes [#120728](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120728). [#126022](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126022) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `ALTER TABLE ... DROP PARTITION` for Iceberg tables. When the parent snapshot was an `overwrite`, the command failed with `Duplicate key in SnapshotSummary`, or it copied the delete counters of the parent snapshot to the new snapshot. [#126023](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126023) ([Den Kalantaevskii](https://github.com/Diskein)).
* Fixed `Reading in parallel from replicas is enabled but cluster to execute query is not provided` (`CLUSTER_DOESNT_EXIST`) when selecting from a `Distributed` table whose cluster is not declared in `remote_servers` - the cluster of a `Replicated` database, or one found by cluster discovery - with parallel replicas enabled. [#126032](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126032) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Fixes `LIMIT n BY ALL` treating a constant of the `SELECT` list with an integer value (for example `5`, `now()` or a `Date` value) as a positional argument when `enable_positional_arguments` is enabled (the default). Previously such queries failed with `Positional argument number ... is out of bounds`, or returned wrong results when the value was a valid position. [#126064](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126064) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results of a `WHERE` equality between float values over a comma, `CROSS` or `INNER` join, or in a correlated `EXISTS`: a pair of `NaN` values was returned, although `nan = nan` is false. [#126071](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126071) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed missing rows in a `JOIN` converted to `IN` by `query_plan_convert_join_to_in = 1` when the query condition cache is enabled: the cache could serve the filter result of one query to another query with a different right side, or to the same query after the right table changed. [#126072](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126072) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed positional arguments (`GROUP BY 1`, `ORDER BY 1`) being ignored in the queries a server analyzes itself while it executes a query received from another server: a materialized view with `GROUP BY 1` no longer rejects an `INSERT` forwarded through `remote` or a `Distributed` table with `NOT_AN_AGGREGATE` (and a background `Distributed` insert no longer gets stuck re-inserting its rows), and row policies, `view`, `executable` and parameterized views read through `remote` return the same rows as a direct query. [#126075](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126075) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a filtered query over an Iceberg table missing rows that hold a NaN in a `Float32`/`Float64` column, for example `WHERE x != 3` skipping a data file that holds `3, NaN, 3`. Iceberg float bounds leave NaN out, so min/max pruning dropped such a file on a negated or chained predicate. It now prunes on such predicates only when the manifest's `nan_value_counts` shows the column holds no NaN, and it ignores a bound that is NaN. Closes [#118112](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/118112). [#126078](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126078) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a server abort when inserting into a NATS table whose `nats_subjects` setting is empty or blank. The NATS engine now rejects such definitions with `BAD_ARGUMENTS`. [#126080](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126080) ([Sridhar Muruganandham](https://github.com/sri102info)).
* Fixed missing rows in a query over a `Distributed` table whose `GLOBAL IN` or `GLOBAL NOT IN` filter is pushed down from an outer query (for example `SELECT x FROM (SELECT * FROM dist) WHERE x GLOBAL IN (SELECT y FROM t)`) when the local replica of a shard lags behind `max_replica_delay_for_distributed_queries` and the shard is read from another replica. The other replica now receives the initiator's set instead of evaluating the subquery against its own data. Closes [#120979](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120979). [#126084](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126084) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `has`, `notHas`, `indexOf`, `countEqual`, `indexOfAssumeSorted`, `mapContainsKey` and `mapContainsValue` comparing an `Enum` value with the strings of a constant array by the enum's number instead of its name, so `WHERE has(['a', 'b'], enum_column)` returned no rows. A `bloom_filter` index on an `Enum` column no longer throws `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM` for `has` with a string that is not in the `Enum`. [#126090](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126090) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a crash (stack exhaustion) when a hash function such as `sipHash64` or `cityHash64` is applied to a `Dynamic` or `JSON` value whose nesting is deep enough to exhaust the thread stack. Such a value now raises `TOO_DEEP_RECURSION` instead of terminating the server. Closes [#123940](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123940). [#126095](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126095) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... DELETE`, `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE` and `DELETE FROM` on Iceberg tables of a `DataLakeCatalog` database failing with `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER` when parallel replicas are enabled. Closes [#122161](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122161). [#126097](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126097) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SAMPLE` with `ORDER BY ... LIMIT` returning too few or wrong rows when the sort column has a `minmax` skip index. Closes [#122563](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122563). [#126103](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126103) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `extract` returning a truncated match, and `match` missing matching rows (also through primary-key analysis), for a regular expression with an empty flag group `(?)`. Also fix `match` missing rows when `?`, `*` or `{0,n}` applies to a multi-byte character, such as `abcdé?x`, or comes after a flag group or an empty `\Q\E` quote, such as `(?:abcd)(?s)*x` or `abcd\Q\E?x`. Closes [#121275](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121275). [#126107](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126107) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `DROP TABLE` in a `DataLakeCatalog` database now respects the query-level setting `iceberg_delete_data_on_drop`. [#126110](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126110) ([Asya Shneerson](https://github.com/asya-ch)).
* Fixed `replaceRegexpAll` and `regexp_replace` replacing only the first match when the pattern starts with an optional `^`, such as `^?`, `^*` or `^{0,3}`: `replaceRegexpAll('xfx', '^?x', 'Z')` returned `Zfx` instead of `ZfZ`. Closes [#121494](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121494). [#126111](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126111) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SELECT ... FINAL` returning stale rows when a data-skipping index is on a floating-point primary key column and one key has versions with `0.0` and `-0.0`, or with different NaN payloads. Closes [#122346](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122346). [#126118](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126118) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `Unexpected value 0 in enum` (`UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM`) thrown by `has`, `notHas`, `indexOf`, `countEqual`, `indexOfAssumeSorted`, `mapContainsKey` and `mapContainsValue`, and by `arrayExists` and `IN` rewritten to `has`, when a `Nullable(Enum)` array element, map value or searched value is NULL. Closes [#122366](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122366). [#126119](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126119) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed crashes of the interactive line editor of `clickhouse-client` and `clickhouse-local`, which also crashed the server when used through the web terminal or an SSH session: after a history search that found nothing on an edited recalled query, when the prompt or the line given to an Alt-R history search contained a tab, when leaving a history search with a double-width character in the prompt (for example in the server's `display_name`), and on Ctrl-W or Alt-. after some non-ASCII characters. Closes [#126008](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126008). [#126120](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126120) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Iceberg and Paimon tables no longer take their metadata from a file in a subdirectory of `metadata/` (`schema/`, `snapshot/` for Paimon) or in a sibling directory whose name starts the same way, such as `metadata_old/`. Such a file, for example a copy of another table's metadata, used to replace the table's schema and rows. `OPTIMIZE TABLE` on an Iceberg table no longer deletes objects in such directories, such as `data_backup/` or `metadata/nested/`. Closes [#126049](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126049). [#126125](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126125) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a segmentation fault in `GROUP BY` with the adaptive aggregator and external aggregation when a memory limit was hit while draining staged records. [#126126](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126126) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix vector search queries on a `vector_similarity` index with the `dotProduct` distance function when `vector_search_with_rescoring` is disabled (the default): the query returned the least similar candidates in reverse order and reported `dotProduct` as `1 - dotProduct`. Closes [#124229](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124229). [#126128](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126128) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `s3`, `s3Cluster`, `azureBlobStorage`, `gcs`, `hdfs` and the other object storage table functions returning the data of the first entry for every entry of an archive that contains several entries with the same name (for example a tar archive to which `tar -r` appended a file again). The row count and schema caches of these functions and of `file` now also tell such entries apart. Detecting the format of a non-Parquet archive entry by its content in these functions no longer fails with `CANNOT_DETECT_FORMAT`. [#126129](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126129) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed vector search with a `vector_similarity` index and without rescoring (the default) returning rows that the index did not select, with a `_distance` of 1000, when the selected rows made up most of a granule. When the true distances were above 1000, these rows displaced the real nearest neighbours. Closes [#124230](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124230). [#126135](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126135) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong query results after one `ALTER TABLE ... RENAME COLUMN` statement renamed columns onto names it freed (for example `RENAME COLUMN a TO tmp, RENAME COLUMN b TO a, RENAME COLUMN c TO b`) on a Wide part: the mutated part kept another column's statistics, so part pruning by statistics skipped parts that matched the filter. [#126140](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126140) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a `LOGICAL_ERROR` when automatic parallel replicas cannot match prepared sets between the single-node and replica plans, including queries using `query_plan_convert_join_to_in`. These queries now retain the single-node plan. [#126141](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126141) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Fixed wrong results of `distinctJSONPaths` with the default `optimize_functions_to_subcolumns = 1`: with `OVER (...)` it returned a single row or failed with `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK`; it returned wrong paths when rows were filtered, multiplied or changed before the aggregation (JOIN, ARRAY JOIN, SAMPLE, row policies, `additional_table_filters`, a `WHERE` next to a subquery, lightweight deletes and updates, on-the-fly mutations, a pending `ALTER`); other aggregates of the same query such as `count()` were wrong; and with `aggregate_function_group_array_action_when_limit_is_reached = 'discard'` it dropped paths beyond the `groupArray` size limit. The rewrite now uses the conditions of the trivial `count()` optimization. Closes [#122645](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122645). [#126143](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126143) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results of queries with parallel replicas and `parallel_replicas_prefer_local_replica = 0` when part of the answer comes from a projection, for example `count()` with a primary key range, `min`/`max`/`count()` over the whole table, or a projection that only some parts have: the rows answered by the projection were lost. Closes [#123958](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123958). [#126144](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126144) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Dictionaries declared in configuration files are now known when the tables are attached at server startup. Previously a `PROJECTION` using `dictGet` on such a dictionary could be skipped after a restart, and the dependency of a table on the dictionary was ignored. [#126157](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126157) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix segfault when reading a `MySQLDump` file whose `CREATE TABLE` query has an `ENUM` column without a list of values or with non-string values. [#126159](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126159) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `SHOW TABLES` and queries on a `DataLakeCatalog` database with `catalog_type = 'rest'` when a namespace name in the catalog contains a space, a non-ASCII character or a URL delimiter such as `?`, `#` or `/`: `SHOW TABLES` failed for the whole database with `Bad URI syntax`, or requests went to the wrong path. Namespace levels are now percent-encoded in REST request paths. Closes [#126053](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126053). [#126166](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126166) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed outer `JOIN` with `join_algorithm = 'full_sorting_merge'` failing with `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` when `optimize_and_compare_chain` derived a bound from `ON` that the join evaluates on its preserved side: for a key over several tables (for example `a.number + b.number` after an inner join, a `FULL JOIN ... USING` key, an `ARRAY JOIN` alias combined with a column, or a key with a lambda), for a bound derived within the other side (from `r.a < r.b AND r.b < 2`), and, with `optimize_redundant_comparisons = 0`, for a derived bound that contradicts another one or cannot hold for the column type. [#126170](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126170) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading a table of a `DataLakeCatalog` database whose location in the catalog contains characters that are not valid in a URL, such as a space or a non-ASCII character. Previously `SELECT` failed with `Bad URI syntax`. [#126172](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126172) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an `INSERT` failing with `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` when `enable_positional_arguments_for_projections` was enabled for it and a projection of the table had an integer constant in `GROUP BY`, like `GROUP BY 'a', 1`. [#126185](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126185) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ipcryptEncrypt`, `ipcryptDecrypt`, `ipcryptPrefixEncrypt` and `ipcryptPrefixDecrypt` throwing `Cannot parse IP address from string ''` on `LowCardinality(String)` columns and on `Nullable(String)` columns containing NULL, although every value was a valid IP address. [#126186](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126186) ([David Meng](https://github.com/davidmenggx)).
* Fixed wrong results when converting `Float32` values to `Int128`, `UInt128`, `Int256`, `UInt256`, `Decimal128` or `Decimal256`, and `BFloat16` values to the four wide integer types: a negative fraction such as `toInt128(toFloat32(-0.5))` returned `-18446744073709551616`, values of magnitude `2^63` or more were clamped to about `2^63`, and negative `BFloat16` values wrapped around. [#126191](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126191) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix truncation of IPv4-compatible and IPv4-mapped IPv6 addresses with a leading zero IPv4 octet, which could produce incorrect HOST IP output and break distributed queries. [#126201](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126201) ([Craig Ringer](https://github.com/ringerc)).
* Fixed a Keeper hang with the on-disk LSM tree storage (`use_lsmt_storage = true`, `storage_memory_only = false`): a background flush or merge could loop forever at 100% CPU when a compressed block group started exactly at the end of the file write buffer, after which Keeper writes were throttled to a few per second. [#126213](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126213) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results when an outer `WHERE` compares columns of a `remote()` or `Distributed` subquery that gives a column the name of another one (`SELECT w AS k ...`) and `optimize_and_compare_chain` derives an `indexHint` from it. Such derived hints are now also used for primary key analysis below `GROUP BY` and `LIMIT BY`. [#126219](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126219) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Hide the credentials of a presigned S3 URL that the remote server echoes in its error response body, such as the access key id and the signature in a `SignatureDoesNotMatch` error, in the exception of a failed HTTP request, `system.query_log` and the server log. [#126229](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126229) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results of an outer `JOIN` or `ANY JOIN` when `isConstant` is applied to a joined column: the rewrites enabled by `query_plan_convert_outer_join_to_inner_join`, `query_plan_convert_outer_join_to_inner_join_transitively` and `query_plan_convert_any_join_to_semi_or_anti_join` could drop or add rows, or change the result of `isConstant`. [#126232](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126232) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `POLYGON`, `POLYGON_INDEX_CELL` and `POLYGON_INDEX_EACH` dictionaries of small polygons (for example building outlines) returning a polygon that does not contain the point, or the default value for a point inside a polygon that crosses an index cell boundary. Polygons with coordinates beyond about `1.8e19` no longer disappear from `POLYGON_INDEX_CELL`. A polygon tens of millions of units away from the others no longer hides the polygons near the opposite side of the bounding box, and the grid layouts reject polygons more than about `3.4e38` apart with a clear error. Closes [#122018](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122018). [#126246](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126246) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix wrong results after external GROUP BY and max_rows_to_group_by>0 (can lead to excessive spills with `max_rows_to_group_by>0` and `group_by_overflow_mode=any`, but only in case of spilling limit is small). [#126249](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126249) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Fixed `timeSlots` with a `DateTime` start time reducing a constant `Size` argument that does not fit in `UInt32` modulo 2^32 (for example, `600 + 2^32` produced 600-second slots). Such a `Size` is now rejected with `ARGUMENT_OUT_OF_BOUND`. Closes [#125947](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125947). [#126261](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126261) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `JSONExtract*`, `JSONHas`, `JSONType`, `JSONKey`, `JSONLength` and the other JSON functions with `indices_or_keys` returning an element for an integer index outside the `Int32` range: the index was taken modulo 2^32, so `JSONHas('[10,20,30]', 4294967297)` returned 1. Such an index is now out of range, like any index past the end. Closes [#125944](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125944). [#126266](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126266) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
