* Fixed a heap-use-after-free in the view dependency graph after renaming a materialized view whose query selects from itself. `CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW` and `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY QUERY` now reject a query that makes a materialized view select from itself. [#126292](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126292) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix wrong values and the exceptions `Unexpected format of compact discriminators granule` and `Invalid type for SerializationLowCardinality index column` when reading several subcolumns of the same `JSON`, `Variant`, `Dynamic` or `LowCardinality`-containing column with `use_columns_cache = 1`. [#126298](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126298) ([Pavel Kruglov](https://github.com/Avogar)).
* Fixed `existing_files_count` in the manifest list written by `INSERT` into an Iceberg table with `iceberg_format_version = 1`: it held the total number of data files of the table instead of 0. Closes [#121222](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121222). [#126307](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126307) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `Coordinator for parallel reading from replicas is not initialized` exception for a distributed `INSERT SELECT` from a cluster table function (or a table function turned into its `*Cluster` variant by `parallel_replicas_for_cluster_engines`) when the target table has a materialized view that joins a `MergeTree` table and parallel replicas are enabled. The view now receives the inserted rows. [#126320](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126320) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Fixed `multiMatchAny`, `multiMatchAnyIndex`, `multiMatchAllIndices` and the other vectorscan-based `multiMatch*` functions matching strings that a negated character class excludes. A class such as `[^A-Za-z]`, `[^[:alnum:]]` or `\W` kept only some of its members, so `multiMatchAny('a', ['[^A-Za-z]'])` returned `1`. [#126332](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126332) ([Eliah Rusin](https://github.com/franchb)).
* Fix `match`, `extract`, `extractAll`, `regexpExtract`, `countMatches`, `splitByRegexp`, `merge`, `regexp_tree` dictionaries and n-gram skip indexes missing matches for a regular expression with a named capture group whose name contains `_` or a non-ASCII letter, such as `(?P<user_id>\d+)`. Closes [#126202](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126202). [#126346](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126346) ([Pulkit Chauhan](https://github.com/pulk17)).
* Fixed `JSONExtract*` on native `JSON` values ignoring `json_type_escape_dots_in_keys` when building a path. A literal dotted key now resolves to its own value instead of silently returning the value of a nested path with the same name. [#126365](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126365) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fixed `system.database_replicas` returning an empty `zookeeper_exception` when that column was selected without another Keeper-backed status column. [#126367](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126367) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fixed `editDistanceUTF8` (`levenshteinDistanceUTF8`) returning the length in bytes instead of code points when one of the arguments is an empty string. [#126511](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126511) ([sz-kristof](https://github.com/sz-kristof)).
* Fix `INSERT` into Amazon S3 Tables (a `DataLakeCatalog` database with `catalog_type = 's3tables'`) failing with `S3TablesUnsupportedHeader` since 26.10. Added the setting `s3_use_idempotency_id` to disable the idempotency id in the metadata of objects uploaded to S3 for S3-compatible storages that reject user-defined object metadata. [#126513](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126513) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a partitioned Iceberg `INSERT` or `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE` failing with `CANNOT_SCHEDULE_TASK` when it writes many partitions, because every finished Parquet data file kept its encoder threads until the end of the query. Closes [#126508](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126508). [#126519](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126519) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed tables whose engine arguments override a named collection key with an expression, for example `IcebergLocal(nc, path = concat('t_', toString(now64(9))))`: the expression was evaluated again on `DETACH`/`ATTACH` and on server restart, so the table could point to another location and fail with `FILE_DOESNT_EXIST`. The table definition now stores the value the expression had at `CREATE`, as it already does for positional arguments. [#126521](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126521) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix wrong results of `arrayReduceInRanges` for a range with a huge length or with a start before the beginning of the array. Such ranges are now clipped to the array, the same as in `arraySlice`. [#126531](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126531) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Function `repeat` with a 128- or 256-bit count larger than `UINT64_MAX` now throws `TOO_LARGE_STRING_SIZE` instead of silently returning a short string. [#126532](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126532) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `widthBucket` returning 0 for an operand above the range when the count is the maximum value of `UInt32`. For the maximum `UInt64` count, where the result does not fit, it now throws an exception. [#126533](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126533) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed short-circuit evaluation of `CASE`, `if`, and `multiIf` when an unselected branch contains exact integer division that would raise an exception. [#126535](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126535) ([Ilya Yatsishin](https://github.com/qoega)).
* Fix the uncompressed cache being disabled when `merge_tree_max_rows_to_use_cache` and `merge_tree_max_bytes_to_use_cache` are set to their maximum value, and the same wraparound for `merge_tree_min_rows_for_seek` and `merge_tree_min_bytes_for_seek`. [#126536](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126536) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a stack overflow when a Redis server replies with deeply nested arrays. [#126547](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126547) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a stack overflow on a deeply nested query plan received in the `QueryPlan` packet of the native protocol. [#126548](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126548) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Bloom filters of skip indexes respect the memory limits, so creating large filters fails with `MEMORY_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` instead of the server being killed by the OOM killer. Text bloom filter indexes (`tokenbf_v1`, `ngrambf_v1`, `sparse_grams`) with the total size of filters in a granule (filter size multiplied by the number of columns) over 1 GiB are rejected on creation. [#126549](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126549) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a stack overflow on a Protobuf schema with a very long chain of nested messages. [#126550](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126550) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix use-after-free in complex key `ssd_cache` dictionaries when the cache file wraps around its partitions. Fix `CANNOT_READ_ALL_DATA` when reading long values of `ssd_cache` dictionaries from the cache file. [#126553](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126553) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix wrong values in the result of `ORDER BY ... WITH FILL ... INTERPOLATE` with `prefer_column_name_to_alias = 1` when an `INTERPOLATE` target has the name of a selected alias but refers to a source column: the alias column showed the interpolated source column on every row. [#126561](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126561) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Read Parquet files written by parquet-cpp before Arrow 3.0 whose compressed `DataPageV2` pages are marked as uncompressed, as Arrow C++ does. [#126566](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126566) ([Jay Pokale](https://github.com/JayPokale)).
* Fix `Nested type ... cannot be inside Nullable type` error in JSON functions returning `Array` or `LowCardinality` (`JSONExtractKeys`, `JSONExtractKeysAndValues`, `JSONExtractKeysAndValuesRaw`, `JSONExtractArrayRaw`, `JSONExtract`) when an argument is `Nullable`. Null rows now return the default value (e.g. an empty array). [#126570](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126570) ([Shintaro Yoshida](https://github.com/syoshida20)).
* Fix a crash when writing a constant `Geometry` column in the `Parquet` format. [#126571](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126571) ([Jay Pokale](https://github.com/JayPokale)).
* Fix `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` when a query on `file`, `url` or object storage selects a subcolumn together with its column (or a JSON subcolumn) and another condition is moved to PREWHERE. [#126573](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126573) ([Jay Pokale](https://github.com/JayPokale)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY COLUMN` failing with a syntax error when it replaces an `EPHEMERAL` column declared without an expression by a `DEFAULT`, `MATERIALIZED` or `ALIAS` one, and dropping the expression of `EPHEMERAL <expr>` in the same case. [#126577](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126577) ([Serhiy Bzhezytskyy](https://github.com/serhiy-bzhezytskyy)).
* Fixed the primary key and `minmax` indices skipping matching rows when a `Decimal` column was compared with a float constant under a key expression such as `toFloat64(d)`, for example `WHERE d = toFloat32(1)`, or with the floats of an `IN` subquery. Such a comparison with a `BFloat16` value no longer fails with "Conversion from BFloat16 to Decimal is not implemented". [#126584](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126584) ([sz-kristof](https://github.com/sz-kristof)).
* Fix a data race in the `Memory` table engine: `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY SETTING` running concurrently with `INSERT` into the same table could make the `INSERT` read settings that were already freed. [#126597](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126597) ([Vadim Lopatin](https://github.com/buggins)).
* Fix a data race in the `EmbeddedRocksDB` table engine: `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY SETTING` running concurrently with `INSERT` into the same table could make the `INSERT` read settings that were already freed. [#126598](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126598) ([Vadim Lopatin](https://github.com/buggins)).
* `perimeterCartesian` and `perimeterSpherical` now return the length of a `LineString` or `MultiLineString`. Previously they returned 0 for these types. [#126620](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126620) ([Adegbite Ayoade Abel](https://github.com/tripleaceme)).
* Fixed `ARGUMENT_OUT_OF_BOUND` (`Decimal value is too big`) in `remote`, `Distributed` and parallel-replicas queries with a `Decimal` constant that has more digits than the precision of its type, such as `toDecimal32(0.6, 9) + toDecimal32(0.6, 9)` (a `Decimal(9, 9)` equal to `1.2`) or `toDecimal64(1, 18)`. [#126652](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126652) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading hive-partitioned files when the partition value of the sampled path is the text `NULL` or `null` (or `[]`, `{}`): the partition column was inferred as a type with `Nothing` (`Nullable(Nothing)` for `NULL`) and every value was read as NULL, or the query failed. Such a column is now inferred as `LowCardinality(String)`, as is one whose sampled value is empty (it was `String`). Closes [#126609](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126609). [#126684](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126684) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Setting `force_primary_key` now applies only to the primary key of what is actually read. Previously, a query that filtered by the table's primary key failed with an `INDEX_NOT_USED` exception if the table had a projection whose key was not constrained by the filter. Now such a projection is simply not used to serve the query, and a projection forced with `force_optimize_projection` whose key is unused throws `PROJECTION_NOT_USED`. [#126694](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126694) ([János Benjamin Antal](https://github.com/antaljanosbenjamin)).
* Fixed `parseDateTime`, `parseDateTime64` and `parseDateTimeInJodaSyntax` returning a time one hour off next to a daylight saving time change. For example, `2026-10-25 01:00:00` in `Europe/Berlin` was parsed as `02:00:00`. A PM time with a time zone name in the string, such as `01:30:00 PM Europe/Berlin` with the format `hh:mm:ss a z`, is no longer parsed 12 hours late. [#126704](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126704) ([sz-kristof](https://github.com/sz-kristof)).
* Reading a brotli-compressed file now accepts several concatenated brotli streams, so a file that a second `INSERT` appended to (`File` table engine or `file` table function, path ending in `.br` or `compression_method = 'br'`) can be read again. Previously every read of such a file failed with `BROTLI_READ_FAILED`, including the rows of the first `INSERT`. [#126757](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126757) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an `INSERT` into an `ORC` or `Avro` `Iceberg` table terminating the server, or committing a data file with wrong or missing field ids so that later queries returned wrong results, when a column the `INSERT` writes was dropped or renamed away while it was running. Such an `INSERT` is now rejected with `INCORRECT_DATA`, as it already was for `Parquet`. [#126768](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126768) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a server or `clickhouse-local --path` refusing to start with `Code: 1001 STD_EXCEPTION` when the directory of a `Filesystem` database cannot be accessed, for example because it became a symlink loop or lost traverse permission. Such a database now loads with no tables and logs a warning, the same as a missing directory. `CREATE DATABASE ... ENGINE = Filesystem(...)` over such a path, including one with a component longer than `NAME_MAX`, now fails with `BAD_ARGUMENTS` instead of `STD_EXCEPTION`. Closes [#121705](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121705). [#126772](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126772) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix signed integer overflow when formatting a `Time64` value that holds the minimal `Int64`. [#126780](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126780) ([Valery Petrov](https://github.com/valerypetrov)).
* Fix wrong results of a text index search combined with `LIKE` on the same column when one searched token occurs in very few rows. [#126781](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126781) ([Valery Petrov](https://github.com/valerypetrov)).
* Fix `TOO_MANY_PARTITIONS` for queries on tables with `max_partitions_to_read` whose filter rules out partitions only through a skip index: with `use_skip_indexes_on_data_read` enabled and a cold query condition cache, parts that the skip index would drop were counted against the limit. Skip indexes are now applied during index analysis when `max_partitions_to_read` is set. [#126792](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126792) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Always skip checking `max_estimated_execution_time` when `timeout_before_checking_execution_speed` is 0. [#126817](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126817) ([Jonathan-Ackerman](https://github.com/Jonathan-Ackerman)).
* Fixed a heap-buffer-overflow when merging a corrupted `uniqCombined`, `uniqCombined64` or `uniqHLL12` state. Such states, and states whose rank counts or stored number of zero registers don't add up, now throw `INCORRECT_DATA`. [#126840](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126840) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `Field ... not found in Avro schema` on every query reading a column of an `Avro` Iceberg table after an `INSERT` was committed while `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN` added that column. A column whose Iceberg field id is not in an `Avro` data file is now read as `NULL`, as the Iceberg spec requires. [#126879](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126879) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `BSONEachRow` output writes column names, `Tuple` element names and `Map` keys as they are instead of JSON-escaping them (`a/b` was written as `a\/b`), so the files read back correctly. A zero byte in a name is still written as an escape sequence. [#126885](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126885) ([Jay Pokale](https://github.com/JayPokale)).
* The `Npy` input format reads NumPy unicode string arrays (`<U<n>`) as UTF-8 instead of corrupting non-ASCII characters, and keeps zero bytes inside byte strings (`|S<n>`), removing only the trailing padding. [#126887](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126887) ([Jay Pokale](https://github.com/JayPokale)).
* Fix a segmentation fault when reading the `MySQLDump` format if the dump contains `CREATE TABLE ... LIKE` or a `CREATE TABLE` without column definitions. The structure is now inferred from the `INSERT` data. [#126905](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126905) ([Ashish Kumar Choubey](https://github.com/aschoube)).
* Fix a server abort during `Protobuf` schema inference with `input_format_protobuf_skip_fields_with_unsupported_types_in_schema_inference = 1` when a `map` field has a value of an unsupported type. Such `map` fields are now skipped. [#126906](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126906) ([Ashish Kumar Choubey](https://github.com/aschoube)).
* Keeper no longer pre-allocates memory for the array size declared in a request (for example the ACL list in `Create`) before it reads the array elements. Before this fix, a small malformed request could cause a large memory allocation. [#126914](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126914) ([Antonio Andelic](https://github.com/antonio2368)).
* Iceberg tables created or written through `DataLakeCatalog` with `catalog_type = 'glue'` now register their columns in the Glue table definition, so they can be queried by Athena and other Glue consumers. Previously the Glue entry had an empty column list and Athena failed with `COLUMN_NOT_FOUND: Relation contains no accessible columns`. [#126924](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126924) ([Melvyn Peignon](https://github.com/melvynator)).
* Fix projections with a `WHERE` clause whose `ORDER BY` reads columns the projection does not select, such as `SELECT _part_offset WHERE s = 'a' ORDER BY (v, k)`: every `INSERT` and `MATERIALIZE PROJECTION` failed with `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER`. Projections with a `WHERE` clause also no longer store their `ORDER BY` expression (for example `tuple(v, k)`) as an extra column, also when the projection query gives another expression the name of such a column, for example `SELECT * REPLACE (v + 1 AS v)`. Fix wrong results when the `WHERE` of a projection reads an alias named like a column, for example `SELECT 100 - v AS v, count() WHERE v = 90 GROUP BY v`: a query filtering the column `v = 90` was answered from the projection. [#126936](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126936) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong strings, sometimes followed by an `UNKNOWN_PACKET_FROM_SERVER` error, when a sparse `Nullable(String)` column is sent over the native protocol to `clickhouse-client` or between servers by `remote`, `cluster` and `Distributed`, for example `toString(nullIf(x, 0))` over a sparse column, or `toString` of a `Nullable` column stored with `nullable_serialization_version = 'allow_sparse'`. Closes [#126854](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126854). [#126937](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126937) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `count()` with `WHERE d = toDate32('1900-01-01')` (or `!=`) over a MergeTree `Date32` column returning the number of `1970-01-01` rows when `optimize_trivial_count_with_sparsity_filter` is enabled (the default since 26.8). Closes [#126856](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126856). [#126942](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126942) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `SELECT ... INTO OUTFILE ... APPEND` in `clickhouse-client` and `clickhouse-local` now fails with `CANNOT_APPEND_TO_FILE` when the file is not empty and the format does not support appends (`JSON`, `Parquet`, `Arrow`, `ORC`, `Avro` and others), as `INSERT INTO FUNCTION file` already does. Previously a second complete document was written, and readers returned only the first export's rows or failed. When appending to a non-empty file, the header of formats such as `CSVWithNames` is no longer written again. Closes [#126853](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126853). [#126944](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126944) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a crash (use-after-free) when an `EmbeddedRocksDB` table was truncated while a query read a `DIRECT` dictionary whose source is that table, for example to load another dictionary from it. [#126947](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126947) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a lightweight `UPDATE` on a partitioned `ReplicatedMergeTree` table that silently skipped rows inserted through another replica right before it, although `update_sequential_consistency` made it wait for them. The same applied to `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE` with `alter_update_mode = 'lightweight'` and to `DELETE` with `lightweight_delete_mode = 'lightweight_update'`. Closes [#126876](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126876). [#126952](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126952) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A MergeTree table with an index named like an implicit min-max index (`auto_minmax_index_<column>`) but of another type or over an `ALIAS` column can now be loaded with `add_minmax_index_for_numeric_columns` (or the string, temporal, `_block_number` or `_block_offset` variant) enabled, and `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN` or `RENAME COLUMN` to that column name no longer fails. Both failed with `Index with name ... already exists`. The existing index is kept and the column gets no implicit index. [#126989](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126989) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a web terminal or SSH session that never ended, with its query still running, after the server had started an `executable` or `executable_pool` process during that session. The process inherited the session's pseudo-terminal or pipes, so closing the web terminal did not stop its query, and an `INSERT` fed through `ssh` never saw the end of its input. [#126995](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126995) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `RENAME TABLE`, `EXCHANGE TABLES` and `RENAME DATABASE` for an `S3Queue` or `AzureQueue` table whose startup failed during `CREATE`. The rename failed with the `LOGICAL_ERROR` `Table with storage id ... is not registered` and left the table hidden under both names, or the database half renamed, until the server restarted. [#127004](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127004) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a server abort or a `LOGICAL_ERROR` ("Invalid number of rows in Chunk") in `ORDER BY ... WITH FILL ... INTERPOLATE` when the alias of an `INTERPOLATE` target is the name of another column of the query, for example when `count()` is aliased to its own name so that it can be interpolated. Such queries return the same result as in 25.12 again. Closes [#126941](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126941). [#127012](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127012) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `polygonConvexHullCartesian` returning garbage for a polygon with an empty exterior ring and a non-empty interior ring. [#127015](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127015) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `supports_append` in `system.formats` showing `0` for output formats that can be appended to, such as `TSV`, `CSV` and `Native`. It now matches the check that an `INSERT` into the `File` engine or the `file` table function uses; input-only formats still show `0`. `is_tty_friendly` now shows `0` for input-only formats. [#127026](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127026) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results from a projection whose `SELECT` gives an expression the name of a column (e.g. `100 - v AS v`) after the projection was added, or the table was altered, in a session with `prefer_column_name_to_alias = 1` and the server was restarted. An unrelated `ALTER` of a table with such an aggregate projection in that session no longer fails with `Cannot apply ALTER because it breaks projection`. Projection queries are now analyzed with the server's value of `prefer_column_name_to_alias`, as on table load. [#127079](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127079) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a bug where an `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE` of a column on a `MergeTree` table was silently lost when it was queued while a `CLEAR COLUMN` of that column (or a `DROP COLUMN` before it was re-added) was still pending; on 26.3 and older the mutated part became unreadable. `MATERIALIZE INDEX` and `MATERIALIZE PROJECTION` queued after `DROP INDEX` / `DROP PROJECTION` were lost the same way. [#127124](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127124) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `make_distributed_plan` queries that reported `EXCHANGE_PEER_DISCONNECTED` instead of the real error when the failed task took a long time to free its resources, e.g. to remove a large spill. [#127126](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127126) ([Alexander Gololobov](https://github.com/davenger)).
* Fix insufficient privilege checks when restoring access entities. Replacement with `create_access='replace'` now requires the corresponding `DROP` privilege, and partial revokes in a backup cannot reduce the privileges required for restoration. [#127136](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127136) ([Nikolai Ovchinnikov](https://github.com/kolsha)).
* `MaterializedPostgreSQL` now builds the sorting key of a replicated table in the order of the primary key or replica identity index columns. Before, primary key columns followed the table column order and replica identity columns were sorted alphabetically. [#127137](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127137) ([AI-Stratov](https://github.com/AI-Stratov)).
* Fixes a crash in estimateCompressionRatio with codecs DoubleDelta and Gorilla. [#127139](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127139) ([Robert Schulze](https://github.com/rschu1ze)).
* Fix a stack overflow crash, and memory usage quadratic in the nesting depth, in queries over a `Dynamic` value nested thousands of levels deep through `Array(Dynamic)`, such as one read from crafted `RowBinary` input, for example in `arraySort([d, d])`, `any(d)`, `arrayJoin` or a filter. Such queries now fail with a `TOO_DEEP_RECURSION` error or use memory linear in the depth. [#127190](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127190) ([Pavel Kruglov](https://github.com/Avogar)).
* Fix reading `metrics` from `system.all_metric_log` with the `bucketed` schema of `system.metric_log` after an upgrade: it failed with `CANNOT_CONVERT_TYPE` or showed the values of older rows under wrong metric names. [#127195](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127195) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* Fix a server crash (stack overflow) when a query in the `clickhouse_json` dialect or a call to `formatQueryFromJSON` contains very deeply nested JSON. [#127210](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127210) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Fix a use-after-free when a query that is still waiting for a CPU slot is cancelled (for example with `KILL QUERY`) under `cpu_slot_preemption = false`: the time spent waiting for the slot was written into the counters of a thread that had already exited, which could corrupt memory or crash the server. [#127242](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127242) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results of `SELECT ... FROM <GraphiteMergeTree table> FINAL WHERE <condition on the time or path>`. The condition moved to `PREWHERE` and ran before `FINAL` merged the rows. [#127253](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127253) ([Yaroslav Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Remove Iceberg orphan files on failure. [#127275](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127275) ([Smita Kulkarni](https://github.com/SmitaRKulkarni)).
* Fixed a crash in `MsgPack` schema inference when the first key of a map is `nil`, an empty array or an empty map. Such a column is now reported as impossible to infer, like a `nil` value, and its type can be given with `schema_inference_hints`. [#127281](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127281) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix stack overflow on a cursor of a huge depth, either in `STREAM CURSOR` of a query or in the refresh cursor stored in the Iceberg metadata of a target of an incremental refreshable materialized view. [#127286](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127286) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The `RawBLOB` output format now writes the serialized state of `AggregateFunction` columns (the same as their conversion to `String`) instead of an in-memory pointer. [#127288](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127288) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The Kusto dialect's `bin` and `bin_at` functions now accept columns of type `DateTime`, `Date` and `Date32`, e.g. `T | summarize count() by bin(EventTime, 1d)`. Previously they threw an exception unless the value was a `DateTime64`. [#127327](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127327) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The `MySQLDump` input format now infers `SET` columns as `String`, as the `mysql` table engine does, instead of `UInt64`, which could not read their values. [#127338](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127338) ([Jay Pokale](https://github.com/JayPokale)).
* GeoParquet files written by ClickHouse no longer have `crs` set to the string `EPSG:4326`, which is invalid in GeoParquet and made DuckDB reject them; `crs` is omitted, meaning `OGC:CRS84` (longitude, latitude). [#127343](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127343) ([Jay Pokale](https://github.com/JayPokale)).
* Fix reading Parquet files written by old versions of Arrow that have a column of the null type (`converted_type` 24): they failed with `Invalid converted type 24`. [#127345](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127345) ([Jay Pokale](https://github.com/JayPokale)).
* Fixed an abort of `clickhouse-local`, and of the whole server for web terminal and SSH sessions, when an `INSERT` failed after its query had already finished, for example an `INSERT` whose `SELECT` names the `input` table function without reading it. [#127364](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127364) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ssd_cache` and `complex_key_ssd_cache` dictionaries returning another key's value from `dictGet` after the cache had wrapped around its partitions, for a key whose value was cached twice (fetched again after it expired, or returned twice by the source). Closes [#127031](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/127031). [#127369](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127369) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a server or `clickhouse-local --path` refusing to start when a table that another object depends on cannot be reached at startup, for example a DDL dictionary reading a table of a `Filesystem` database whose file or directory became a symlink loop or lost traverse permission, or of a `PostgreSQL` database whose server is down. Closes [#127300](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/127300). [#127377](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127377) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a data part being detached as `broken-on-start` (its rows disappearing from the table) after a single transient I/O error (`EIO`) while reading the part's metadata at server startup or `ATTACH TABLE`. The load is now retried, and an I/O error marks the part broken only when the load has failed on all 3 attempts. Closes [#127233](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/127233). [#127381](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127381) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix int96 overflow in parquet reader. [#127401](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127401) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* Fixed a skip index whose type was written in uppercase (e.g. `TYPE TEXT(...)`) not getting the default granularity the lowercase form gets. For a text index this made its mark-cache prewarm throw (wedging a `SharedMergeTree` replica's startup) and could make queries using the index silently return fewer rows than a full scan. [#127407](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127407) ([Shaohua Wang](https://github.com/tiandiwonder)).
* Fixed the `Cannot fold actions for projection` logical error for queries with `join_use_nulls = 1` in which a `LEFT` or `RIGHT JOIN` is turned into an inner join by a `WHERE` condition and then reordered by the join order optimizer, for example `a JOIN b ON ... LEFT JOIN c ON b.x = c.x ... WHERE c.v = 'x'`. Closes [#127331](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/127331). [#127416](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127416) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Update `brotli` from v1.2.0 to v1.2.1, fixing an out-of-bounds write in the brotli decoder reachable through brotli-compressed input (HTTP `Content-Encoding: br`, `.br` files, Parquet). [#127419](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127419) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Fix `wkt` rounding coordinates to 6 significant digits. Coordinates are now written with the shortest representation that round-trips, as in the text output of `Float64`, e.g. `POINT(139.699325900317 35.67639759983872)` instead of `POINT(139.699 35.6764)`. [#127431](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127431) ([Shintaro Yoshida](https://github.com/syoshida20)).
* Fix a logical error when insert deduplication removes all rows of a partition block before it is merged on insert into a ReplacingMergeTree or another special MergeTree engine, e.g. when coalesced async inserts with the same insert deduplication token push to a partitioned materialized view target. [#127439](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127439) ([János Benjamin Antal](https://github.com/antaljanosbenjamin)).
* The `Arrow` and `ArrowStream` input formats again read nulls in fields declared non-nullable as NULLs instead of rejecting the data, so they are converted to default values when `input_format_null_as_default` is enabled (the default). This restores the behavior of the Apache Arrow library reader that was removed in 26.8. [#127453](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127453) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fix a heap-buffer-overflow in `GROUP BY ... ORDER BY aggregate LIMIT n` with short `String` keys when `enable_packed_string_keys_in_aggregation` is disabled. [#127496](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127496) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `DROP`, `DETACH` and server shutdown of a materialized view failing when its `TO` table cannot be resolved, for example when its `DataLakeCatalog` is unreachable. [#127585](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127585) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* Pad the buffer an Azure read owns with PADDING_FOR_SIMD. [#127593](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127593) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Fix `WHERE` filters on `Nullable(Bool)` columns of the `MongoDB` table engine and table function, which silently returned no rows for conditions like `b = true` or `b IN (...)`. [#127602](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127602) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fixed a heap buffer over-read when parsing a text format such as `CSV` from object storage through the userspace page cache (`use_page_cache_for_object_storage = 1`). [#127603](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127603) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading from a local disk with `min_bytes_to_use_direct_io` and `local_filesystem_read_prefetch` enabled: after a seek back, a read could return data from a wrong file offset, which showed up as wrong values of a `Variant` subcolumn or as the exception `Size of deserialized variant column less than the limit`. [#127614](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127614) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix values silently changed when inserting into the `JSON` type or using `JSONExtract` into `Dynamic`: once a path held a `Date` or `DateTime` value, a later date or time outside its range was saturated, fractional seconds were dropped, and `JSONExtract` replaced invalid array elements with `NULL`. Such values now get their own type. [#127622](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127622) ([Pavel Kruglov](https://github.com/Avogar)).
* Check the source table SELECT grant in parallel_distributed_insert_select. [#127627](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127627) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Fix server failing to start once after an upgrade when all_... system log tables are replaced on startup. [#127630](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127630) ([Kseniia Sumarokova](https://github.com/kssenii)).
* Fix `RESTORE` from `S3` ignoring the `backup_restore_s3_retry_*` settings for the upload into the destination disk when a server-side copy is not used, which made it retry on the `s3_retry_attempts` of that disk instead and give up earlier than the restore was configured to. Also log when a copy of a byte range does not use the server-side copy and reads and reuploads the range instead. [#127638](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127638) ([Julia Kartseva](https://github.com/jkartseva)).
* Fixed `geoToUTM`, `UTMToGeo`, and `geoToMGRS` for latitudes just below the equator: the band letter and the false northing now agree, the round trip returns the original coordinates, and `geoToMGRS` returns a square that `MGRSToGeo` accepts. [#127655](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127655) ([Vevan O Narain](https://github.com/Vevan05)).
* Fixed `SYSTEM RESTORE REPLICA` leaving a replica behind the metadata version of the table when the replicas were restored at the same time (for example with `ON CLUSTER`) or one after another, after which every `ALTER` on that replica failed with `CANNOT_ASSIGN_ALTER`. Closes [#127629](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/127629). [#127656](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127656) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `INSERT INTO <replicated or Distributed table> SELECT ... FROM s3Cluster(...)` (and other object storage cluster table functions, or `s3` converted to `s3Cluster` by parallel replicas) writing the default values of the column types (`0`, empty string, `1970-01-01`) instead of the `DEFAULT` expressions of the destination table for fields missing in the source files. [#127657](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127657) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* The settings `iceberg_insert_max_bytes_in_data_file` and `iceberg_compaction_max_bytes_in_data_file` now limit the compressed size of a data file, as written to the storage, instead of the uncompressed size of the data. This is consistent with the Iceberg property `write.target-file-size-bytes` and with `iceberg_data_file_size_lower_threshold_compaction`, and fixes the Iceberg compaction endlessly rewriting files that compress well. [#127670](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127670) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Throw an exception instead of aborting the server when an Azure Blob Storage endpoint omits the optional `ETag` header in a response to blob download, `GetProperties`, or listing. [#127673](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127673) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `MaterializedPostgreSQL` no longer retries a failing synchronization in a tight loop, which could make `DETACH TABLE` hang for minutes. [#127678](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127678) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `EmbeddedRocksDB`, `KeeperMap` and `Redis` tables with a floating-point primary key returning only one of the rows stored under `-0.` and `0.` for `WHERE key = 0` (and `IN`, `OR`, multi-column and `Tuple`/`Array`/`Map` key lookups), and `ALTER TABLE ... DELETE` / `UPDATE WHERE key = 0` skipping the other row. [#127685](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127685) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `text_log.level` having no effect when it is set to a more verbose level than `logger.level`: messages below `logger.level` never reached `system.text_log`, neither at startup nor after a configuration reload. Behaviour is unchanged when `text_log.level` is not set. [#127717](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127717) ([Nikita Vaniasin](https://github.com/nikita-vanyasin)).
* Fix null values being stored as strings or numeric zero in `Dynamic` columns when reading `VALUES`, CSV, and TSV, including with `input_format_null_as_default` disabled. [#127725](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127725) ([Mihai Zegheru](https://github.com/mihaizegheru)).
* The `obfuscate` table function and `clickhouse obfuscator` no longer move `DateTime` and `DateTime64` values to the previous date on days when DST starts at midnight, e.g. in `America/Havana`, `Africa/Cairo` and `America/Santiago`. [#127749](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127749) ([oleg-milanov](https://github.com/oleg-milanov)).
* Fixed incremental updates (`update_field`) of dictionaries with a `ClickHouse` source. The lower bound was sent as the server's local time, so it was shifted when the column's time zone differed from the server's (for example, with a `DateTime('UTC')` column on a server east of UTC, new rows were never loaded), and in the hour repeated at the end of DST every update re-read the last hour of rows. The bound is now sent as a Unix timestamp for non-text columns. [#127765](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127765) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix loss of coordinate precision when inserting `GEOGRAPHY` values into BigQuery, including repeated fields. [#127767](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127767) ([Ashish Kumar Choubey](https://github.com/aschoube)).
* Fixed a leak of objects in the bucket of an S3 or Azure disk when `DROP TABLE`, `DETACH TABLE`, `SYSTEM STOP MERGES` or server shutdown cancelled a merge or mutation before it committed its result part. [#127813](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127813) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reading an Apache Paimon table throws an exception instead of returning wrong results when a data file was written before a column was renamed, or before a column was dropped and another one with the same name was added. Before, the renamed column read as `NULL` and the re-added column returned the dropped column's values. Closes [#127545](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/127545). [#127816](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127816) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `obfuscate` table function and `clickhouse obfuscator` no longer move `DateTime` values to another century, e.g. from `1970-01-01` to `2106-02-07`, when the source value is on the first or the last local day of the `DateTime` range, e.g. `toDateTime(0)` in `Asia/Tokyo` or `toDateTime(4294967295)` in `UTC`. [#127819](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127819) ([oleg-milanov](https://github.com/oleg-milanov)).
* Fix a `LOGICAL_ERROR` `Cannot write to canceled buffer` in the server when the terminal of an SSH or Web Terminal session is closed while the embedded client shows the progress of a query. [#127834](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127834) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed wrong results when the query condition cache served the entry of a filter on one column to a filter on another column whose name and type spell the same text, e.g. `WHERE a` on `a UInt8` after `WHERE aU` on `aU Int8` of the same table returned no rows. [#127851](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127851) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix async inserts with deduplication in dependent materialized views failing with `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` when the output of a view for one batch exceeded `min_insert_block_size_rows` or `min_insert_block_size_bytes`, even if the view reduces rows (e.g. a `GROUP BY` with many groups). The view output of a batch is now written as one block, limited only by the memory limits of the query and the server. [#127852](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127852) ([Christoph Wurm](https://github.com/cwurm)).
* Fixed a server crash in a distributed query (`remote`, `Distributed`) with `ARRAY JOIN` over an `AggregateFunction` column with an older state version, e.g. a `uniq` state column of a table created before 26.10. [#127876](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127876) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixes a crash when a deeply nested `Dynamic` or `JSON` value is sent to or received from a remote server, for example as a constant in a `remote()` query or as a column read through `remote()`. Such a value now raises `TOO_DEEP_RECURSION`. [#127880](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127880) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an abort of `clickhouse-client`, `clickhouse-local` and `clickhouse-format --hilite` when highlighting a query whose string literal ends with an incomplete UTF-8 sequence, for example `SELECT length(x'E2')`. Such a query is now highlighted without reading past its end, and the closing bracket gets its own color. [#127906](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127906) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a possible server crash (use-after-free) when a web terminal session is closed while the client asks whether to output a binary format such as `RowBinary`. [#127912](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127912) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a failed query staying in `system.processes` while the web terminal or SSH session that ran it was idle. Such a query counted toward `max_concurrent_queries_for_user`, and `KILL QUERY ... SYNC` on it hung. [#127913](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127913) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix Azure `extra_credentials(client_id = ..., tenant_id = ...)` being lost from the table definition after `DETACH`/`ATTACH` or a server restart, and failing when the arguments are rewritten for `azureBlobStorageCluster` / parallel replicas. [#127932](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127932) ([Omar Eladawy](https://github.com/omar12eladawy)).
* Fixed `SHOW CREATE DATABASE` and `SELECT * FROM system.databases` failing with `SYNTAX_ERROR` when a `Filesystem`, `S3` or `HDFS` database had a quote or a backslash in its path, URL or keys. [#127934](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127934) ([Vadim Lopatin](https://github.com/buggins)).
* Fixes a query hanging during planning when `max_rows_to_read` with `read_overflow_mode = 'throw'` is below the rows of a table where a normal projection exists in only some parts. `EXPLAIN projections = 1` now also lists the projections of such a read. [#127937](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127937) ([Vincent Voyer](https://github.com/vvo)).
* Fix the server failing to start with `PATH_ACCESS_DENIED` when a local disk directory cannot be created. The disk is now reported as broken, as in versions before 26.7. [#127941](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127941) ([Luís Lizardo](https://github.com/lizardoluis)).
* Fixed a gRPC call that never returned when its query failed while writing the result (for example with `MEMORY_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`). The client now receives the query's exception. [#127991](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127991) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an exception (`LOGICAL_ERROR` `Bad cast from type ...` or `ILLEGAL_COLUMN`) in distributed queries that select the same column more than once with `LIMIT` or `distributed_group_by_no_merge = 2`, when the column type of the `Distributed` table differs from the type on the shards. [#128005](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128005) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `Merge` table evaluating the query's `PREWHERE` and `WHERE` on rows that a row policy of a local underlying table hides. A condition that throws on a value, such as `toUInt64(column) > 10`, printed values of the hidden rows. The policy is now applied by the underlying table's read before `PREWHERE`, as for a direct read of that table. [#128013](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128013) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `ExecutablePool` tables returning stale rows from a previous request after a query such as `LIMIT 0` or `WHERE 1 = 0` leaves the worker's reply unread. [#128014](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128014) ([Ashish Kumar Choubey](https://github.com/aschoube)).
* An `INSERT` that writes no rows into a `StripeLog` table on an S3 disk no longer makes every later `SELECT` from the table fail with `S3_ERROR` (The specified key does not exist). [#128040](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128040) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `SELECT` and `BACKUP` of a `StripeLog` table on an S3 disk failing with `S3_ERROR` (The specified key does not exist) after an `INSERT` that wrote no rows, including such tables written by 26.3 and read after an upgrade. [#128042](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128042) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a regression in 26.8: with `compatibility` set to a version before 26.5 and `date_time_input_format = 'best_effort'`, `JSONExtract` silently returned `1970-01-01 00:00:00` for ISO 8601 `DateTime` values. `compatibility` with versions before 26.8 now restores the old behavior, controlled by the new setting `json_extract_use_cast_string_to_date_time_mode`. [#128048](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128048) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Fixed `LEFT` and `FULL JOIN ... USING` with `join_use_nulls = 1` onto an `EmbeddedRocksDB` or `KeeperMap` table, or onto a `MergeTree` table with `join_algorithm = 'direct'`, when the right `USING` column has a different type than the left one (for example `LowCardinality(String)` and `String`). Such queries failed with `Bad cast ... to DB::ColumnLowCardinality` or `Block structure mismatch`. [#128049](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128049) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix possible duplicate rows in StorageKafka2: on retry, consume exactly the same batch of messages as the failed insert, so deduplication can drop it. [#128053](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128053) ([János Benjamin Antal](https://github.com/antaljanosbenjamin)).
* Fixed missing rows with a `set` skip index when an outer `WHERE` compares columns of a subquery that gives a column the name of another one (`SELECT w AS k ...`), for example over `remote()`, and `optimize_and_compare_chain` or `optimize_or_like_chain` derives an `indexHint` from it. [#128079](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128079) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Query cancellation and timeouts within the merge tree reader no longer mark parts as potentially broken and trigger part checks. [#128083](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128083) ([Andy Bradshaw](https://github.com/andybradshaw)).
* Fixed `EmbeddedRocksDB`, `KeeperMap` and `Redis` tables with a `Decimal`, `DateTime64` or `Time64` primary key returning no rows for a float constant such as `WHERE key = 19.99` or `key IN (SELECT 19.99)` when the key holds the matching value, and `ALTER TABLE ... DELETE` / `UPDATE` with such a condition changing nothing. Such a condition now reads the whole table unless it is a `Float32` or `Float64` constant proven to match only its cast to a `Decimal32` or `Decimal64` key (`key = 12.5`); a constant written in the key's type (`key = '19.99'`) still uses the key lookup. [#128086](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128086) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `clickhouse-client` and `clickhouse-local` printing the totals or extremes of a query that returned no rows before the beginning of the output (for example before `meta` in `JSON`, or before the header row in `TSVWithNames`), which produced an invalid document. [#128107](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128107) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed missing rows in a `JOIN` whose probe-side key is a narrowing `CAST` of an indexed column compared with a key of a wider type, for example `ON CAST(t.k AS UInt8) = d.k` with `d.k` of type `UInt64`: the JOIN runtime filter pruned granules by the column value without the inner cast. Such keys are no longer used for primary key, skip index or seal-gated pruning. [#128128](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128128) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `CurrentMetrics` values drifting upward when objects holding a metric increment are move-assigned (for example removed from a vector with `std::erase_if`), visible as an ever-growing `IcebergCompactionCleanupTasks`. [#128163](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128163) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Fix `SELECT ... FINAL` from a `GraphiteMergeTree` table, which returned several partially rolled-up rows for one rollup bucket when the parts were read in parallel layers, split into non-intersecting ranges or merged per partition. Closes [#127522](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/127522). [#128165](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128165) ([Yaroslav Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Fixed a crash of the server and of `clickhouse-keeper-client` when ZooKeeper answers with an error code that ClickHouse does not know, for example `QUOTAEXCEEDED` (-125) from Apache ZooKeeper 3.7+ with `zookeeper.enforceQuota=true`. Such a code is now reported as `API error` or `System error`. [#128176](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128176) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `PARTITION BY` of `file`, `url` and object storage writes (table functions and table engines) evaluating stateful and non-deterministic functions anew for every block since 26.1: `PARTITION BY intDiv(rowNumberInAllBlocks(), N)` restarted its counter on every block and wrote rows into the wrong files, and `now64()` produced one file per block instead of one per `INSERT`. Closes [#128211](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128211). [#128217](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128217) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Lazy materialization for LIMIT queries over joins now also works when the query has ARRAY JOIN: the columns of a table below the ARRAY JOIN are read only for the rows that the LIMIT returns. [#128218](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128218) ([Nikolai Kochetov](https://github.com/KochetovNicolai)).
* Fix ZNONODE race when reading StorageKafka2 topic partition locks: a lock vanishing between `getChildren()` and `get()` no longer aborts the polling round. [#128237](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128237) ([Anton Yackushev](https://github.com/yackushevas)).
* Fixed wrong results of filtering and sorting a `Date32` column through `toStartOfInterval` or `dateTrunc` with the legacy `Date` / `DateTime` result types (the only behavior before 26.11; now `compatibility` below 26.11, `to_start_of_interval_preserves_argument_type = 0` or `function_date_trunc_return_type_behavior = 1`). Values outside the result range wrap, but the primary key and partition analysis treated the rounding as monotonic, so rows were skipped, counted wrongly or returned out of order. Closes [#116945](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/116945). [#128282](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128282) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `round`, `roundBankers`, `floor`, `ceil` and `trunc` of integers with `|N| >= 20`: a constant `N` threw `BAD_ARGUMENTS`, and with a negative `N` 128/256-bit integers were rounded to a multiple of `2^64 - 1` (`floor(toInt128('100000000000000000005'), -20)` returned `92233720368547758075` instead of `100000000000000000000`). [#128283](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128283) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a segmentation fault in `ReverseIndex::insert` when an exception (typically `MEMORY_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`) was thrown while a `LowCardinality` dictionary was building its reverse index and the dictionary was then reused, for example by the next entry of an asynchronous insert flush. [#128291](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128291) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Fixed lost, duplicated or corrupted rows when a `Pretty*` result was streamed over gRPC (`ExecuteQueryWithStreamOutput`) while consecutive blocks were squashed (`output_format_pretty_squash_consecutive_ms`, enabled by default). [#128292](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128292) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixes `round`, `roundBankers`, `floor`, `ceil` and `trunc` of `Float32`, `Float64` and `BFloat16` values returning `inf` when the scaled value overflows (`round(1e300, 10)`, `floor(toFloat32(3.4028235e38), -2)`) and a wrong value for `|N| >= 20` (`round(1.5e-20, 20)` returned `0`). `inf` is still returned when the exact result is beyond the range of the type (`ceil(1., -400)`, `round(1.7e308, -308)`). Closes [#132573](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/132573). Closes [#132575](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/132575). [#128321](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128321) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a server abort while reading files from a `zip` archive (for example with `s3`) when the query reaches its memory limit. [#128325](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128325) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` when a query over Parquet data (`file`, `url`, `s3` and other object storages) selects a subcolumn such as `n.null` or `arr.size0` and an explicit `PREWHERE` consumes its column, for example `SELECT countIf(n.null) FROM file('x.parquet') PREWHERE n > 5`. The same fix applies to ORC data files in an Iceberg table with the Parquet format. [#128326](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128326) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `system.query_log` recording both `QueryFinish` and `ExceptionWhileProcessing` for a gRPC query whose final result could not be delivered to a disconnected client, and a `LOGICAL_ERROR` `Weak pointer to process_list_elem expired` in the same case for `SHOW ...` queries. [#128328](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128328) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Iceberg tables created with `iceberg_format_version = 1` now carry the `schema` and `partition-spec` fields that format version 1 requires, so PyIceberg and other spec-following readers can open them, and an `INSERT` adds them to such tables written by earlier versions. For a format version 1 table, the current schema is now read from `schemas` and `current-schema-id`, so a table whose schema PyIceberg evolved is no longer read with its old schema. Closes [#128301](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128301). [#128332](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128332) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `floor`, `ceil`, `round` and `roundBankers` of an integer with a negative `N` returning a wrong value, often with the wrong sign, when the argument is close to the limit of its type or `N` is large for the type, although the rounded value fits the type. For example, `round(toInt32(2000000000), -9)` returned `-1000000000`. [#128333](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128333) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix reading the `keys` and `values` subcolumns of a `Map` column (`m.keys`, `m.values`, also nested like `t.x.keys`) from Parquet files, data lakes and object storages: they returned empty arrays unless the query also read the whole map. Also fix `m.key` returning empty arrays when a Parquet map is read with an `Array(Tuple(key, value))` structure. Closes [#113976](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/113976). [#128334](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128334) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reading an Apache Paimon table now uses its latest schema file, as Spark, Flink and pypaimon do, so a column added, renamed or dropped by `ALTER TABLE` is reflected before the next commit. Before, ClickHouse used the schema of the latest snapshot, and for example `SELECT` of an added column failed with `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER`. Closes [#128308](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128308). [#128335](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128335) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading of Parquet files with data pages that have no values. Arrow C++ (e.g. pyarrow) writes such pages in `Array`, `Map` and other repeated columns when pages are small, and these files were rejected with `Invalid offset index` or `Attempt to read after eof`. A data page v2 with an empty values section (all values are `NULL`) is also read now instead of failing with `CANNOT_DECOMPRESS`, and without the offset index a row that continues in the next data page v2 (arrow-go writes such pages) is no longer cut at a block boundary. Closes [#128300](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128300). [#128337](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128337) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading Hudi Copy-on-Write tables after `insert_overwrite`, `insert_overwrite_table` or clustering: the `hudi` table function and the `Hudi` table engine also returned the rows of the replaced file groups (duplicate and deleted rows) until the Hudi cleaner removed the old files. Parquet files of the archived timeline under `.hoodie/` are no longer read as data. A layout 2 table with a custom `hoodie.timeline.path` is now refused with `NOT_IMPLEMENTED`. [#128341](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128341) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `INSERT ... SELECT` from `s3Cluster`, `azureBlobStorageCluster` and `hdfsCluster` (and from `s3`, `azureBlobStorage` and `hdfs` with `parallel_replicas_for_cluster_engines`) writing the default values of the column types instead of the destination's `DEFAULT` expressions for fields missing in the files when the `INSERT` runs on the initiator: with `parallel_distributed_insert_select = 0`, into a non-replicated table, or into a `Distributed` table with a deterministic sharding key. With a TTL on such a column the rows were deleted right after insertion. Closes [#128299](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128299). [#128344](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128344) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `DEFAULT` expression that reads another column with a `DEFAULT` through a subcolumn (`d DEFAULT payload.v`) or through an `ALIAS`, `MATERIALIZED` or `EPHEMERAL` column: when an insert omitted that column only in some rows of a block, the rows that supplied it stored a value computed from its `DEFAULT` instead of the inserted one. A `DEFAULT` cycle through a subcolumn whose columns are omitted in different rows of one block now fails with `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER`; supplying the same column of the cycle in every row avoids it. [#128345](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128345) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed credentials leaking into the server log, `system.text_log` and OpenTelemetry spans through the HTTP interface: the `Request URI` trace line and the `clickhouse.uri` span attribute wrote the `query` parameter and `param_<name>` values verbatim, while the `executeQuery` line masks secrets in them. Their values are now logged as `[HIDDEN]`. Closes [#128373](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128373). [#128386](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128386) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `arrayIntersect` throwing `CANNOT_CONVERT_TYPE` or `NO_COMMON_TYPE` when one of the arguments is an array of `NULL`s, such as `arrayIntersect([1, NULL], [NULL])`. [#128425](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128425) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fixed `sequenceMatchEvents` returning the events of an abandoned matching attempt instead of the events of the chain that actually matched the pattern, when a time condition follows the last event. [#128434](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128434) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fix parsing empty trailing fields in `TSV` and `CSV` input with a variable number of columns and `input_format_tsv_empty_as_default` or `input_format_csv_empty_as_default` enabled, including truncated rows without a final newline. [#128436](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128436) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* `CASE expr WHEN ... THEN ... END` now respects `use_variant_as_common_type` and returns a `Variant` when the branch values have no common supertype, like `multiIf`, instead of throwing `NO_COMMON_TYPE`. [#128437](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128437) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fixed wrong results of `PREWHERE` when reading `Parquet` files with `DELTA_BINARY_PACKED` integer columns that need a conversion, such as `INT32` columns with the `INT_8` or `INT_16` converted type. [#128449](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128449) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a syntax error message that contained bytes read past the end of the query text when an `ARRAY[` was not closed before the `;`, for example `SELECT ARRAY[f(1); 2]`. The error points at the `;` again. [#128475](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128475) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix very slow queries with `FINAL` when both `query_plan_optimize_lazy_final` and `optimize_aggregation_in_order` are enabled. [#128484](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128484) ([Manuel](https://github.com/raimannma)).
* Fixed `INSERT ... SELECT` from `fileCluster` and `urlCluster` failing with `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER` when the files lack columns that have `DEFAULT` expressions in the destination table and the insert runs on the initiator. This was a regression in 25.3. Also fixed `urlCluster`, and `url` with `parallel_replicas_for_cluster_engines`, ignoring the structure of the destination table on the initiator. [#128492](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128492) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN` on Iceberg tables created by ClickHouse 26.6 or older: the new column could get the field id of an existing nested field (an `Array` element, a `Map` key or value, a `Tuple` element), and the table could no longer be read (`Duplicate field id`). New field ids now start after the largest id used by any schema of the table. Closes [#128455](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128455). [#128525](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128525) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Quote identifiers named `not`/`columns`/`interval`/`as`/`strict` when formatting queries. [#128543](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128543) ([Philip Dubé](https://github.com/serprex)).
* Fix unquoted JSON integers being silently wrapped when read into `Date` and `Date32` columns. They now follow the day-number or Unix-timestamp conversion rules of `toDate` and `toDate32` and respect `date_time_overflow_behavior`. [#128550](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128550) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* `count()` from files (`file`, `url`, object storages) now respects `input_format_allow_errors_num` and `input_format_allow_errors_ratio` and returns the same result as reading the rows. Previously the fast counting path and the count cache ignored these settings. [#128551](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128551) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* `dictGetOrNull` now returns `NULL` for a `NULL` key (a `NULL` literal or a `NULL` row of a `Nullable` key column) instead of throwing an exception, consistent with `dictGet` and `dictHas`. [#128552](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128552) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* `isIPAddressInRange` now accepts a `NULL` literal as the address and returns `0`, like a `NULL` value of type `Nullable(String)`, instead of throwing `ILLEGAL_TYPE_OF_ARGUMENT`. [#128553](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128553) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* `groupArraySorted` over `Float32`, `Float64` and `BFloat16` now orders NaN last, like `arraySort` and `ORDER BY`. Previously the result depended on the limit `N`, and real values could be dropped in favour of NaN. [#128555](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128555) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fixed wrong results of `PASTE JOIN` when the `ORDER BY` in its subqueries was removed by the `query_plan_remove_redundant_sorting` optimization, for example under an aggregation or an outer `ORDER BY`. [#128556](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128556) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fixed `system.all_query_log` failing with `TYPE_MISMATCH` on a server upgraded to 26.10 from an earlier release. A `Merge` table now reads a column whose unnamed tuple has fewer or more elements in a child table than in the `Merge` table: missing trailing elements get default values and extra ones are dropped, as for missing and extra columns. Closes [#128463](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128463). [#128561](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128561) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ON CLUSTER` queries and `system.distributed_ddl_queue` failing with `ASYNC_LOAD_WAIT_FAILED`, and distributed DDL from other hosts not being executed, on a server where one table failed to load at startup (for example, a table created by a newer version with a setting value this version does not know). [#128563](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128563) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed Parquet bloom filter and dictionary filter push-down skipping row groups whose `NULL` rows match an `IN` set containing `NULL` under `transform_null_in = 1`, or `has` with a constant array containing `NULL`. [#128570](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128570) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Update `sqlite-amalgamation` from 3.53.4 to 3.54.1. [#128580](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128580) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Fixed writes to Iceberg tables in a Glue `DataLakeCatalog` database that is also written by Spark, pyiceberg or another Iceberg writer: after a few alternating commits every `INSERT` failed with `Write into iceberg was not successful` and `ALTER TABLE ... DELETE` with `LIMIT_EXCEEDED`. Metadata files of catalog-backed tables are now named `<version>-<uuid>.metadata.json` as the Iceberg spec requires. [#128600](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128600) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Fixed merges of a table with `TTL ... GROUP BY` failing with `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` when none of the merged parts stores a column, e.g. after `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN` without a default or after a column TTL removed the column. Closes [#128051](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128051). [#128605](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128605) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed geometry functions such as `wkt`, `svg`, `polygonAreaCartesian` and `polygonsIntersectionCartesian` returning an empty result for a `MultiPolygon` or `MultiLineString` value that follows an empty one in the same block. Closes [#126843](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126843). [#128606](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128606) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `AsynchronousMetrics` (server and Keeper) logging `Cannot read from file .../cpu.max ... errno: 19` every second after the cgroup v2 `cpu.max` file it opened at startup was removed, for example when systemd disables the `cpu` controller after the last user session ends. The nearest `cpu.max` is now looked up on every update, so `CGroupMaxCPU` follows the controller being disabled and enabled again. Closes [#128137](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128137). [#128621](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128621) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the error `Support for more than one ARRAY JOIN in query is not implemented` for a query with more than one `ARRAY JOIN` that reads a `MySQL`, `PostgreSQL` or `SQLite` table. The error appeared in 26.10. [#128633](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128633) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed mutations hanging forever on a `ReplicatedMergeTree` table with `ttl_only_drop_parts = 1` after a mutation (`MATERIALIZE TTL`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE` or lightweight `DELETE`) rewrote a part whose rows had all expired: the empty part that replaced it got a different name than the replication queue expected. Closes [#128331](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128331). [#128644](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128644) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the error `Support for more than one ARRAY JOIN in query is not implemented` for a query with more than one `ARRAY JOIN` and a `LIMIT` that reads `numbers`, `numbers_mt`, `system.numbers`, `generate_series` or `primes`. The error appeared in 26.6. [#128653](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128653) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A user defined in the server configuration (`users.xml`) can no longer escape its own settings constraints by selecting another settings profile with `SET profile`, a `SETTINGS profile = ...` clause or the HTTP `profile` parameter. Since 26.10 such a switch replaced settings pinned with `CONST` or bounded by `MIN`/`MAX`, while a user created by SQL got `SETTING_CONSTRAINT_VIOLATION`. Closes [#128370](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128370). [#128657](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128657) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the error `Support for more than one ARRAY JOIN in query is not implemented` when reading from a view whose query has more than one `ARRAY JOIN`. It also failed every refresh of a refreshable materialized view reading such a view. The error appeared in 26.8. Closes [#128410](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128410). [#128662](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128662) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reading an Apache Hudi Merge-on-Read table with the `hudi` table functions or the `Hudi` table engine silently returned the old values from the base files when the latest changes were still in log files (updated rows came back with superseded values, deleted rows came back). Such a read now fails with `NOT_IMPLEMENTED`; compact the table in Hudi to read it. Copy-on-Write tables and compacted Merge-on-Read tables are read as before. [#128673](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128673) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Keeper no longer pre-allocates memory for the string length declared in a request before it reads the string bytes. Before this fix, a small malformed request could cause a large memory allocation. [#128674](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128674) ([Antonio Andelic](https://github.com/antonio2368)).
* Fixed wrong results and a server abort when a table was created again with the UUID of an earlier table, by `CREATE TABLE ... UUID`, by `ATTACH TABLE ... UUID` with a full definition, or by `Replicated` database replica recovery: the query condition cache and the uncompressed, mmap, userspace page and columns caches served the earlier table's data. The caches are now dropped in these cases. [#128677](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128677) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a crash and wrong `TYPE_MISMATCH` or `CANNOT_CONVERT_TYPE` errors when `contingencyState`, `cramersVState`, `cramersVBiasCorrectedState` or `theilsUState` is used as a window function and its result goes through a spill to disk, a `CAST`, or a distributed query plan. `contingencyMapMerge` and the other `-MapMerge` functions of this family now work as window functions. [#128685](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128685) ([Alexander Gololobov](https://github.com/davenger)).
* Reading an `Avro` file with an out-of-range union index now fails with `INCORRECT_DATA` instead of returning wrong rows (for a `["null", T]` union) or failing with `STD_EXCEPTION` (for a union read into `Variant`). Closes [#128635](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128635). [#128711](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128711) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reject malformed zero-row Bloom-filter skip-index granules instead of aborting the server. [#128727](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128727) ([Rahul Nair](https://github.com/motsc)).
* Fixed a crash in `ssd_cache` and `complex_key_ssd_cache` dictionaries after their cache file failed to grow (for example, when the disk is full): the next read of `system.dictionaries` or the next update of the dictionary aborted the server. Now the update fails with `CACHE_DICTIONARY_UPDATE_FAIL` and the dictionary stays usable. Closes [#128096](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128096).
