[#128730](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128730) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix reading a `Hive` table with an empty partition key when the Hive metastore returns a partition for it. [#128731](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128731) ([Asya Shneerson](https://github.com/asya-ch)).
* Fixed an exception (for example from `throwIf`) in a query whose `PREWHERE` had several substring conditions on one `String` column, pushed into the scan by `apply_string_filters_during_scan`, when the result of one of them was also used elsewhere in `PREWHERE`. [#128734](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128734) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix nested namespaces in Iceberg REST commit. [#128735](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128735) ([Aleksandr Musorin](https://github.com/AVMusorin)).
* Fixed the userspace page cache of local disks (`use_page_cache_for_local_disks`) returning the data of a removed file for a new file written at the same path, for example a `MergeTree` part written again after `TRUNCATE` and `DETACH`/`ATTACH TABLE`. [#128738](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128738) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `toUTCTimestamp` (`to_utc_timestamp`) returning a time one hour off for local times in the hours around a daylight saving time change, for both `DateTime` and `DateTime64`. It now converts a local time as `toDateTime(s, time_zone)` does, also a local time that happens twice or does not exist. Closes [#128266](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128266). [#128744](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128744) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed Iceberg tables created with `ORDER BY` on a `Tuple` element (for example `ORDER BY t.a`): the sort order was written with `source-id` 0, and every later `SELECT` and `INSERT` on the table failed with `SYNTAX_ERROR`. The sort field now refers to the element's field id and `INSERT` sorts by it, also under a transform such as `icebergTruncate(1, t.u.s)`. A sort column that is neither a column nor a primitive field of a tuple column (for example `arr.size0`) is now rejected at `CREATE` with `BAD_ARGUMENTS`, and tables already written with such a sort order are read and written as unsorted. Closes [#123753](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123753). [#128747](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128747) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading, merging and mutating parts with a `Geometry` column written by versions 25.11 to 26.7, which failed after an upgrade with `Stream g.MultiPoint.size0 ... is not found` because `MultiPoint` was added to `Geometry` in 26.8. The same applied to `Array(Geometry)` and `JSON(p Geometry)`. A `Nested` column with `Geometry` was read as `NULL` and a merge wrote the `NULL` back, and `MATERIALIZE COLUMN` of a `Geometry` column with a `DEFAULT` replaced the stored values with the default. Closes [#128592](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128592). [#128761](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128761) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reading a `Native` block no longer allocates memory for a column name, type name, binary-encoded type or `Dynamic`/`JSON` structure entry whose declared length the data does not back: a 7-byte input made ClickHouse allocate and zero 1 GiB that `max_memory_usage` did not refuse. A truncated name or type now fails with `ATTEMPT_TO_READ_AFTER_EOF` instead of `CANNOT_READ_ALL_DATA`. [#128768](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128768) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `formatReadableTimeDelta` no longer prints `0 seconds` before a smaller unit for values below one second. For example, `formatReadableTimeDelta(0.5, 'seconds', 'milliseconds')` now returns `500 milliseconds` instead of `0 seconds and 500 milliseconds`. [#128785](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128785) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* `roundToExp2` now returns NaN for a NaN argument instead of `inf`. [#128788](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128788) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fixed a bug when a string like `1` or `31` was parsed as a date time while inserting into a `JSON` or `Dynamic` column that already had a `DateTime` or `DateTime64` value in the same path: such strings became dates in the current year instead of staying strings. [#128791](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128791) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fixes `null` elements inside arrays, tuples, maps and nested objects being stored as empty defaults in the `JSON` and `Dynamic` data types when a matching type was already present in the column. For example, inserting `{"a":[null]}` after `{"a":[[1]]}` stored `{"a":[[]]}` instead of keeping the `null`. [#128792](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128792) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Keeper now fails startup if it cannot bind any explicitly configured Raft listener. [#128813](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128813) ([bbrown-oai](https://github.com/bbrown-oai)).
* Fix a crash of DDL queries in a `Replicated` database (including refreshes of refreshable materialized views) that run concurrently with `SYSTEM RESTORE DATABASE REPLICA` or `DROP DATABASE`. [#128824](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128824) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix the server hanging forever on shutdown while a query with `make_distributed_plan = 1` is running. [#128832](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128832) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* A `RESTORE` into a `Replicated` database can now be cancelled (`KILL QUERY`, server shutdown) while it waits for a restored table to appear; previously it kept waiting for up to `backups.create_table_timeout`. [#128849](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128849) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix undefined behavior in the asynchronous metrics of a disk whose `s3_max_get_rps` / `s3_max_put_rps` (or the Azure equivalents) is set to the maximum value. [#128858](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128858) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed wrong results of `ORDER BY` (unsorted rows, wrong rows under `LIMIT`) and the `GroupingAggregatedTransform pushed bucket ... twice` error (duplicated groups in older versions) of queries with `parallel_replicas_mode = 'sampling_key'`, or a custom key mode on a cluster with more than one shard. Such a query now reads a shard from one replica unless the initiator only aggregates or fully sorts the parts returned by the replicas. [#128865](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128865) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a crash when appending `PostgreSQLWire` output to a non-empty file, e.g. a second `INSERT INTO FUNCTION file(..., 'PostgreSQLWire')` into the same file or `SELECT ... INTO OUTFILE ... APPEND FORMAT PostgreSQLWire`. Closes [#127289](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/127289). [#128871](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128871) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `LOGICAL_ERROR` `Block structure mismatch in joined block stream` on `ALTER TABLE ... DELETE` of a `Join` table created with `join_use_nulls = 1` that has a `LowCardinality` column. [#128874](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128874) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE` and `CLEAR COLUMN` never completing on a Wide part, failing with `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` (`While executing TTL_CALC`) and retrying forever, when the table has several TTL rules and the mutation changes or clears the input of one rule but not of another. Such a mutation now reads the inputs of every TTL rule whose TTL info it recalculates. [#128882](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128882) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `LOGICAL_ERROR` when reading through the filesystem cache with `filesystem_cache_boundary_alignment` larger than the cache's `max_file_segment_size`. The alignment is now capped at `max_file_segment_size`. [#128883](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128883) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SYSTEM RELOAD CONFIG` not taking full effect for an increased `background_message_broker_schedule_pool_size` or another `background_*_schedule_pool_size` setting: the streaming consumers of `Kafka`, `RabbitMQ`, `NATS` and `FileLog` tables kept running at most as many at once as the startup pool size allowed, until a restart. This was a regression in 25.9. [#128899](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128899) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a reverse DNS lookup spinning forever at 100% CPU and holding the DNS cache lock when the DNS server port is unreachable, which also made `SYSTEM DROP DNS CACHE` and server shutdown hang. [#128932](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128932) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed a server abort, wrong rows, or a rejected valid row when a `MsgPack` input that ends in the middle of an object is followed by another input parsed by the same format, as with the entries of one asynchronous insert batch or the messages of a streaming engine such as `Kafka` with `kafka_handle_error_mode = 'stream'`. [#128943](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128943) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Parts whose packed skip index archive `skp_idx.packed` was written by a newer version (for example after a downgrade from 26.8 to 26.6) are now readable and mergeable. Their skip indices are not used until the part is merged or the index is materialized again, instead of every query and merge failing with `UNKNOWN_FORMAT_VERSION`. [#128951](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128951) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Hide secrets in `system.s3_queue_settings` and `system.azure_queue_settings`. [#128955](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128955) ([Kseniia Sumarokova](https://github.com/kssenii)).
* Fixed `s3` and `azureBlobStorage` reads returning cached data of another endpoint or bucket with the same path and ETag. [#128964](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128964) ([Samuel Krempaský](https://github.com/NIKTONIKTO717)).
* Fixed `UNKNOWN_FUNCTION` in the refresh of a refreshable materialized view that reads a parameterized view as `db.view(...)` into a `ReplicatedMergeTree` table (always in a `Replicated` database, after a restart in an `Atomic` one), and in `INSERT ... SELECT` from `` `db.view`(...) `` into a `ReplicatedMergeTree` or `Distributed` table. A formatted query now keeps `db.view(...)` in two parts. Closes [#128872](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128872). [#128992](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128992) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `ATTACH TABLE ... AS REPLICATED` and `ATTACH TABLE ... AS NOT REPLICATED` now check the `TABLE ENGINE` grant on the engine the table is converted to before changing anything. Previously a query refused for this grant had already rewritten the stored definition of the detached table, so a later `ATTACH TABLE` loaded it with the new engine. [#128995](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128995) ([Nikolai Ovchinnikov](https://github.com/kolsha)).
* Fixed dynamic disks (`disk(...)` in table settings) that use `from_env`, `from_zk` or `include`: after an upgrade, or depending on `s3_allow_server_credentials_in_user_queries`, they got a different identity, so they could not be shared or re-attached, or the table was attached to a new, empty disk. A session without `s3_allow_server_credentials_in_user_queries` can no longer use a disk with server credentials through a wrapper disk. [#129044](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129044) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fixes off-by-one results from `dateDiff` before 1970 when the unit is finer than a second but coarser than the value's precision (e.g.`dateDiff('millisecond', ...)` on `DateTime64(6)`). Also fixes `age` being off by one when its two `DateTime64` arguments have different precision. [#129056](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129056) ([Ryan Cunningham](https://github.com/ryan-c-2024)).
* HDFS disks created in a query now respect `remote_url_allow_hosts`. HDFS, S3 and Azure disks created in a query are now also checked when a table or database reuses an existing disk, wraps it, or selects it through `storage_policy` or by name. Previously, an S3 or Azure disk that was already loaded could be reused after its host was removed from the list. A definition whose host is not allowed is rejected with `UNACCEPTABLE_URL`. Existing tables and databases on such disks still load, with a warning in the log and in `system.warnings`. [#129061](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129061) ([Arsen Muk](https://github.com/arsenmuk)).
* Fixed `EXPLAIN SYNTAX` and `EXPLAIN AST optimize = 1` showing the definition of a parameterized view to a user without the `SHOW COLUMNS` privilege on the view, which `SHOW CREATE` requires. For such a user the view call is now printed as written. [#129074](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129074) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a partitioned write into a Delta Lake or Iceberg table accepting a `String` partition value that is not valid UTF-8, for example `unhex('FF')`, after which the table could not be read (Delta Lake: `Encountered non-UTF-8 data`; Iceberg: by other readers such as pyiceberg). Such an `INSERT`, an Iceberg `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE` of the partition column, or an Iceberg `OPTIMIZE TABLE` that would copy such a value into a new manifest is now rejected with `INCORRECT_DATA`. A failed Iceberg mutation no longer leaves the files it already wrote. [#129091](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129091) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix reading structs inside arrays and maps of Delta Lake tables with column mapping: such columns were read as empty arrays or default values, and their subcolumns failed with `INCORRECT_DATA`. [#129103](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129103) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `EXPLAIN QUERY TREE` and `EXPLAIN SYNTAX run_query_tree_passes = 1` showing the columns of a parameterized view to a user without grants on it. Such a user now gets `ACCESS_DENIED` for any call of the view, instead of errors that name its columns, parameters or the tables it reads, or quote its definition. A user whose only grant on a parameterized view is on a subcolumn (``SELECT(`t.a`)``) can no longer read it, as for tables since [#117979](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/117979). With the old analyzer (`enable_analyzer = 0`, before 26.9), an unqualified call of a parameterized view named like a table function, such as `numbers(n = 3)`, now calls the table function, as the analyzer does; use `db.numbers(n = 3)` for the view. [#129106](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129106) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the memory worker of the server and Keeper failing on every tick (an error 20 times a second, and no resident memory updates, memory tracker corrections or memory limit adjustments) and `CGroupMemoryTotal`/`CGroupMemoryUsed` disappearing from `system.asynchronous_metrics`, until restart, after the cgroup v2 `memory` controller of the server's cgroup is disabled at runtime, for example with systemd `MemoryAccounting=no`. Both now follow the controller being disabled and enabled again. [#129131](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129131) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An exhausted `MAX FAILED SEQUENTIAL AUTHENTICATIONS` quota is now reported as a regular authentication failure, so it no longer reveals whether the named user exists. [#129137](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129137) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* The `MySQL` database engine now respects `remote_url_allow_hosts`. MySQL hosts that the client library would read as a connection string, a plugin URL or a list of hosts are rejected, and `localhost` without an explicit `socket` now connects over TCP to the given port instead of the default Unix socket. [#129139](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129139) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` for a query that reads no column, such as `SELECT count()`, from a `Buffer` table whose destination is a `Distributed` table, when the `Buffer` table has an `ALIAS` or `EPHEMERAL` column or only columns larger than its `_table` virtual column. [#129143](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129143) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` for a `LIMIT BY` key that is a `GROUP BY` key spelled with a different alias, for example a positional `LIMIT 1 BY 1` over `GROUP BY toString(c0)` `WITH ROLLUP`, `WITH CUBE` or `WITH TOTALS`. Closes [#121423](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121423). [#129146](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129146) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a valid `JOIN ON` query rejected with `CANNOT_CONVERT_TYPE` by `optimize_extract_common_expressions` when the extraction removes the only `Nothing`-typed operand, for example a comparison with an `ARRAY JOIN []` alias. Closes [#124771](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124771). [#129148](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129148) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `LEFT JOIN` over a `Merge` table with a `WHERE _table = ...` condition and `ORDER BY ... LIMIT` failing with an error (`ILLEGAL_TYPE_OF_ARGUMENT`, `UNKNOWN_TABLE`, `ACCESS_DENIED`) that comes from a table the condition excludes. Closes [#124406](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124406). [#129163](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129163) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `additional_result_filter` being applied to a non-correlated `EXISTS` subquery, which made such queries fail with `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER`, or with `TOO_DEEP_RECURSION` when the filter itself contained `EXISTS`. [#129164](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129164) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SELECT ... FROM <GraphiteMergeTree> FINAL` returning a partially rolled-up row when a condition on the time column is wrapped in `indexHint`, which `optimize_and_compare_chain` (enabled by default) does when it derives a time bound through another column, e.g. `WHERE toUInt32(Time) + 3600 < Version AND Version < 960004000`. Closes [#128198](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128198). [#129165](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129165) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed JIT-compiled `multiIf` and `CASE` returning a wrapped, wrong value instead of throwing `DECIMAL_OVERFLOW` when a `Decimal` branch is lifted to a larger scale of a `Decimal128` result. Closes [#126516](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126516). [#129166](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129166) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` for a query that groups by an alias of an `OR` chain of `LIKE` conditions whose haystack is another alias containing such a chain, when that inner alias is also used elsewhere, for example `WITH if(s LIKE '%a%' OR s LIKE '%b%' OR s LIKE '%c%' OR s LIKE '%1%', s, 'z') AS h SELECT (h LIKE '%a%' OR h LIKE '%b%' OR h LIKE '%c%' OR h LIKE '%1%') AS r, count() FROM t WHERE h != '' GROUP BY r`. Closes [#126788](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126788). [#129177](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129177) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed query planning that never finished for a filter over a subexpression that is reused many times (for example `x + x` in 30 nested subqueries) below a `JOIN` or a `GROUP BY` with keys, while hash table statistics are collected (the default). Such a query also ignored `KILL QUERY` and `max_execution_time`. [#129184](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129184) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a secret written in plaintext to `system.query_log` and the server log by `CREATE DATABASE ... ENGINE = Backup(...)` when the `S3` destination has two positional arguments and a `headers(...)` argument, for example `S3(url, 'secret', headers('a' = 'b'))`. The value after the url is now hidden, as `BACKUP ... TO` already does. [#129189](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129189) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a server crash (`std::terminate`) when an Arrow Flight call (`DoPut`, `DoGet`, `GetFlightInfo`, `GetSchema`) failed while finishing its query, for example with `MEMORY_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`. The call now returns an error to the client. [#129193](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129193) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a query returning too few rows when its filter has a negated comparison of `coalesce` or `ifNull` inside an `OR`, for example `NOT (coalesce(c, b) > 10) OR x = 9`, or a comparison of a `coalesce` wrapped in `materialize` or a `CAST` to its own type, and the columns have `minmax` or `set` skip indexes. Closes [#128360](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128360). [#129203](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129203) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `DESCRIBE TABLE` of a table function, and `viewIfPermitted` with an `ELSE` table function, now check the same access rights as reading from that table function: the `CREATE TEMPORARY TABLE` grant is required for users with `readonly = 0` otherwise access is refused and users under `readonly = 1` are refused. Table function is allowed in readonly mode like `numbers` and this behaviour is not changed. [#129205](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129205) ([Foad Lotfifar](https://github.com/foadl)).
* Fixed lost, rejected or misparsed rows when several `INSERT ... FORMAT SQLite`, `One`, `Npy`, `MySQLDump`, `JSON`, `RowBinaryWithNamesAndTypes`, `CustomSeparated`, `Template`, `TSV` or `Values` queries are combined into one asynchronous insert batch (asynchronous inserts are enabled by default), and in `FileLog` tables with these formats: each input is now parsed as if it were the only one. [#129209](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129209) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `LOGICAL_ERROR` `No available columns` when a query reads only virtual columns (such as `_file`) from a Delta Lake table in which every column is a partition column, which Spark produces by dropping the last non-partition column of a table with column mapping. [#129220](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129220) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a crash when an S3 client was configured with an invalid SSE-C key (`server_side_encryption_customer_key_base64`), for example in `CREATE TABLE ... SETTINGS disk = disk(type = 's3', ...)`. Such keys are now rejected with `BAD_ARGUMENTS`; the key must be the base64 encoding of a 256-bit key. [#129240](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129240) ([Antonio Filipovic](https://github.com/antoniofilipovic)).
* Fixes `max_estimated_execution_time` with fractional or very large values. Fractional values were truncated to whole seconds, so `0.5` acted as `0` and rejected every query whose estimated time was checked. Values above about 68 years overflowed and could become a negative limit, with the same effect. [#129263](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129263) ([Christoph Viebig](https://github.com/cv4g)).
* `ReplicatedMergeTree` merges now use the settings of `background_profile`, like `MergeTree` merges and all mutations. [#129270](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129270) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Fixed a memory-safety defect in Keeper where a malformed Raft log pack from a peer could corrupt the heap; such packs are now rejected. [#129291](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129291) ([Samuel Krempaský](https://github.com/NIKTONIKTO717)).
* Fixed `invalidate_query` for a dictionary with a `ClickHouse` source on a remote server. Every periodic check failed with a logical error `Invalid number of columns in chunk pushed to OutputPort`, so the dictionary was fully reloaded on every check instead of only when the result of `invalidate_query` changed. [#129336](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129336) ([Vladimir Cherkasov](https://github.com/vdimir)).
* Fixed a crash on `INSERT` into the source table of a materialized view whose query reads that table again through `merge` (with a `Distributed` table among its tables), `loop` or `executable`, including the `APPEND` refresh of a refreshable materialized view into that table. Closes [#125023](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125023). [#129338](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129338) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `CAST` to `JSON` and `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY COLUMN` of a `JSON` column replacing `inf`, `-inf` and `nan` of floating-point paths with `0` (or `NULL`). Such a value is now converted like the JSON string `"inf"`: a `String` path keeps `'inf'`, a dynamic path stores it as a `String` (a `Float64` with `input_format_json_try_infer_numbers_from_strings = 1`), and a typed path that cannot hold it, such as an integer, `Decimal`, `Date` or `Array` path, throws `INCORRECT_DATA` instead of storing the default. [#129343](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129343) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `clickhouse-local` now applies `custom_settings_prefixes` from its config file, so settings profiles in the users config can set custom settings, as on the server. [#129346](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129346) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Fixed a rare spurious `Coordination error: No node` exception when waiting for a replicated queue entry to be processed, e.g. in `OPTIMIZE` or `ALTER` with synchronous replication. [#129347](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129347) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed the `compatibility` setting restoring the old default of a setting whose new default was backported to the pinned release. With `compatibility` set to that release, `iceberg_tolerate_conflicting_manifest_schemas`, `backup_s3_expired_token_retry_timeout_ms`, `azure_validate_etag_on_read`, `use_text_index_postings_cache`, `filesystem_cache_wait_for_concurrent_download_timeout_milliseconds`, the `ai_function_*` limits, the `http_*` header limits and the MergeTree setting `enable_row_mask_update_coalescing` now get that release's default. Closes [#129014](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/129014). [#129348](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129348) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` when lazy materialization over joins (`query_plan_lazy_materialization_for_join`) meets a `WITH TOTALS` subquery whose result is read from the query cache. [#129350](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129350) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* There was an issue with the `NATS` engine in combination with `nats_handle_error_mode = 'stream'` where messages with multiple rows were incorrectly discarded as invalid entries, which meant that the data did not reach the materialized view. [#129363](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129363) ([marouane el fadli](https://github.com/Marouane-elfadli)).
* Fix duplicated rows after `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE` on a partitioned Iceberg table when the update moves the rows of one data file into several partitions: every row was written into every new partition. Closes [#101665](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/101665). [#129374](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129374) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Backported in [#133536](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133536): Fixed a table with the `keeper_path` of another `S3Queue` or `AzureQueue` making it delete, move or tag its source files. The source must now match, and these actions need the `WRITE` grant. [#129383](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129383) ([Samuel Krempaský](https://github.com/NIKTONIKTO717)).
* Add sanity checks and memory-tracked containers to query plan deserialization. [#129395](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129395) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Fixed `std::out_of_range` (`Code: 1001`) when opening a `DeltaLake` table or a `deltaLake` table function, or when following an S3 `301` redirect, on an S3 URL whose endpoint is at most 10 characters long, such as `http://s3/bucket/table/`. [#129408](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129408) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `max_result_rows` and `max_result_bytes` are now applied to queries without `FROM` and to queries that read from `system.one` or the quota system tables. [#129418](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129418) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Reading an Iceberg table no longer fails with `ICEBERG_SPECIFICATION_VIOLATION` when a Parquet data file contains a column whose field id is not in the table schema. Such columns are skipped, as the Iceberg spec requires for column projection. This is needed to read BigQuery managed Iceberg tables, whose data files carry a hidden `_CHANGE_TIMESTAMP` column. [#129421](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129421) ([Asya Shneerson](https://github.com/asya-ch)).
* Fix DeltaLake file pruning for comparisons over a non-nullable `Date`/`Date32` cast of a `Nullable(Date32)` column, e.g. `d::Date = '2026-01-01'` with the default `cast_keep_nullable = 0`. Previously such filters scanned every data file. [#129451](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129451) ([Arsen Muk](https://github.com/arsenmuk)).
* Fixed `RESTORE ... ON CLUSTER` not checking the `TABLE ENGINE` grant for the engines of the restored tables and of the inner tables of materialized views, so a user without the grant could restore, for example, a `File` table and read the server file through it. Such a restore now needs the same grant as `RESTORE` without `ON CLUSTER`, e.g. `GRANT TABLE ENGINE ON File` or `GRANT READ, WRITE ON FILE`. [#129468](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129468) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `CREATE HYPOTHETICAL INDEX` now runs the same checks as `ALTER TABLE ... ADD INDEX`. Before, it accepted a `minmax` index on a `JSON` column. [#129484](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129484) ([Yaroslav Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Fix the `mongodb` table function, the `MongoDB` table engine and MongoDB dictionaries returning the document at each block boundary twice when a collection has more documents than `max_block_size`. [#129516](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129516) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed `count(DISTINCT)`, `uniq*`, `SELECT DISTINCT`, `arrayDistinct` and hash `JOIN` treating some different `JSON` and `Dynamic` values as equal, for example `{"x":[{"a":1},{"b":2,"c":3}]}` and `{"x":[{"a":1,"b":2},{"c":3}]}`. `uniq*` states of such values stored by older versions do not deduplicate against new ones. Closes [#129458](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/129458). [#129517](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129517) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix JIT memory growing without bound when `compile_expressions` compiles many queries with different literals. The memory was not limited by `compiled_expression_cache_size` and was only released by `SYSTEM DROP COMPILED EXPRESSION CACHE`. [#129529](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129529) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fix duplicated rows when reading the change data feed of a Delta Lake table with the `deltaLake` table function and parallel replicas. [#129548](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129548) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* A `Dynamic` or `Variant` argument of a parameterized view is passed as the value it holds, like the same value passed directly. Previously the view got the number the value is stored as, e.g. `1704067200` for a `DateTime` or `2` for an `Enum` value, and `DESCRIBE` / `EXPLAIN SYNTAX` could disagree with `SELECT`. [#129554](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129554) ([Yakov Olkhovskiy](https://github.com/yakov-olkhovskiy)).
* Fixed `OPTIMIZE TABLE` on an Iceberg table (`allow_experimental_iceberg_compaction`) committing an empty partition spec and a renumbered schema: a later `ALTER TABLE ... DELETE` left the rows it matched visible, a second `OPTIMIZE TABLE` failed with `LOGICAL_ERROR`, and a table whose field ids are not in column order (for example a column added after a `Tuple` column) read other columns' values after `OPTIMIZE`. A data file whose partition tuple has a different number of values than the default partition spec is now refused with a `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` error before anything is written. Closes [#129498](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/129498). [#129564](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129564) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `tupleElement` with a default argument over an `ARRAY JOIN`-ed `Nested` column, when the default references another subcolumn of an `ARRAY JOIN`-ed column. [#129581](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129581) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fix `ARRAY JOIN requires array or map argument` and `AMBIGUOUS_COLUMN_NAME` errors for `ARRAY JOIN` over a view that also uses `ARRAY JOIN`. [#129582](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129582) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* `toDecimalString` with precision 0 no longer writes a trailing decimal point for a `Decimal` with a non-zero scale. [#129587](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129587) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fix `key = value` arguments of table functions such as `s3` and `gcs` when the query has a column with the same name as the key. [#129592](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129592) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fix a stall, and expiration of the session, of a secure ZooKeeper/Keeper client connection (`<secure>1</secure>`) when a large request and a large response overlap. [#129604](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129604) ([eoger-ant](https://github.com/eoger-ant)).
* Fixed filtered `WRITE` grants (such as `GRANT WRITE ON S3('<regex>')`) being bypassed by `INSERT INTO FUNCTION` into `url`, `s3`, `azureBlobStorage` and `hdfs`. Previously only the URI as typed was checked, so a `PARTITION BY` value like `../other/x`, or a file renamed by `s3_create_new_file_on_insert`, could be written outside the granted path. Now every destination is checked. If a renamed file falls outside a filtered grant, widen the grant or use `s3_truncate_on_insert`. `CREATE TABLE ... AS s3(...)` and other source table functions now need `TABLE ENGINE ON <engine>` grant, like `ENGINE = <engine>`; with only a filtered source grant, grant the engine explicitly. This also applies to `CREATE TABLE copy AS src` when `src` is such a table, and to local `RESTORE` of such a table. [#129605](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129605) ([Foad Lotfifar](https://github.com/foadl)).
* Fixed a hang when a write to a `readonly` local `plain_rewritable` disk is refused: its rollback, refused too, was retried without end. For example, an `encrypted` disk with a `path` over such a disk never finished `CREATE TABLE`, and with these disks in the configuration the server never finished starting. Now the write fails with `READONLY`. [#129613](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129613) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed missing tables in `SHOW TABLES` and `system.tables` of a `DataLakeCatalog` database over an Iceberg REST catalog when a namespace ends with an empty level, such as `["a", ""]`: its sub-namespaces were requested as those of `["a"]`. Creating a table in such a namespace also requested `["a"]` instead of it. [#129622](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129622) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* With `s3_create_new_file_on_insert`, `azure_create_new_file_on_insert` or `hdfs_create_new_file_on_insert`, the sequence number of a new file is now inserted before the first dot of the file name, not before the first dot of the whole path. Previously, if a directory name contained a dot (e.g. `team.v1/data.tsv`), the new file was written into a different directory (`team.1.v1/data.tsv`). [#129630](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129630) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a query that never finished and used memory until the server was killed when it read an `ALIAS` column whose expression reads the column itself through a subcolumn, directly or through other `ALIAS` columns (for example `c0 Nullable(IPv6) ALIAS c0.null`). Such a read now fails with `CYCLIC_ALIASES`. [#129640](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129640) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* During TLS certificate rotation, mismatched certificate and private key files are now rejected, keeping the previous working pair active until both replacement files match. [#129659](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129659) ([Craig Ringer](https://github.com/ringerc)).
* Fix `STRING_AGG(DISTINCT x, sep)`, `STRING_AGG(x, sep) FILTER (WHERE ...)` and `groupConcatIf`/`OrNull`/`OrDefault`/`State` with a separator argument failing with `NUMBER_OF_ARGUMENTS_DOESNT_MATCH`. [#129661](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129661) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed `RESTORE ... ON CLUSTER` never finishing when `create_table`, `create_database`, `create_access`, `create_function`, `create_named_collection` or `create_workloads_and_resources` is set to `'create'` or `'must-exist'`: the hosts rejected the forwarded setting with `Cannot parse creation mode`. The Bool literal `true` is now accepted for all six settings, and `false` for `create_table` and `create_database`, like `1` and `0`. [#129693](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129693) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `LOGICAL_ERROR` `Unexpected return type from materialize` on `INSERT` into a `Buffer` table, or on its flush, when the destination is an Iceberg table whose stored column types differ from the declared ones (for example a `Date` column, which Iceberg reads back as `Date32`). [#129694](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129694) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix the declared size of the `OK` packet of the MySQL protocol when `last_insert_id` needs a multi-byte length-encoded representation, and accept `OK` packets without the optional `info` field when ClickHouse acts as a MySQL client. [#129709](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129709) ([wangshun](https://github.com/xixi-box)).
* Reading `JSON` from `Native` or `RowBinary` input no longer allocates memory that `max_memory_usage` does not refuse for a path name, or for a value read as a string (`Native` string serialization, `input_format_binary_read_json_as_string`), whose declared length the data does not back: a 5-byte `RowBinary` input made ClickHouse allocate and zero 1 GiB. Such a truncated path or value now fails with `ATTEMPT_TO_READ_AFTER_EOF` instead of `CANNOT_READ_ALL_DATA`. [#129721](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129721) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `CAST` into `SimpleAggregateFunction(f, Bool)` for `f` returning `Bool` (`any`, `anyLast`, `min`, `max`) keeping the source value: `CAST(300 AS SimpleAggregateFunction(any, Bool))` was printed as `true` but did not compare equal to `true`, and `0.5` became `false`. Such a column filled from a `DEFAULT`, an `INSERT ... SELECT` of another numeric type or `VALUES` now gets 0 and 1, as `Bool` does, and so does `JSONExtract` into it or into `LowCardinality(Bool)`. Also fixed wrong rows from `optimize_read_in_order`, `optimize_distinct_in_order` and a minmax skip index over a `CAST` into `Bool`. [#129731](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129731) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix the alignment of Pretty formats for emoji, which take two columns in terminals, and for characters added in recent Unicode versions. [#129748](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129748) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* `INSERT INTO FUNCTION viewIfPermitted(... ELSE f(...))` now requires the write grant of `f` (for example `WRITE ON URL`), as an insert into `f` directly does. Before, only the read grant was checked, so a user with `READ ON URL` and no `WRITE ON URL` could make the server send an insert to a URL. Also fixed `Logical error: 'Unexpected table function name: viewIfPermitted'` when reading through the `ELSE` function with parallel replicas enabled; reading through an explicit cluster function after `ELSE` (`urlCluster`, `s3Cluster`, ...) now fails with `NOT_IMPLEMENTED`. [#129756](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129756) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A `Buffer` table no longer counts its destination's rows in `system.tables.total_rows` for a user who has no privilege on the destination, so that table's row count is no longer disclosed. Such a user sees only the rows held in the buffer, and `DROP TABLE IF EMPTY` judges the buffer by those rows. [#129801](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129801) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A `Merge` table and the `merge` table function no longer count the matched tables the user cannot see (no `SHOW TABLES`) when `count()` is answered from metadata or in `system.tables` `total_rows`/`total_bytes`, and their `system.columns` sizes include only the columns the user can see (`SHOW COLUMNS`) on each matched table. Before, these disclosed the row count and size of tables the user has no access to, while reading the same data skipped them. [#129818](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129818) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `DESCRIBE TABLE`, `CREATE TABLE ... AS` and `viewIfPermitted` with an `azureBlobStorage` table function whose structure is inferred no longer create a missing container, which requires `WRITE ON AZURE`. [#129848](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129848) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* When `http_forbid_headers` forbids `Authorization`, Basic credentials that would be sent in that header are now refused too: the userinfo of `url`, `urlCluster` and `URL` table URLs (including url wildcards and `web` disks), `credentials` of `HTTP` dictionaries created by DDL, the userinfo of `format_avro_schema_registry_url`, and `user`/`password` of the `Elasticsearch` engine. Before, only an explicit `Authorization` header was refused. [#129850](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129850) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `INSERT` failing with `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER` into a table whose `PROJECTION` has `GLOBAL IN` in `WHERE`, when the projection was created (or the table was loaded) with parallel replicas enabled. [#129863](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129863) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed wrong results of an aggregating query whose `AND` over aggregate functions was simplified to a constant: for example `SELECT max(a) >= 0 AND max(b) >= 0 FROM t` with `UInt32` columns lost its aggregate functions and returned one row per table row instead of one row. Closes [#129858](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/129858). [#129880](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129880) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `TRUNCATE` of an `S3` table whose URL names no object (for example `S3('http://host/bucket/', ...)` or a named collection with `filename = ''`) no longer deletes the bucket: S3 requests with an empty object key are now rejected with `S3_ERROR` instead of being sent to the bucket itself. [#129881](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129881) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a graceful server shutdown that waited `max_delay_to_insert` (60 seconds by default) for every flush of a `Buffer` table or of the asynchronous insert queue into a `Distributed` table whose pending bytes were above `bytes_to_delay_insert` (for example, with its sends stopped or its shards unreachable), and then dropped those rows. Once the shutdown has started, such an `INSERT` is no longer delayed or rejected by `bytes_to_delay_insert`. [#129890](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129890) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `INSERT ... SELECT` into an Iceberg table failing with `CANNOT_SCHEDULE_TASK` on hosts with many cores, because every parallel insert stream kept its own pool of `max_threads` Parquet encoder threads. All Parquet writers now share one encoder thread pool of the size of the number of cores. Closes [#129864](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/129864). [#129893](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129893) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `JOIN` of a `Bool` key with an integer key matching `true` with integers other than 1 (such as 2) depending on the order of the tables. The keys are now compared by value, as `=` does. [#129894](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129894) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a filtered source grant (e.g. `GRANT WRITE ON S3('https://bucket/allowed/.*')`) authorizing a location outside the filter. `BACKUP`/`RESTORE` and table functions like `s3`, `url` and `azureBlobStorage` with `.`/`..` or empty path segments, a fragment, escapes that change the name, a query in an S3 URI (where `?` is a wildcard) or `url` alternatives outside the path now need the grant on the whole source. A glob character (`*?{}|`, and `,` in `url`) must match the filter both as written and percent-encoded. [#129899](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129899) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `NOT_ENOUGH_SPACE` errors on a volume of several disks with `load_balancing = round_robin` (the default) when one disk was full and several threads reserved space at the same time: a reservation could give up before trying a disk with free space. This affected spills of external sorting, aggregation and joins to a `tmp_policy` of several disks, and inserts into `MergeTree` tables, whose parts could also go to the next volume of the storage policy instead. [#129914](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129914) ([Nihal Z. Miaji](https://github.com/nihalzp)).
* Fixed the `USING` key of a `FULL JOIN` over an `Enum` key (also inside a `Tuple` or a typed `JSON` path) for a row that only the right table has: it was the first value of the `Enum` instead of that row's key, so `WHERE`, `GROUP BY` and a following `JOIN ... USING` saw a wrong value. The right key of a not-matched row of a `LEFT` or `FULL JOIN` is now the first value of the `Enum`, as in the other columns and join algorithms, instead of `0`, which threw `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM`. `firstNonDefault` now treats the first value of an `Enum` as its default. [#129920](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129920) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed missing rows when reading an Iceberg table partitioned by several columns with a filter such as `a = 2 AND b IS NULL`: manifest-list partition pruning (`use_iceberg_manifest_list_partition_pruning`, enabled by default since 26.9) skipped manifests that hold a `NULL` partition value, as PyIceberg and Spark write them. Closes [#129913](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/129913). [#129929](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129929) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed Iceberg partition pruning skipping rows of an `int` column (or a column promoted from `int` to `long`) partitioned by `truncate[W]` when their truncated value is below `-2147483648`, in tables written by Spark, PyIceberg and other engines that store the 32-bit wraparound of that value. Closes [#129910](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/129910). [#129933](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129933) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix volumes with `load_balancing = least_used` sending every write to one disk between the reads of the free space of their disks, which happen every `least_used_ttl_ms` (60 seconds by default). When that disk was full, inserts into `MergeTree` tables and spills of temporary data failed with `NOT_ENOUGH_SPACE` until the next read, although other disks of the volume had room. [#129942](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129942) ([Nihal Z. Miaji](https://github.com/nihalzp)).
* Fixed exponential parsing time of nested expressions in which `CASE` or `INTERVAL` is used as an identifier or a function name, such as `(interval + (interval + ...))` over a column named `interval`. Such valid queries failed with `TOO_SLOW_PARSING` at about 18 levels of nesting. [#129946](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129946) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a heap-use-after-free in `DROP DATABASE` and `DETACH DATABASE` when the dependency graphs of a table held a stale UUID, for example after `ALTER` of a view that a dictionary reads, then `DROP TABLE` and `CREATE TABLE` with the view's name. [#129947](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129947) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `EXECUTE AS` ignoring the query construction settings (`limit`, `offset`, `page`, `select`, `filter`, `order`, `sort`) in the `SETTINGS` clause of its statement, so `EXECUTE AS u SELECT ... SETTINGS limit = 1` returned every row. [#129949](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129949) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results when an `if`, `multiIf` or `CASE` with a constant condition had the query's aggregate functions or an `arrayJoin` only in the branch not taken: for example `SELECT if(1, toUInt32(5), max(a)) FROM t` returned one row per table row instead of one row, and `SELECT if(1, toUInt8(5), arrayJoin([1, 2, 3])) FROM t` did not multiply the rows. [#129954](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129954) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Secrets in the inner query of `COPY (query) TO STDOUT`, such as the password of the `remote` table function, are now hidden in `system.query_log`, the server log and the output of `clickhouse-format`, as in any other query. [#129965](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129965) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed correlated scalar and `EXISTS` subqueries returning `NULL` or `0` for an outer row whose correlated column is `NULL`, although the subquery produces rows for that row (for example with `WHERE outer.c IS NULL` or `isNotDistinctFrom(inner.a, outer.c)`). Closes [#112576](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/112576). [#129971](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129971) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix reading Hudi tables that contain a partition metafile (`.hoodie_partition_metadata.parquet`, written when `hoodie.partition.metafile.use.base.format=true`). Before, every read of such a table failed with `CANNOT_PARSE_INPUT_ASSERTION_FAILED`. [#129978](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129978) ([marouane el fadli](https://github.com/Marouane-elfadli)).
* A failed `INSERT` into a `Log` or `StripeLog` table could leave the table in a state where the next `INSERT` failed with a logical error `Wrong number of marks generated from block`, if the rollback of the failed insert hit the memory limit. [#130037](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130037) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a crash (division by zero) when merging malformed `groupArraySample` states whose value counter overflows. Such states are now rejected with an `INCORRECT_DATA` error. [#130045](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130045) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed reading Parquet files in which the key of a `MAP` is a `LIST` or a `MAP`, for example a Spark-written Iceberg table with a `map<array<float>, string>` column. Such a key was read as a `Tuple` of its Parquet children, so schema inference returned `Map(Tuple(list Array(Tuple(element Nullable(Float32)))), ...)` and an explicit `Map(Array(Float32), String)` type, or any Iceberg table with such a column, failed with `TYPE_MISMATCH`. The key is now read as `Array` or `Map`. Closes [#130000](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130000). [#130080](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130080) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `Decimal` and `DateTime64` values inserted into `Dynamic` or `Variant` columns being stored with the wrong scale (for example `1.234` read back as `12.34`) when the column already had a `Decimal` type with another scale. [#130094](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130094) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed filters on the `Iceberg` v3 virtual columns `_row_id` and `_last_updated_sequence_number` missing rows of data files that store some of these values instead of inheriting them from `first_row_id` and the data sequence number, for example a file rewritten by a copy-on-write `UPDATE`. Closes [#130022](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130022). [#130100](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130100) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading Iceberg tables with columns added with an `initial-default`: rows of data files written before such a column was added now return the default instead of `NULL`, also in filters and for fields added inside a struct, and a required column added with a default no longer makes the table unreadable. [#130101](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130101) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading a Parquet `Map` column as a `Tuple` that does not describe the map, for example an Iceberg table whose schema has a struct where a data file stores a map: the elements of the `Tuple` were silently returned as `NULL` or default values, now the query throws `TYPE_MISMATCH`. Closes [#130020](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130020). [#130102](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130102) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading Iceberg tables with equality delete files: a row whose equality key is `NULL` was dropped although no delete row matched it. A `NULL` in an equality delete file now matches only `NULL`, as the Iceberg specification requires. [#130108](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130108) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `CREATE HANDLER` and `ALTER HANDLER` storing a query whose top level is a `UNION DISTINCT` without its trailing `SETTINGS` clause and its `INTO OUTFILE` options, so requests to such a handler ran without those settings. Over HTTP, `... UNION DISTINCT ... INTO OUTFILE` is now rejected with `INTO_OUTFILE_NOT_ALLOWED`, the same as `UNION ALL`. [#130109](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130109) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading Iceberg tables in which a decimal inside an array, a map or a struct was widened to a precision with a wider storage type (for example `decimal(12, 4)` to `decimal(20, 4)`): every query to such a table failed with `Bad get: has Decimal64, requested Decimal128`. [#130114](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130114) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Aggregate function combinators no longer run for minutes ignoring `max_execution_time` when nested states that occupy no memory multiply: creating one aggregate state, or growing a `-ForEach` state, now throws `ARGUMENT_OUT_OF_BOUND` if it would create more than 1048576 such states, e.g. `-ForEach` over a zero-sized `-Resample`, or a forged `sumForEach` state. [#130120](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130120) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a hang of a query in a CPU workload that is the first to use a dictionary with a `ClickHouse` source (`dictGet`, `dictHas` with a non-constant key) and runs no more threads than the workload's CPU slots (for example `max_concurrent_threads = 1` with `max_threads = 1`). The waiting query kept its CPU slot, which the dictionary load in the same workload needed; now it releases the slot while it waits. [#130133](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130133) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results when `optimize_inverse_dictionary_lookup` replaced a comparison `dictGet(dict, attr, key) = c`, whose constant matches no dictionary value, by `0` while `key` held the query's aggregate functions or an `arrayJoin`: for example `SELECT dictGet('d', 'name', count()) = 'zzz' FROM numbers(5)` returned five rows instead of one. [#130138](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130138) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* This patch changes sliding indexes logic from unconditional calculation to requirements-driven. Now, peer groups for each row will not be calculated if they are not needed by any function or frame-advancement logic (group frames). [#130139](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130139) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* Fixed `_row_id` and `_last_updated_sequence_number` of Iceberg v3 tables with ORC and Avro data files: the values stored in the data file are used, and ORC rows without them inherit `_row_id` from `first_row_id` (it was `NULL`). A filter on these columns no longer skips rows by the values stored in the data file. The `_row_number` virtual column now returns row numbers for ORC files. Closes [#130032](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130032). [#130140](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130140) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `INSERT`, `MATERIALIZE PROJECTION` and merges failing with `LOGICAL_ERROR` `Projection ... calculated the columns ... while its metadata describes the columns ...` for a table with a projection that has a subquery (`x IN (SELECT ...)`, `EXISTS (SELECT ...)`) in its `SELECT` list or `GROUP BY` and a `WHERE` clause or aggregation. [#130143](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130143) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results of a `WHERE` or `JOIN ON` condition where `optimize_extract_common_expressions` removed a redundant `OR` operand that contained `arrayJoin`: for example `WHERE (a > 0 AND arrayJoin(arr) > 0) OR a > 0` lost the rows produced by `arrayJoin`. [#130152](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130152) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed failing merges, an invalid primary index and parts detached as broken after a restart when `ALTER TABLE` ran with `transform_null_in = 1` on a MergeTree table whose sorting or partition key applies `IN` to a column. `ALTER` now derives the table's keys in the server's global context, as `CREATE` and a table reload do. [#130154](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130154) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading an ORC `list`, `map` or `struct` column requested as a type of another kind, for example a `Tuple` over a map, or a data lake table whose schema disagrees with an ORC data file: the column was silently returned as default values, and a `Map` whose value had another kind crashed the server. Now the value is cast to the requested type, as the ORC format documentation describes, or the query fails with the cast error. [#130171](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130171) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed Iceberg partition pruning skipping the data files of a `NULL` partition when the filter's set holds `NULL`, such as `has([NULL, 1], b)`. [#130177](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130177) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed Delta Lake partition pruning skipping the data files of a `NULL` partition when the filter's set holds `NULL`, such as `has([NULL, 1], p)` or `p IN (NULL, 1)` with `transform_null_in = 1`. [#130192](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130192) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SELECT` returning no rows and `ALTER TABLE ... DELETE` on `ReplicatedMergeTree` skipping rows when the filter reads a column named like an expression of the partition key, for example a column `` `toYYYYMM(d)` `` in a table with `PARTITION BY toYYYYMM(d)`. [#130196](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130196) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed row estimates from column statistics that could be 0 for a non-empty filtered table (an equality matching about one row, or a filter with a constant condition such as `x IS NOT NULL` on a non-`Nullable` column). The join order optimizer took such a table for empty and could choose very slow join orders, for example in the Join Order Benchmark. [#130202](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130202) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a server crash and wrong results of a `JOIN` onto a `COMPLEX_KEY_DIRECT` or `COMPLEX_KEY_CACHE` dictionary when the left join key is a constant (for example `SELECT 'a' AS k`), comes out of an `ARRAY JOIN`, or is stored with sparse serialization. [#130203](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130203) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results when a `WHERE`, `HAVING` or `QUALIFY` condition that contains `arrayJoin` is always true, for example `arrayJoin(arr) OR 1`, `arrayJoin(arr) IS NOT NULL` or `arrayJoin(arr) NOT IN ()`: the rows of the `arrayJoin` were not produced, so `SELECT count() FROM numbers(10) WHERE arrayJoin([0, 1]) IS NOT NULL` returned 10 instead of 20. [#130207](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130207) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a segmentation fault in `clickhouse-local` and in the client embedded into the server (SSH, web terminal) when a format that accumulates the result (`Pretty*` with the default `output_format_pretty_squash_consecutive_ms`, `*MonoBlock`, `JSONColumns`, `JSONCompactColumns`) hit a memory limit while copying it. The query now fails with `MEMORY_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`. [#130211](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130211) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed exponential parsing time of nested scalar subqueries and nested type arguments in which `CASE` or `INTERVAL` is used as an identifier, such as `case + (SELECT case + (SELECT ...))` over a column named `case`. Such valid queries failed with `TOO_SLOW_PARSING` at 17 levels of nesting. [#130213](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130213) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `dateDiff`, `age` and `timeDiff` with units from `hour` down to `nanosecond` returning wrong results for the `Date` 1970-01-01 in a time zone east of UTC (for example `dateDiff('second', toDate('1970-01-01'), toDate('1970-01-02'))` returned `0` in `Europe/Berlin`), and with unit `second` for a `Date` after 2106-02-07. `toRelativeMinuteNum` and `toRelativeHourNum` of that `Date` no longer wrap around to a value near `4294967295`, which made primary key, partition and statistics pruning drop rows. If a table stores such a value in a sorting key, partition key, skip index, projection or `MATERIALIZED`/`DEFAULT` column, rebuild it. [#130228](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130228) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SHOW CREATE DATABASE` and `SELECT * FROM system.databases` failing with `SYNTAX_ERROR` when a `Dictionary` database had a comment. [#130252](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130252) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed reading an ORC struct whose requested `Tuple` has none of the struct's fields (for example after schema evolution): such a list or map no longer loses its elements or crashes, the struct keeps its `NULL`s, and the requested fields are filled with default values. [#130279](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130279) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `system.licenses` in official builds: `pdqsort` was missing, and `libxml2`, `rapidjson` and `sz3` showed the license file of a test fixture or a bundled tool instead of the library's own license. [#130283](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130283) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Update vendored `expat` from 2.9.0 to 2.10.0 to fix a crash, denial-of-service issues and an integer overflow in XML parsing. [#130311](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130311) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Fixed an `ALTER TABLE` that combines a column change with a mutation or a partition command, such as `ALTER TABLE t ADD COLUMN c UInt8, UPDATE c = 1 WHERE c = 0`, failing with `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER` (or `INCOMPATIBLE_COLUMNS` for `ATTACH PARTITION ... FROM` and `MOVE PARTITION ... TO TABLE`) when `enable_shared_storage_snapshot_in_query` is enabled, which is the default. Closes [#130110](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130110). [#130322](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130322) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Update `mongo-c-driver` from 2.5.7 to 2.5.8 to fix a crash and a memory leak when a MongoDB server sends malformed replies. [#130323](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130323) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Prevent impersonation via forged SECONDARY_QUERY on secret clusters. [#130362](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130362) ([Vitaly Baranov](https://github.com/vitlibar)).
* Fix LOGICAL_ERROR when rejecting a non-convertible setting value. [#130372](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130372) ([Vitaly Baranov](https://github.com/vitlibar)).
* Fix async inserts in `JSON` format rejecting or dropping a valid document when an earlier entry in the same batch had no metadata and was parsed as `JSONEachRow`. [#130411](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130411) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed `joinGetOrNull` failing with `ILLEGAL_TYPE_OF_ARGUMENT` on a `LowCardinality` column of a `Join` table. [#130413](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130413) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Messages of the PostgreSQL wire protocol that are parsed before authentication are now bounded by the server, so a client can no longer make the server buffer a payload that it never sends. [#130462](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130462) ([Antonio Andelic](https://github.com/antonio2368)).
* Aggregate function `gini` now validates values when reading a serialized state, rejecting negative and infinite values (and NaN) the same way as ordinary input. [#130488](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130488) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Projections no longer provide `_block_number`, `_block_offset` and `_row_exists` unless they store them, so queries reading these columns use the main table. Previously the values came from the projection part, which caused wrong results and made incremental refreshable materialized views (`APPEND INCREMENTAL`) silently skip source rows. [#130515](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130515) ([Smita Kulkarni](https://github.com/SmitaRKulkarni)).
* Fix a server crash (SIGSEGV in `Poco::LogFileImpl::sizeImpl`) when the log file was rotated while the process had no free file descriptors. [#130517](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130517) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Treat an Azure answer without an ETag as `LOGICAL_ERROR`. [#130519](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130519) ([János Benjamin Antal](https://github.com/antaljanosbenjamin)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY COLUMN ... ADD ENUM VALUES` executed through an `Alias` table overwriting the enum values that a concurrent `ALTER` of the target table had added. Both queries succeeded, but the target's values were lost, and with `enable_shared_storage_snapshot_in_query = 0` the target table could no longer be attached after a restart. [#130528](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130528) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed filters on `toString` of an integer key, such as `toString(key) = '999999999999999999'`, skipping matching rows when one granule (or one `minmax` skip index granule) held integers of 15 or more digits with different numbers of digits. `toString(key) IN (subquery)` (also `CAST(key AS String)`) over an integer or `Date` key uses the primary key again when the subquery returns integers (or dates), as before 26.10. Closes [#130315](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130315). [#130537](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130537) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the `RabbitMQ` table engine not sending acknowledgements after a message was nacked. Improved validation of messages received by the `RabbitMQ` table engine. [#130541](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130541) ([Alexander Tokmakov](https://github.com/tavplubix)).
* Fixed `DeltaLake` queries hanging forever, without reacting to `KILL QUERY`, when delta-kernel could not create the thread that hands a result back (for example at a container PID limit). [#130544](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130544) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `GRANT` and `REVOKE` queries from the `grants` section of users configuration file on a database, table or column no longer affect privileges with a parameter of the same name, the same as SQL queries. Previously, e.g. `REVOKE ALL ON db.tbl` also revoked `READ, WRITE ON db('tbl')`, and `GRANT CURRENT GRANTS` failed with `Partial revokes of source filters are not supported` for such users. [#130557](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130557) ([Pervakov Grigorii](https://github.com/GrigoryPervakov)).
* The password in the connection string of the `odbc` and `jdbc` table functions and of the `ODBC` and `JDBC` table engines is no longer shown in cleartext in the bridge request URL quoted by errors, `system.query_log`, the server log and `system.schema_inference_cache`. The query itself already showed it as `[HIDDEN]`. [#130576](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130576) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE t ... AS SELECT ... FROM t` and `REPLACE TABLE` never returning when `database_atomic_wait_for_drop_and_detach_synchronously` is enabled: the statement waited for its own cached snapshot of the table it replaces. When the replaced table was a materialized view, the query could not be killed. [#130577](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130577) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `deltaLake`, `deltaLakeAzure`, `deltaLakeLocal` and the `DeltaLake` table engines using another table when the table path contains `#`: they read the snapshot of, and committed `INSERT`s into, the table at the part of the path before the `#`. [#130582](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130582) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a crash, or wrong results from a text index, after merging parts of a `MergeTree` table that has a text index and a column named `_part_index`. [#130584](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130584) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Backported in [#134104](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/134104): ARRAY JOIN of a Nested column with an alias (ARRAY JOIN nst AS n) now reads only the subcolumns that the query uses through n.x. Before, the query read all subcolumns of the Nested column. [#130600](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130600) ([Yaroslav Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Fixed a refreshable materialized view in a `Replicated` database failing to load when the server starts while Keeper is unreachable. The failed load was never retried, so the view stayed missing and DDL in the database failed until a restart. Now the view is attached and sets up its Keeper coordination once Keeper is reachable. Closes [#130250](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130250). [#130611](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130611) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a hang of queries over `Executable` and `ExecutablePool` tables and the `executable` table function with `check_exit_code = 1` that stop reading the command's output early, for example with `LIMIT`. Such a query never returned and was not stopped by `max_execution_time`. The exit code is now checked only when the output is read to the end. [#130614](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130614) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `SELECT ... ORDER BY` returning NULLs first on an Iceberg table that ClickHouse created with `ORDER BY` on a `Nullable` column, after another engine such as Spark wrote sorted files to it. `CREATE TABLE ... ENGINE = Iceberg... ORDER BY` now declares `null-order` `nulls-last` for an ascending field, the order ClickHouse sorts by, instead of `nulls-first` for every field. Closes [#130441](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130441). [#130628](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130628) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `formatReadableTimeDelta` throwing `std::length_error` when the minimum unit is above seconds, and use ` and ` before the last printed unit in that case. [#130637](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130637) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fixed `KILL QUERY` and a graceful server shutdown not cancelling an `INSERT` into a `Distributed` table that waits because its pending bytes are above `bytes_to_delay_insert`. Such an `INSERT` waited up to `max_delay_to_insert`, so with `max_delay_to_insert` above `shutdown_wait_unfinished` the server exited forcefully and lost the rows held in `Buffer` tables and in the asynchronous insert queue. [#130640](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130640) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SYSTEM` commands that take a table (for example `SYSTEM STOP MERGES`, `SYSTEM SYNC REPLICA`, `SYSTEM RESTART REPLICA`, `SYSTEM PREWARM MARK CACHE`) run `ON CLUSTER` with a table name without a database: they now act on the table in the current database, the one the access check uses, instead of the same-named table in each host's default database. Clusters whose hosts set `default_database`, and fully qualified names, behave as before. [#130644](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130644) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `addDate`, `subDate`, the `add*`/`subtract*` interval functions and `+`/`-` with an `INTERVAL` throwing `CANNOT_PARSE_DATETIME` for the `NULL` rows of a `Nullable(String)` date. Since 26.10 this also happened for the result of `formatDateTime` over a `Nullable` column, for example `addDate(formatDateTime(x, '%Y-%m-%d 00:00:00'), INTERVAL 1 DAY)`. Closes [#130381](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130381). [#130646](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130646) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `BACKUP` and `RESTORE` with `ON CLUSTER` now resolve an unqualified table name in the current database when the cluster's addresses do not set `default_database`, as the access check and other `ON CLUSTER` queries do; previously every host used its own default database, so the query could back up or restore another table than the one it was authorized for. On a cluster that mixes addresses with and without `default_database`, an unqualified name is now rejected, as for other `ON CLUSTER` queries. [#130663](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130663) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reading a binary string from input data or from an aggregate function state no longer allocates its declared length before the bytes arrive. A few bytes of `RowBinaryWithNames` input, a skipped `String` field in `RowBinaryWithNamesAndTypes`, or a state of `distinctDynamicTypes`, `distinctJSONPaths`, `groupArraySample` or `quantile` could make ClickHouse allocate and zero up to 1 GiB that `max_memory_usage` did not refuse. [#130665](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130665) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading a Delta Lake shallow clone with `deltaLake`, `deltaLakeLocal` and the `DeltaLake` table engine on local storage, and on S3 and GCS when the source table is in the same bucket: the data files of the source table, which the clone references by an absolute `file:`, `s3a://` or `gs://` path, were looked up inside the clone's directory, so every query that read data failed with a file-not-found error. Closes [#130550](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130550). [#130666](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130666) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `dictGetKeys` returning keys matched on the wrong attribute for `DIRECT` and `COMPLEX_KEY_DIRECT` dictionaries with more than one attribute. `dictGetKeys` on a `REGEXP_TREE` dictionary now throws `UNSUPPORTED_METHOD` instead of comparing the regexp tree nodes. [#130667](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130667) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `CREATE`, `ALTER`, `DROP` and `MOVE ROW POLICY ... ON CLUSTER` with a table name without a database now apply to the table in the current database, the one the privileges are checked against. Previously every host applied them to the table with that name in the server's default database. [#130673](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130673) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the query cache storing an empty result for a gRPC query with `use_query_cache = 1` that the client cancelled before the query returned its first block. Identical queries then returned no rows until the cache entry expired. [#130675](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130675) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `fuzzBits` with a constant first argument returning the argument unchanged when its result is read through a subquery (including `DISTINCT` and `LIMIT BY` over it), and a window function partitioned by such an expression computing wrong partitions. Closes [#118934](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/118934). [#130676](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130676) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
