* Fixed a crash, wrong values or an `INCORRECT_INDEX` exception when a query reads a subcolumn (such as `x.null` or `arr.size0`) through the `merge` table function or a `Merge` table from a table that cannot read subcolumns, for example `system.detached_parts`, `system.mutations`, `system.errors` or a `StripeLog` table. [#130680](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130680) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* With `backups.remove_backup_files_after_failure = false`, a `BACKUP` that failed before it started writing files (for example because its `base_backup` does not exist) no longer leaves its `.lock` behind. Every later `BACKUP` to the same destination was rejected with `BACKUP_ALREADY_EXISTS` until the lock was deleted by hand. [#130695](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130695) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a crash (`std::terminate`) when a log file with `logger.stream_compress` enabled cannot be written, for example because the disk is full: the process aborted when the log was rotated, reopened on `SIGHUP`, or closed at shutdown. Also fix a memory leak of about 208 KiB on every failed attempt to open such a log file. Closes [#130679](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130679). [#130724](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130724) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY SETTING buckets` with `s3queue_migrate_old_metadata_to_buckets = 1` on an Ordered `S3Queue` or `AzureQueue` table created without `buckets`. Such a table already spreads its files over `processing_threads_num` buckets, so the migration rewrote a layout it does not handle, and files that were already processed could be ingested again. The `ALTER` is now rejected with `SUPPORT_IS_DISABLED`. [#130733](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130733) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a Keeper crash when a client sends `CheckWatch` with an unknown watch type. Such requests now get `ZBADARGUMENTS`. [#130755](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130755) ([Samuel Krempaský](https://github.com/NIKTONIKTO717)).
* Fixed `DataLakeCatalog` building the URL of an Azure table without its container when `storage_endpoint` is set and the container name is a prefix of the storage account host or of an endpoint path segment (for example container `data` in account `datalake`), which made the table read from the wrong container. [#130783](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130783) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results of `ORDER BY ... LIMIT` on a Delta Lake table with column mapping when a renamed column has the name that another column is stored under: the top-K dynamic filter (`use_top_k_dynamic_filtering`) compared the other column against the threshold and dropped rows of the true result. [#130793](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130793) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix objects that stayed in the object storage after a failed write to a `cache` disk: the rollback evicted the written blobs from the filesystem cache, but did not delete them. [#130795](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130795) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY SETTING buckets` with `s3queue_migrate_old_metadata_to_buckets = 1` on an Ordered `S3Queue` or `AzureQueue` table with at most one bucket (`buckets = 1`, or `buckets` unset with `processing_threads_num = 1`). Migrating such a table to more buckets made it stop ingesting new files, and migrating it to one bucket made it ingest its already processed files again after a restart. [#130798](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130798) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an Iceberg table becoming unreadable (`INCORRECT_DATA`) when another engine had upgraded it from format version 1 to 2 or 3 and ClickHouse then made the next commit (`INSERT`, `ALTER TABLE ... DELETE/UPDATE`, `ALTER TABLE ... DROP PARTITION`). Also fixed any ClickHouse commit writing 0 as the file counts of manifests listed by Iceberg Java before 1.5, which made Java-based readers skip those manifests. Closes [#123045](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123045). [#130800](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130800) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix reading Iceberg data files written before an optional `variant`, `geometry` or `geography` field was added to the table (for example by `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN v VARIANT` in Spark). Such reads failed with `ICEBERG_SPECIFICATION_VIOLATION` (`Cannot add a column ... with required values`), even for queries that did not read the new field. The new field now reads as `NULL` in those files. Closes [#125427](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125427). [#130804](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130804) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `LOGICAL_ERROR` (`Metadata is not initialized`) in `ALTER TABLE ... DROP PARTITION` on an `Alias` table whose target is an Iceberg table in a `DataLakeCatalog` database. Closes [#126527](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126527). [#130807](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130807) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SELECT count()` on an Iceberg table counting deleted rows after an `INSERT` or `OPTIMIZE TABLE ... MANIFEST` on top of a snapshot whose summary omits the optional delete totals (`total-delete-files`, `total-position-deletes`, `total-equality-deletes`). Such totals now stay absent instead of being written as `0`. Closes [#125555](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125555). [#130810](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130810) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading Parquet `INT96` timestamps outside 1677-09-21 .. 2262-04-11 into `DateTime64` with a scale below 9 or into `Date32` (an explicit structure, or a Delta Lake `timestamp` column): a value the requested type can hold was rejected with `VALUE_IS_OUT_OF_RANGE_OF_DATA_TYPE`, or clamped to 1677/2262 with `date_time_overflow_behavior = 'saturate'`. Closes [#128415](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128415). [#130813](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130813) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a crash and wrong query results when a table has a column literally named `mapKeys(m)` or `mapValues(m)` with a `bloom_filter` index on it. [#130814](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130814) ([Elmi Ahmadov](https://github.com/ahmadov)).
* Fix server termination when outdated data parts cannot be scheduled for loading, e.g. when the global thread pool is exhausted. [#130816](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130816) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* Fix incomplete multipart uploads and orphaned blobs left on S3 when a cleanup request after a failed or cancelled query or merge failed once with a transient error: the cleanup now keeps its S3 retries after the cancellation. [#130818](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130818) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Fixed `GROUP BY GROUPING SETS` with an empty grouping set `()` returning the row of `()` when a `HAVING` condition, or a `WHERE` condition of an outer query, reads no column and is false, e.g. `SELECT a, count() FROM t GROUP BY GROUPING SETS ((), (a)) HAVING identity(0)`. Closes [#129888](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/129888). [#130822](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130822) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Use one source instead of N for parallel_inserts of AzureQueue/S3Queue. [#130864](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130864) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* `GRANT CURRENT GRANTS ... WITH REPLACE OPTION` now checks that the current user has the grant option for the privileges it revokes from the grantee, the same as a regular `GRANT ... WITH REPLACE OPTION`. [#130873](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130873) ([pufit](https://github.com/pufit)).
* Fix duplicated rows from a merge join of a distributed query plan (`make_distributed_plan`) when `query_plan_join_shard_by_pk_ranges` is enabled. [#130887](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130887) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a wrong order of rows for a query like `SELECT ... WHERE toDate(ts) = '2024-01-01' ORDER BY toDate(ts), b LIMIT 5` over a table with the sorting key `(ts, b)` when `optimize_read_in_order` is enabled. A query that filters a leading sorting key column by equality and lists it in `ORDER BY` (`WHERE a = 1 ORDER BY a, b`) is read in order through an `INNER JOIN` again. [#130893](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130893) ([Vladimir Cherkasov](https://github.com/vdimir)).
* Fixed a type confusion (possible crash or out-of-bounds read) triggered by a client sending a column with a `Sparse` or `Replicated` serialization kind, or a `Tuple` with a `Replicated` element, in data over the native protocol. [#130895](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130895) ([Vladimir Cherkasov](https://github.com/vdimir)).
* Fix `age` returning one whole unit too many when a `DateTime64` operand is before 1677-09-21 or after 2262-04-11. Closes [#119574](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/119574). [#130907](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130907) ([Yaroslav Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Exclude time spend for admission from max_execution_time. [#130910](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130910) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Lazy materialization of `MergeTree` columns (also over `JOIN`) no longer disables projections. A normal projection can filter the parts and granules of the read, and can serve the read if it has `_parent_part_offset` or all lazily read columns. [#130913](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130913) ([Nikolai Kochetov](https://github.com/KochetovNicolai)).
* Fix `make_distributed_plan` queries that failed when a replica of the worker cluster was stopped but still registered in the cluster. Such a replica no longer gets tasks. [#130917](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130917) ([Alexander Gololobov](https://github.com/davenger)).
* This patch fixes peer-group calculations for window functions with collation enabled. [#130946](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130946) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* Fix `SIMILAR TO ... ESCAPE` with a negated bracket expression such as `[^a]`: the `^` was duplicated, so the pattern returned wrong results (for example, `'^' SIMILAR TO '[^a]' ESCAPE '#'` returned 0) or failed with `CANNOT_COMPILE_REGEXP` (for example, `[^-0-9]`). [#130950](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130950) ([khu](https://github.com/kuxn)).
* `CREATE NAMED COLLECTION`, `ALTER NAMED COLLECTION` and `DROP NAMED COLLECTION` are now rejected in readonly mode and with `allow_ddl = 0`, like other DDL queries. [#130952](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130952) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `clickhouse-local` with a system log (e.g. `query_log`) configured on a persistent path keeps the system log tables between runs. Previously, the table was missing on the next run, because its metadata was not saved while its data was. [#130960](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130960) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The `MySQLDump` input format now reads values by the column list of the `INSERT`, so dumps of tables with generated columns can be read. [#130972](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130972) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed `SHOW CREATE TABLE`, `DESCRIBE` and `SELECT` failing with `POCO_EXCEPTION` on a non-Delta table of an open-source Unity catalog when `use_unity_catalog_v2` is disabled. [#130974](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130974) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed an empty or missing `CSV`/`TSV` field being stored as 0 in an `Enum` column that has no 0 element, which made later reads fail with `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM`. Now the field gets the first `Enum` element. [#130975](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130975) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed skipping an unknown `JSON` column in `RowBinaryWithNamesAndTypes` when `input_format_binary_read_json_as_string` is enabled. [#130978](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130978) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fix reading a `CSV` or `TSV` field that starts with a carriage return not followed by a line feed: it was read as an empty field and the row failed to parse. [#130983](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130983) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fix RowBinary input with a `JSON` column producing extra or wrong rows, or a `LOGICAL_ERROR`, when an object repeats a path and `type_json_skip_duplicated_paths` is enabled. The first value of the path is now kept. [#130984](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130984) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fix a possible crash of the server after a memory allocation failed while a background task was rescheduling itself. [#130997](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130997) ([Sergei Trifonov](https://github.com/serxa)).
* Fix a column TTL on an `Enum` column without `DEFAULT`: a merge or `MATERIALIZE TTL` replaced expired values with `0` instead of the first value of the `Enum`, so reading the table failed with `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM`. [#131025](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131025) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed a use-after-free on reading a Delta Lake table with column mapping in which a column is stored under the name of another column (for example, after column mapping is enabled on an existing table and columns are renamed), when the columns are requested in a particular order, such as `SELECT k_old, k`. [#131029](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131029) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix ORC files with `DateTime64` values before 1970 with a fractional part being rejected by Java readers (Hive, Spark): the nanoseconds were stored negative. [#131036](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131036) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* ... [#131046](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131046) ([Foad Lotfifar](https://github.com/foadl)).
* Fixed a query like `WHERE t.safe != '' AND toUInt64(t.val) > 50` throwing an exception such as `CANNOT_PARSE_TEXT` when the condition on one subcolumn of a `Tuple`, `JSON` or bucketed `Map` column guards a conversion on another subcolumn of the same column: moving the conditions to `PREWHERE` no longer evaluates the conversion before the guard. [#131054](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131054) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Fixed `zk_outstanding_requests` in the Keeper `mntr`, `stat` and `srvr` commands sometimes showing 18446744073709551615 under load. [#131074](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131074) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed lost rows after `DETACH`/`ATTACH` or a restart when an `INSERT` into a `Set` or `Join` table was stopped by `max_execution_time` with `timeout_overflow_mode = 'break'`. [#131090](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131090) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed reading a Parquet list of structs with an explicit structure or a data lake schema that requests only elements the file does not have: `l.d` and `l Array(Tuple(d ...))` returned empty arrays, so `ARRAY JOIN` and `length` saw no elements, instead of arrays of default values with the sizes of the list in the file. [#131125](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131125) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a possible server crash when reading a `Decimal` value with an out-of-range scale from serialized data; such a value is now rejected with an error. [#131154](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131154) ([Shaohua Wang](https://github.com/tiandiwonder)).
* Fix KILL QUERY having no effect on an INSERT into a Buffer table when the flush waits in the bytes_to_delay_insert delay of a Distributed destination table. [#131159](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131159) ([marouane el fadli](https://github.com/Marouane-elfadli)).
* Fixed a leak of objects in the bucket of an object storage disk (such as S3) when a replicated merge or mutation wrote its part and then failed to add it to the table, for example when it was retried after a failed commit to Keeper. [#131162](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131162) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `clickhouse-client` parsing the data of `INSERT ... SELECT ... FROM input(...)` partly or entirely in its own time zone instead of the `session_timezone` the server uses for the query (for example one set in a user profile with `apply_settings_from_server = 0`). This also fixes a data race on `session_timezone` in the client. [#131177](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131177) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `Map` columns with `map_serialization_version = 'with_buckets'` returning their pairs grouped by bucket instead of in the inserted order when stored in Compact parts written with `write_marks_for_substreams_in_compact_parts = 0`, which made `m = <inserted map>` false and let merges keep the reordered value. Closes [#130897](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130897). [#131182](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131182) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an element added to a named `Tuple` by `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY COLUMN`, `INSERT ... SELECT` or `CAST` getting 0 in existing rows instead of the default of its type. For an `Enum` whose first value is not 0 this is the wrong value, and often not a value of the `Enum` at all: after such an `ALTER`, `SELECT *` failed with `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM`. [#131186](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131186) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a table on a read-only `plain_rewritable` disk loading its parts from a stale copy of the disk metadata on `ATTACH` or on the first access with `lazy_load_tables`, which returned wrong results or failed with `TOO_MANY_UNEXPECTED_DATA_PARTS` after the writer had merged and removed parts. Closes [#130933](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130933). [#131188](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131188) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A column missing from `Native` or `JSONColumns` input, and an element of a named `Tuple` missing from a JSON object, get the default of their type instead of the zero of the column: an `Enum` without a 0 member stored an invalid value, and every later read of the table failed with `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM`. [#131189](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131189) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an explicit `NULL` read into a non-`Nullable` column with `input_format_null_as_default` storing the zero of the column instead of the default of its type in the text formats and `BSONEachRow`. An `Enum` without a 0 element got 0, after which every `SELECT` of the table failed with `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM`; `INSERT ... VALUES (NULL)` already stored the default. [#131190](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131190) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `DEFAULT` in `INSERT ... VALUES`, the per-field default flag of `RowBinaryWithDefaults` and `RowBinaryWithNamesAndTypesAndDefaults`, and an empty string read with `input_format_json_empty_as_default = 1` storing 0 instead of the default of the column type. An `Enum` column without a 0 element received a value outside the type, after which the table could not be read (`UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM`); an `Enum` whose 0 element is not the first one received the wrong value. [#131198](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131198) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a valid query failing with an exception such as `ILLEGAL_DIVISION` when a JOIN runtime filter (`enable_join_runtime_filters`, enabled by default) built by an `INNER JOIN` was also pushed to the side of a `LEFT`, `RIGHT` or `ANTI JOIN` below it that is only matched against. That join then produced rows the query never returns, and expressions were evaluated on them. Closes [#131130](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131130). [#131201](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131201) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `aiClassify`, `aiFilter` and `aiExtract` now handle a model reply that has whitespace before the JSON object. Before, they returned the raw JSON, or `0` for `aiFilter`. [#131212](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131212) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed `JSONExtract` functions and the `JSON` data type filling a value missing from the input (an absent key, an invalid `Array` or `Map` element, a missing `Tuple` element) with 0 instead of the default of its type. An `Enum` without a 0 element no longer gets a value outside its elements, which made `SELECT` fail with `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM`. [#131213](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131213) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an Avro `null` or a MsgPack `nil` read into a non-`Nullable` column with `input_format_null_as_default` storing the zero of the column instead of the default of its type. An `Enum` without a 0 element got 0, after which every `SELECT` of the table failed with `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM`. [#131219](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131219) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The first `INSERT` into an empty Iceberg table in a `DataLakeCatalog` database now works with catalogs that validate the commit against the Iceberg REST specification, such as BigLake: the `assert-ref-snapshot-id` requirement now carries `"snapshot-id": null` instead of omitting it. Closes [#131155](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131155). [#131225](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131225) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A `SELECT` from an `IcebergS3`, `IcebergAzure` or `IcebergLocal` table with an `Array` column no longer fails with `Iceberg schema with schema-id 0 is bound to two different schemas across metadata versions` after another writer, such as PyIceberg, commits to the table. ClickHouse no longer writes a `required` member, which the Iceberg spec does not define, into `list` types. Closes [#131156](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131156). [#131240](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131240) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `WHERE` condition on a Hive partition column (`use_hive_partitioning = 1`) skipping files whose rows match it when the path filter parsed the partition value differently from the reader, for example `k0=300` for a `Nullable(UInt8)` column (read as NULL) or `a=NULL` for a `Nullable(String)` column (read as the string `'NULL'`). Closes [#127133](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/127133). [#131256](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131256) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix the native ORC reader returning fewer rows than expected for filters on `TIMESTAMP` columns when `input_format_orc_filter_push_down` is enabled and the reader or writer time zone is not `UTC`. [#131274](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131274) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fixed reading a Delta Lake shallow clone on Azure with `deltaLakeAzure`, `deltaLakeAzureCluster`, the `DeltaLakeAzure` table engine and `DeltaLake` on an Azure disk: the data files of the source table, which the clone references by an absolute `abfss://` or `wasbs://` URI, were looked up inside the clone's directory, so every query that read data failed with `404 The specified blob does not exist`. [#131282](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131282) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed formatting of a `LIMIT ... AFTER` / `UNTIL` boundary that starts with a quoted identifier named like a keyword (e.g. `` `settings` ``): the formatted query could not be parsed back. [#131287](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131287) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The `disk` setting is now rejected by the `paimon*` and `hudi` table functions. Previously they silently used a Delta Lake configuration over the disk, or crashed in builds without Delta Lake support. [#131288](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131288) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Throw an exception instead of computing a wrong buffer size when the result of `ARRAY JOIN` over a `String` or `FixedString` column would be larger than the address space. Original fix by @alexey-milovidov. [#131294](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131294) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Better validation of malformed Parquet files with RLE and `DELTA_BINARY_PACKED`-family encodings in the native Parquet reader. Original fix by @alexey-milovidov. [#131295](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131295) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Throw an exception instead of reading or writing out of bounds when a patch part of lightweight updates has invalid values of `_part_offset`. Original fix by @alesapin. [#131296](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131296) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Reject invalid file names in the index of packed data parts. Original fix by @alesapin. [#131297](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131297) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* A key of a dynamic disk definition (`disk(...)`) can now be specified only once. Original fix by @alesapin. [#131298](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131298) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* More validation of the data read from the file of an `ssd_cache` dictionary. Original fix by @oranjeai. [#131299](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131299) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* On a cluster without an interserver secret, remote nodes now apply the row policies of the roles set by `SET ROLE` on the initiator, not of the default roles of the user. Original fix by @fm4v. [#131300](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131300) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Throw an exception on too deep nesting of the `Multiple` compression codec in compressed data. Original fix by @tavplubix. [#131301](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131301) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Validate the bucket numbers of two-level aggregation in blocks received over the native protocol. Original fix by @motsc and @CheSema. [#131302](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131302) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed exponential parsing time of queries with nested unclosed brackets around subqueries, which could exceed `max_parser_backtracks` instead of reporting a syntax error. [#131310](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131310) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `optimize_extract_common_expressions` moving the conditions that every branch of an `OR` starts with behind the rest of the `OR` when the `OR` is part of an `AND`. On tables with Compact parts and no statistics this made a lookup like `m['key'] = 'x'` the first `PREWHERE` step, so the whole `Map` was read for every granule (up to 5x slower), and a guard such as `x != 0` in `(x != 0 AND intDiv(10, x) = 1) OR (x != 0 AND intDiv(10, x) = 2)` could stop protecting `intDiv`, failing the query with `Division by zero`. [#131341](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131341) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a server crash on a `Decimal` parameter with a scale above the maximum precision. [#131343](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131343) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Fix the logical error `Context has expired` when the Kusto `*` operator (`kqlMultiply`) is used in a query with parallel replicas. [#131363](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131363) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Check the scale of a `Decimal` value before formatting it as text, so a value with an out-of-range scale produces an exception instead of undefined behavior. [#131366](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131366) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed parsing of decimal text with an exponent close to the `Int32` limits: values such as `1e2147483647` were read as `0` instead of being rejected as too big. [#131373](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131373) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `Unsupported storage type: abfs://` when reading a table from a `DataLakeCatalog` database (for example OneLake) whose catalog reports table locations with the plain `abfs://` scheme. Previously only `abfss://` was accepted, although both name the same ADLS Gen2 storage. [#131389](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131389) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Fixed `count()` counting rows with a `NULL` key value that the filter rejects, when the filter has `NOT IN` (or `NOT (... IN ...)`) over a `Nullable` primary key column that the in-memory primary index does not load (it follows a high-cardinality key prefix), with `allow_nullable_key = 1` and `use_lightweight_primary_key_index_analysis = 1` (the default). [#131390](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131390) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `Division by zero` and similar exceptions when `optimize_redundant_comparisons` removed a comparison that guarded another condition because a later comparison implied it, e.g. `x != 0 AND intDiv(10, x) > 0 AND x > 5`. The stronger comparison now takes the place of the removed one. [#131396](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131396) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `TimeSeries` table with an external target in a database named `inner` failing to load with `SYNTAX_ERROR`. [#131405](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131405) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fix `dictGetKeys` returning keys from another dictionary in the same query when the dictionary and attribute names of both calls concatenate to the same string. [#131406](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131406) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed a crash when a serialized query plan contains an expression node that references a node which is not decoded yet; such a plan is now rejected with `INCORRECT_DATA`. Closes [#131338](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131338). [#131412](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131412) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix the `SQLite` input format storing 0 instead of the type default for a `NULL` value read into a non-Nullable `Enum` column (or an `Enum` inside a `Tuple`) with `input_format_null_as_default`. [#131415](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131415) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed `ie_join` (used by default for a `JOIN` whose `ON` section has two inequality conditions and no equality) filling the unmatched rows of an outer or anti join with `0` for `Enum` columns, also inside `Tuple` and `JSON` typed paths, when `join_use_nulls = 0`. `0` is not a value of the type, so reading the column threw `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM` and filters on it returned wrong results. The rows now get the type default, as in the `hash` join. [#131418](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131418) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `INSERT ... VALUES (NULL)` into a non-`Nullable` `Enum` column with `input_format_null_as_default` failing with `CANNOT_CONVERT_TYPE` (or `SUPPORT_IS_DISABLED`) when `input_format_values_interpret_expressions = 0`. [#131422](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131422) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* The error of AI functions for an insecure endpoint no longer contains the endpoint of the named collection, which could carry a secret not visible to the user. [#131438](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131438) ([George Larionov](https://github.com/george-larionov)).
* Fixed the `direct` join with a MergeTree right table filling the right columns of not-found keys of a `LEFT` or `LEFT ANTI` join with `0` for `Enum` columns, also inside a `Tuple` or a `JSON` typed path, when `join_use_nulls = 0`. `0` is not a value of the type, so reading the column threw `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM` and filters on it returned wrong results. These rows now get the type default, as with the other join algorithms. [#131456](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131456) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `JOIN` whose `ON` section has no equality (the block nested loop join) filling the unmatched rows of an `Enum` column with `0`, a value the type does not declare, when `join_use_nulls = 0`: reading such a column threw `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM` and filters on it returned wrong results. The rows now get the type default, as with the `hash` join. Closes [#130556](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130556). [#131474](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131474) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `aiExtract` with a JSON schema whose only field is `result` now returns the JSON object, like any other schema, instead of the bare value (or an empty string for `null`). [#131487](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131487) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM` when reading a `JSON` column whose typed `Enum` path was absent from a `RowBinary` value. The path now gets the type default instead of 0. [#131498](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131498) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* The `MySQLDump` input format now reads double-quoted and multi-line column lists of `INSERT`, so values of `mysqldump --compatible=ansi` dumps are no longer replaced with defaults. [#131499](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131499) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed wrong results of `CollapsingMergeTree`: a merge could keep a stale cancel row and change `sum(sign)` of a key, and `SELECT ... FINAL` could return a row of a cancelled object, when the oldest part holding the key was a merged part with a cancel and a state row of that key. The bug existed since 24.1. [#131513](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131513) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a data race when inserting into an Iceberg table whose data files have a text format, such as `JSON`. [#131515](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131515) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY COLUMN` of an Iceberg table can increase the precision of a `Decimal` column, and rejects a change the Iceberg spec does not allow with a proper error instead of a `Poco` bad cast exception. [#131518](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131518) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `ALTER TABLE ... RENAME COLUMN` of a column used in the `PARTITION BY` or `ORDER BY` of an Iceberg table no longer fails with `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER`. [#131519](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131519) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed the `merge` table function with an empty database, `merge('', 'regexp')`, in a subquery or a `JOIN` of a query that is sent to other servers (`remote`, a `Distributed` table, `*Cluster` table functions, parallel replicas): those servers read the tables of their own default database instead of the current database of the query, which returned wrong results or failed with `CANNOT_EXTRACT_TABLE_STRUCTURE`. [#131528](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131528) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `JSONExtract` (and `JSONExtractKeysAndValues`, and `Variant` targets) now returns `NULL` or the default value when a number does not fit the target `Decimal` range, instead of throwing `DECIMAL_OVERFLOW`. This matches the existing behavior for integer targets and for the same number passed as a string. [#131538](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131538) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fixed `INSERT` into a `NATS` or `RabbitMQ` table with a `MATERIALIZED` column, which always failed with `Block structure mismatch`. `MATERIALIZED` columns are not written to the messages, matching what these engines read back. Closes [#131329](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131329). [#131556](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131556) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM` when reading a `JSON` column with an `Enum` typed path filled with the default of its type (an `INSERT` that omits the column, parts written before `ADD COLUMN`, `CLEAR COLUMN`, or an expired column TTL in a Wide part). Such a path now holds the first `Enum` value instead of 0. [#131565](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131565) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `CAST` of a NULL `Dynamic`, `Variant` or `JSON` path value to an `Enum` (also inside a `Tuple`, `Array`, `Map` or a typed `JSON` path) producing 0 instead of the first value of the `Enum` when `cast_keep_nullable = 0`. When 0 is not a value of the `Enum`, a table written from such a column by `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY COLUMN`, `INSERT ... SELECT` or a materialized view could no longer be read (`UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM`). [#131567](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131567) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a `WHERE` on a Hive partition column of a `File`, `URL`, `S3`, `AzureBlobStorage` or `HDFS` table skipping files whose rows match it, or failing with `CANNOT_PARSE_BOOL`, when the table's format settings (for example `bool_true_representation` in its `SETTINGS`) change how the column's values are parsed. [#131571](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131571) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `FINAL` query with `use_skip_indexes_if_final = 0` and `distributed_index_analysis = 1` returning rows superseded by newer versions: the replicas of distributed index analysis pruned parts by skip indexes that `FINAL` had disabled. [#131573](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131573) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `CORRUPTED_DATA` from `dictHas` and keys never leaving memory in `ssd_cache` and `complex_key_ssd_cache` dictionaries without attributes. [#131579](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131579) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed `Logical error: Chosen multipart upload for an empty file` when an Azure Blob Storage disk with `max_single_part_upload_size = 0` copies an empty file, for example on `ATTACH TABLE` of a permanently detached table in a database stored on a `plain_rewritable` Azure disk. [#131587](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131587) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed formatting of a `LIMIT`, `OFFSET` or `FETCH` count that starts with an identifier named `after` or `until`, such as ``LIMIT `after` - 5``. It was printed as `LIMIT after - 5` and read back as the range `LIMIT AFTER -5`, so a view with such a count returned other rows after a restart, or its database failed to load. Views created before this fix with such a count must be re-created. [#131591](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131591) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a memory leak when the merge of the per-thread hash tables of `GROUP BY` failed with an exception, for example on `max_memory_usage`: the states of aggregate functions such as `uniqExact` or `quantileExact` that waited to be merged were never freed. [#131595](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131595) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `x IN (...)` with a constant tuple returning different rows depending on the order of the tuple members when one member is a `Bool` holding a byte other than 0 or 1 (for example from `reinterpret`). Such a member now always counts as `true`, as it already did when it was the first member. [#131598](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131598) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed incorrect row numbers in error messages and incorrect splitting of rows containing a `\r` character when the `Regexp` input format is parsed in parallel. [#131601](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131601) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `Unknown expression or function identifier __grouping_set` error of the `grouping` function with `GROUPING SETS`, `ROLLUP` or `CUBE` over a `Merge` table (or the `merge` table function) that reads both local and `Distributed` tables. [#131614](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131614) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SummingMergeTree` and `CoalescingMergeTree` changing the alternative of `Variant` values in columns that are not summed: `INSERT` (with `optimize_on_insert`), `SELECT ... FINAL` and merges turned a `UInt64` in a `Variant(Date, UInt64)` column into a `Date`, also inside `Array`, `Tuple` and `Map`. [#131615](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131615) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results of a subquery that enables parallel replicas in its own `SETTINGS` clause when the result comes from a projection, the `min`/`max`/`count()` part metadata or an exact count from the primary key. For example, `SELECT * FROM (SELECT max(x) FROM t SETTINGS enable_parallel_replicas = 1, ...)` returned 0. [#131618](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131618) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `rows_before_limit_at_least` with parallel replicas and the default `parallel_replicas_local_plan = 1`: the rows read by the local replica were not counted, so the value was often 0. The same is fixed for the local shard of a `Distributed` or `cluster()` query with `prefer_localhost_replica = 1` when the shard query runs to `WithMergeableState`, for example with `extremes = 1`. Closes [#113279](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/113279). [#131626](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131626) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `decrypt` and `tryDecrypt` in AES-GCM modes accepting a modified authentication tag when the plaintext is empty. An empty string is no longer accepted as AES-GCM ciphertext, since it has no tag: `decrypt` throws, and `tryDecrypt` returns NULL for it and for any other input shorter than the tag. [#131629](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131629) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fix `sum` and `avg` over nullable `Int128`, `UInt128`, `Int256`, `UInt256`, `Decimal128` and `Decimal256` counting NULL rows whose null-map byte is not 1. [#131630](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131630) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fix `CHECK GRANT ALL ON db.table` returning 0 for a user who was granted `ALL ON db.table`. [#131631](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131631) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed connecting over the native protocol (`clickhouse-client`, `remote`, `Distributed` without an interserver `secret`) to an older server whose settings profile has a negative or overflowing value of a `Seconds` setting, for example `max_execution_time = -1`: the connection failed with `A setting's value in seconds cannot be negative`. Such a value sent by the server is now skipped with a warning. [#131636](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131636) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `DROP WORKLOAD` removing a resource with the same name and `DROP RESOURCE` removing a workload with the same name when workload entities are stored in Keeper (`workload_zookeeper_path`). A user with only one of the two privileges could remove the other kind of entity. [#131688](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131688) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `parallel_inserts` of `S3Queue`/`AzureQueue`: the setting had no effect on the parallelism of reading, because it was read from table metadata where it was never stored. [#131689](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131689) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `CREATE WORKLOAD`, `DROP WORKLOAD`, `CREATE RESOURCE` and `DROP RESOURCE` with `workload_zookeeper_path` overwriting definitions written by other replicas when the query was the first to use a renewed Keeper session. [#131719](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131719) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `count()` and schema inference of `s3` with `versionId` in the URL: the schema and row-count caches shared one entry between all versions of an object, so the row count or the inferred schema of one version could be returned for another version read later on the same server. [#131721](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131721) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix wrong results with `group_by_use_nulls = 1` and `ROLLUP`, `CUBE` or `GROUPING SETS` when a `GROUP BY` key uses another key in several places, for example `if(x = 1 OR x = 2, 'a', 'b')` grouped together with `x`. The key was computed again from the `NULL` of the inner key, with a non-`Nullable` type, instead of being read as the key. [#131726](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131726) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a possible crash of the `Template` input format when the format string for the result set does not contain `${data}`. Now it is an error. [#131730](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131730) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Backported in [#133185](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133185): Fix a possible crash of the `RabbitMQ` table engine when the broker confirms a message with an unknown delivery tag. [#131731](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131731) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a server abort when merging a malformed serialized state of a `-Map` aggregate function with a `FixedString` key. Now such a state is rejected with `INCORRECT_DATA`. [#131732](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131732) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a possible crash when an exception (for example, a memory limit) is thrown during the short-circuit evaluation of a function returning an `AggregateFunction` state. [#131733](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131733) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a possible crash of the `PostgreSQL` table engine, table function and database engine when the remote server reports an absurd number of array dimensions. [#131734](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131734) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a stack overflow in the `ParquetMetadata` format for files with a very deeply nested schema. The depth is now limited by `max_parser_depth`, as in the `Parquet` format, and never exceeds 1000. [#131735](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131735) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix out-of-bounds reads when deserializing a query plan with a step lacking some of its inputs or having extra children. Now such a plan is rejected with `INCORRECT_DATA`. [#131736](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131736) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a server crash when a client sends a serialized query plan (with `process_query_plan_packet` enabled) that contains a constant node without a value. [#131742](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131742) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a crash of the `DWARF` input format on an ELF file with a malformed attribute length. [#131787](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131787) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix out-of-bounds reads when deserializing a query plan with a `FillingStep` repeating an `INTERPOLATE` column. Now such a plan is rejected with `INCORRECT_DATA`. [#131788](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131788) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Backported in [#133177](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133177): Fix type confusion when deserializing a query plan whose aggregate functions do not match the types of their input columns. Now such a plan is rejected with `INCORRECT_DATA`. [#131789](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131789) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix out-of-bounds memory access with an aggregate state containing a `JSON` value with a repeated path. Now such a state is rejected with `INCORRECT_DATA`. [#131791](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131791) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix out-of-bounds memory access with a state of `mergedJSONPatch` containing a repeated path. Now such a state is rejected with `INCORRECT_DATA`. [#131792](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131792) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix PromQL regex matchers with a top-level `|`, such as `{pod=~"zzz|gold.*"}`, matching values that only contain one of the alternatives. Now every alternative has to match the whole value, as in Prometheus. [#131794](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131794) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fix a one-byte heap overflow when reading a `String` state of `any`/`min`/`max` (and similar) aggregate functions in an old format that ends without the zero terminator. Original fix by @motsc. [#131795](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131795) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a possible out-of-bounds read in the `Parquet` reader when `PREWHERE` filters out a whole page of a `PLAIN`-encoded string column read through a conversion (e.g. to `Geometry`). [#131797](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131797) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a possible crash when restoring a backup from a zip archive with a password if an encrypted entry of the archive is smaller than the encryption header. [#131806](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131806) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a hang of the workload scheduler when a configuration reload or a Keeper refresh of workloads makes a workload a child of its former child. [#131808](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131808) ([Sergei Trifonov](https://github.com/serxa)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` for an explicit `PREWHERE` on a Parquet read (`file`, `url`, `s3` and their table engines) when the condition uses a column whose name is another read column's name plus a dot, for example ``SELECT v ... PREWHERE `v.tag` = 1``. This was a regression in 26.8. [#131856](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131856) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix wrong order of rows and wrong results of `LIMIT WITH TIES` when a sort key follows the same key with `COLLATE`, or is a function of another sort key in a query with `ROLLUP`, `CUBE` or `GROUPING SETS`. Fix lost rows in `LIMIT BY` when `ORDER BY` has the same key twice. [#131863](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131863) ([eoger-ant](https://github.com/eoger-ant)).
* Fix wrong results of `uniq` over a subquery with `GROUP BY` or `DISTINCT` with `distributed_group_by_no_merge`, for an empty set of `Nullable` values, as a window function over subtotals, with repeated arguments, or of a correlated column, and an exception for `uniqCombined` with parameters. [#131864](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131864) ([eoger-ant](https://github.com/eoger-ant)).
* `UNDROP TABLE` now works with an `UNDROP TABLE` grant on the database or the table. Before, it always required the grant `ON *.*`. [#131892](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131892) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* `SYSTEM SYNC DATABASE REPLICA` without `ON CLUSTER` now requires the `SYSTEM SYNC DATABASE REPLICA` grant on the database. Before, any user could run it. [#131893](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131893) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* The `/binary` web UI now displays the symbol name and source line in the popup as text instead of interpreting the server response as HTML. [#131895](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131895) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `THERE_IS_NO_COLUMN` / `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` exception with the analyzer when a server-constant function such as `hostName` or `tcpPort` is used both in an outer query and in a subquery over `clusterAllReplicas` (e.g. in branches of `UNION ALL`). [#131901](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131901) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Backported in [#133192](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133192): Fixed a `Merge` table or the `merge` table function over a `Distributed` table returning one column when the query selects the same column more than once, for example `SELECT k, k`. [#131928](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131928) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading a nullable Parquet column into a non-`Nullable` `FixedString` or `String` column with `input_format_null_as_default = 1` (the default): a `NULL` was read as the value of another row instead of the default value. This affected `FIXED_LEN_BYTE_ARRAY` columns and fixed-size numeric columns read as `FixedString(N)`, also inside `Array`, `Tuple` and `Map`. Closes [#131919](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131919). [#131939](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131939) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a regression from [#110006](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/110006) where `(range) OR x IS NULL`, with `x` a `Nullable` cast of a sort key expression (for example a column of a `UNION ALL` view whose other branch returns `NULL`), could not use the primary key or partition pruning and read all parts. [#131944](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131944) ([Seva Potapov](https://github.com/seva-potapov)).
* Fixed a rare `LOGICAL_ERROR` exception in Keeper with the on-disk LSMT node storage (`use_lsmt_storage`) when several requests read a node whose block had been evicted from the block cache at the same time. The exception made Keeper abort. [#131947](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131947) ([Shaohua Wang](https://github.com/tiandiwonder)).
* Fixed a constant column of a subquery returning the type default (`0`, `''`, `[]`) instead of its value for the unmatched rows of an outer join, or failing with `ILLEGAL_COLUMN`, when join reordering merges the subquery's projection into the join (`query_plan_merge_expression_into_join`). Closes [#131590](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131590). [#131961](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131961) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a graceful server shutdown that lost the rows of a `Buffer` table or of the asynchronous insert queue flushed into a `Distributed` table whose pending bytes were above `bytes_to_throw_insert` (for example, with its shards unreachable). Once the shutdown has started, such a flush is no longer rejected by `bytes_to_throw_insert`. [#131969](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131969) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER` for `__grouping_set` when a query uses `grouping` with `GROUPING SETS`, `ROLLUP` or `CUBE` over a `Buffer` table whose destination is a `Distributed` table. [#131976](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131976) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed wrong results of a query with a lambda function. They occurred when the sorting key, the partition key or an aggregate projection of the table had another lambda function with the same captured columns. [#131982](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131982) ([Yaroslav Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Fixed `DateTime64` and `Time64` query parameters changing value or failing to parse once the query is stored (a row policy filter, a column `DEFAULT`, a mutation of a `Replicated` table): a row policy `USING ts <= {ts:DateTime64(3, 'UTC')}` with a value before 1973-03-03 showed rows it must hide, and a `Time64` parameter made every read of the table fail with `CANNOT_PARSE_TEXT`. [#131998](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131998) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong query results on a table restored from a backup of a `MaterializedPostgreSQL` database when the PostgreSQL primary key or replica identity of the table changed after replication started (or the key column order changed with an upgrade): the backup stored a sorting key built from the current PostgreSQL table instead of the key of the nested table that holds the data. `BACKUP`, `SHOW CREATE TABLE` and `system.tables` now use the stored definition of the nested table. Closes [#128271](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128271). [#132000](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132000) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `TRUNCATE`, `DROP PARTITION` and `REPLACE PARTITION` on a `ReplicatedMergeTree` table that had been idle for a long time hanging for up to 10 minutes (`background_processing_pool_task_sleep_seconds_when_no_work_max`) when the common background pool was full at the moment the replication queue entry was scheduled. Closes [#131617](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131617). [#132001](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132001) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `CREATE TEMPORARY VIEW` is now refused with `readonly = 1`, the same as `CREATE TEMPORARY TABLE`. [#132016](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132016) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed reading a Parquet `VARIANT` column or an Iceberg Avro variant as `JSON` with an `Enum` typed path: a null value, an empty object or a null field stored 0 in the path instead of the default of its type, so reading it failed with `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM`, or returned another element when 0 is one. [#132017](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132017) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the two-argument `arrayResize`, which filled new elements of an `Enum` (also inside a `Tuple` or a `JSON` typed path) with 0 instead of the default of the type, failing with `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM` or returning the wrong member. Regression since 26.6. [#132022](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132022) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` with `join_algorithm = 'full_sorting_merge'`, `'parallel_full_sorting_merge'` or `'direct'` (with a dictionary) for a join whose `ON` has an `indexHint` (a `LEFT`, `FULL`, `ANY` or `ASOF` join under `full_sorting_merge`), including the one `optimize_or_like_chain` adds to an `OR` chain of `LIKE`/`ILIKE` conditions. Closes [#126786](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/126786). [#132034](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132034) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `url` table function and the `URL` table engine now accept HTTP response headers with fields longer than 8 KiB. The limits come from the `http_max_fields`, `http_max_field_name_size` and `http_max_field_value_size` settings. [#132069](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132069) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Fixed endpoints configured in the `<s3>` section taking the `s3_*` settings of the first S3 query after a server start: later queries and backups to such an endpoint ran with that query's settings, which could slow down backups (for example with `s3_max_inflight_parts_for_one_file = 1`) or reject valid settings with `INVALID_SETTING_VALUE`. Closes [#131964](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131964). [#132083](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132083) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a `NULL` element of an `Array`, `Tuple` or `Map` (key or value) of a non-`Nullable` `Enum` being stored as 0 instead of the default of the element type when read with `input_format_null_as_default` from text formats (`Values`, `JSONEachRow`, `CSV`, `TSV` and others) or by a `CAST` from `String`. For an `Enum` without a 0 element, every later `SELECT` of the column failed with `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM`. The same is fixed for an empty string element with `input_format_json_empty_as_default`. [#132086](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132086) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `LEFT ARRAY JOIN` and `emptyArrayToSingle` padding an empty array of `Enum` (or of a `Tuple` or `JSON` containing one) with 0 instead of the type default, the lowest member: the query failed with `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM`, `INSERT ... SELECT` stored a value the table could not read back, and an `Enum` whose 0 member is not the lowest returned that member. Such a value computed before the upgrade and stored in a key, skip index, projection or `MATERIALIZED`/`DEFAULT` column keeps the old padding; rebuild it (`ALTER TABLE ... MATERIALIZE INDEX` for a skip index). [#132107](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132107) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `transform()` and `CASE` with expression never matching a `FixedString` input against a shorter `String`/`FixedString` entry (and vice versa): the entry was silently dropped from the lookup table, so the default value was always returned. [#132109](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132109) ([Aathithya Arasu S](https://github.com/Aathi-27)).
* `arrayFirst`/`arrayLast` without a matching element and `arrayMin`/`arrayMax` of an empty array now return the default of an `Enum` element type (its lowest member, also inside a `Tuple` or `JSON`) instead of the value 0, which failed with `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM` when 0 is not a member and was the wrong member when 0 is not the lowest. If a table stores a value computed by these functions in a sorting key, partition key, skip index, projection or `MATERIALIZED`/`DEFAULT` column, rebuild it. [#132114](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132114) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `DROP TABLE ... SYNC` waiting until `max_execution_time`, and `system.detached_parts`, `ATTACH PART`, `ALTER TABLE ... UNFREEZE` and `SYSTEM UNFREEZE` failing with a `filesystem error`, while a `local` disk is broken because its path cannot be resolved (a symlink loop, or a parent directory the server cannot search). [#132118](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132118) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `timeSeriesStddevToGrid` and `timeSeriesStdvarToGrid` (PromQL `stddev_over_time` and `stdvar_over_time`) returning a tiny non-zero value instead of 0 for a constant series, and NaN for values above about 1.34e154. [#132123](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132123) ([Maksim Dergousov](https://github.com/m7kss1)).
* Fixed `clickhouse-keeper-client` and `clickhouse-disks` failing with `CANNOT_CREATE_FILE`, even for `--help` and `--query`, when `$HOME` does not exist or is not writable. The history file is now created only by an interactive session, which continues without history if the file cannot be created, as `clickhouse-client` does. [#132142](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132142) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `URL ... is not allowed in configuration file, see <remote_url_allow_hosts>` error now shows only the host and port of the URL, so secrets in its path or query string are no longer exposed. [#132160](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132160) ([George Larionov](https://github.com/george-larionov)).
* `left`, `right`, `substring`, `byteSlice`, `bitSlice`, `substringIndex` and their UTF8 variants now throw `VALUE_IS_OUT_OF_RANGE_OF_DATA_TYPE` for a `UInt64` offset, length or count of 2^63 or more, instead of reading it as a negative number and counting from the other end of the string. [#132169](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132169) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fixes schema inference for the `Form` format, where the type inferred for a field depended on the fields before it. For example, in `format(Form, 'n=1&a=x&m=2')` the field `m` was inferred as `String` instead of a number. [#132172](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132172) ([Henry Su](https://github.com/hsusul)).
* Fixed formatting of a `TimeSeries` target named `inner`, `engine`, `ttl` or `settings`, which did not parse back to the same query. [#132217](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132217) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed a memory leak of the `timeSeries*ToGrid` aggregate function states when a `GROUP BY` query failed while writing their results, for example on `max_memory_usage`. [#132219](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132219) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `sparkbar` now throws `BAD_ARGUMENTS` when `min_x` or `max_x` does not fit the type of `x`, instead of silently wrapping the bound and drawing a different interval. [#132294](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132294) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fix `conv` accepting a base outside 2..36 when its low 32 bits fall in that range, for example `conv('ff', 4294967312, 10)` returned `255` instead of throwing `BAD_ARGUMENTS`. [#132295](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132295) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fix `addDays`, `addMonths`, `addSeconds` and the other date add/subtract functions treating a constant `UInt64` count above `Int64` max as a negative count when the date is a column. They now throw `DECIMAL_OVERFLOW`, like they already do for a constant date or a column count. Also fix `fromModifiedJulianDay` and `fromModifiedJulianDayOrNull` wrapping such day numbers instead of returning the out-of-range error or NULL. [#132296](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132296) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fix `round`, `floor`, `ceil`, `trunc` and `roundBankers` treating a `UInt64` scale close to 2^64 as a negative scale. Such scales now throw `ARGUMENT_OUT_OF_BOUND` like any other scale outside the `Int16` range. [#132299](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132299) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fix an asynchronous `INSERT ... FROM INFILE` sent directly to the server opening the file on the server before the query was rejected. [#132305](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132305) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Fix a server crash when deserializing a malformed aggregate state that contains a `LowCardinality(String)` value. [#132322](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132322) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fixed `RowBinary` output (and the binary fallback of `Arrow`/`ArrowStream`) with `output_format_binary_write_json_as_string = 1` writing a `JSON` value stored in the shared variant of a `Dynamic` column (for example `Dynamic(max_types=0)`, a `Dynamic` over its `max_types` limit, or an `Array(JSON)` path of a `JSON(max_dynamic_types=0)` column) in the binary object layout instead of as a string, so that reading the output back with `input_format_binary_read_json_as_string = 1` failed with `INCORRECT_DATA`. Closes [#132110](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/132110). [#132326](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132326) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `GRANT` and `REVOKE` of a role are now refused in readonly mode, like granting a privilege. Previously a read-only session (including any HTTP GET request) of a user holding a role `WITH ADMIN OPTION` could grant that role to another user or revoke it from one. Closes [#132005](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/132005). [#132330](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132330) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Error messages and logs that mention an Azure disk configured with `connection_string` now show the blob service URL of the disk instead of its full connection string. [#132335](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132335) ([Arsen Muk](https://github.com/arsenmuk)).
* Fixed `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM` when reading a `Tuple` column holding a `JSON` with an `Enum` typed path (for example `Tuple(j JSON(a Enum8('x' = 1, 'y' = 2)))`) filled with the default of its type (an `INSERT` that omits the column, parts written before `ADD COLUMN`, `CLEAR COLUMN`, an expired column TTL in a Wide part, `defaultValueOfTypeName`). Such a path now holds the `Enum` default (its smallest value) instead of 0. Closes [#132026](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/132026). [#132340](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132340) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An `INSERT` into a `Kafka`, `NATS` or `RabbitMQ` table with a row-based format now fails with `BAD_ARGUMENTS` when `kafka_max_rows_per_message`, `nats_max_rows_per_message` or `rabbitmq_max_rows_per_message` is 0, or when `output_format_avro_rows_in_file = 0` for `Avro`. Before, such an `INSERT` never finished and kept producing empty messages, and with a `_key` column it read out of bounds. `CREATE TABLE` now also rejects `nats_num_consumers` and `rabbitmq_num_consumers` of 0 or above 2147483647 (which failed with an internal assertion or wrapped around) and a `nats_reconnect_wait` above 2147483647. [#132378](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132378) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a crash in the `Arrow` and `ArrowStream` input formats when a requested type changed how a nested field was decoded while another requested column read that field with a type that does not describe it: a struct column together with a dotted subcolumn of it, such as `t String, "t.s" IPv6` (including dictionary-encoded `UUID` children), or an Arrow union with a struct child named like another requested column. [#132416](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132416) ([Nikolai Kochetov](https://github.com/KochetovNicolai)).
* Fixed `readonly` not refusing `ALTER DATABASE` (`MODIFY COMMENT`, `MODIFY SETTING`), `KILL TRANSACTION`, `INSERT INTO FUNCTION` and temporary tables with an engine other than `Memory`, and `allow_ddl = 0` not refusing `ALTER DATABASE`. A read-only session, including any HTTP GET request, could change database metadata, roll back another user's transaction, and write to a file or URL through a table function or a `File` or `URL` temporary table. [#132424](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132424) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `timeSeriesGroupArrayIf` with array arguments skipping rows whose `UInt8` condition is greater than 1. [#132438](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132438) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Fixed the totals row of a `JOIN` with `WITH TOTALS` holding `0` in an `Enum` column, a value the type does not declare, so reading it threw `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM`. It happened when only the other side had totals or an `arrayJoin` left a side's totals empty. Such a column now gets the type default. [#132448](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132448) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Backported in [#133611](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133611): Fixed `LOGICAL_ERROR` `Directory '...' is virtual` on the first `INSERT` into a `Set` or `Join` table whose `disk` setting names a `plain_rewritable` disk, on `ALTER TABLE ... DELETE` of such a `Join` table, and on `clickhouse-disks move` or `link` into a directory that `mkdir --parents` created only as a parent. [#132449](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132449) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A local `REVOKE` of a privilege and `GRANT CURRENT GRANTS` are now refused in readonly mode, like `GRANT` of a privilege. Previously a read-only session (including any HTTP GET request) of a user holding a privilege `WITH GRANT OPTION` could revoke it from another user or pass its own grants to another user. [#132450](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132450) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results and a possible server abort of queries using the query condition cache when a `MergeTree` table was attached again (`DETACH`/`ATTACH TABLE` or `ATTACH DATABASE`) after `TRUNCATE` or `ALTER TABLE ... DROP PARTITION`, so that a new part got the name of a removed one. [#132457](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132457) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Backported in [#133714](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133714): Fix abrupt server termination when MongoDB returns malformed query results. [#132506](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132506) ([János Benjamin Antal](https://github.com/antaljanosbenjamin)).
* Fix the formatting of queries with quoted names that are not plain identifiers. In several statements, for example `ALTER NAMED COLLECTION ... DELETE` and `CREATE DICTIONARY`, the formatted query lost the backquotes and was not parseable. [#132510](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132510) ([János Benjamin Antal](https://github.com/antaljanosbenjamin)).
* Fixed a read-only session (`readonly` above 0, which includes every HTTP GET request) renaming the files it read in `user_files` when `rename_files_after_processing` is set. Such a `SELECT` is now refused with `READONLY`, because renaming the files is a write. [#132517](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132517) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a memory leak in queries answered by `_minmax_count_projection` that compute `min` or `max` of a `Tuple`, `Array` or `FixedString` primary key or partition column: every part read leaked one aggregate state. [#132557](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132557) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `DeltaLake` queries returning fewer rows than the table holds when a data file's min/max statistics are inverted (min greater than max): data skipping skipped such a file in plain reads with `delta_lake_enable_engine_predicate` enabled (the default) and in Change Data Feed reads. Closes [#131644](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/131644). [#132566](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132566) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* With `compatibility` set to 26.4, 26.5 or 26.6, `MergeTree` tables now build `basic, uniq_v2` automatic statistics (the default since 26.7) instead of `minmax, uniq`. This fixes query planning that was many times slower for such tables after an upgrade. [#132600](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132600) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Fixed `RESTORE` failing when a restored object depends on a table that is not in the backup and belongs to a database that reads its data on access, for example a dictionary over a `Filesystem` database table outside `user_files` (`PATH_ACCESS_DENIED`) or in a directory that became a symlink loop, or over a `PostgreSQL` database whose server is down. Closes [#132345](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/132345). [#132650](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132650) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a crash when `automatic_parallel_replicas_mode` switched a query to parallel replicas and a `set` skip index over a column filtered by `IN (subquery)` was applied during the read (`use_skip_indexes_on_data_read`). [#132659](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132659) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Fix reading a table from a multi-table MySQL dump with the `MySQLDump` input format, which failed with `CANNOT_PARSE_QUOTED_STRING` (or `CANNOT_EXTRACT_TABLE_STRUCTURE` during schema inference) when a statement of another table had a quoted name with a space, for example the column list written by `mysqldump --complete-insert`. Closes [#132440](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/132440). [#132660](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132660) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `conv` returning wrong results for a `Bool` or a float first argument. A `Bool` was converted from the text `true`/`false` instead of its value `1`/`0`. A float was converted from its text, so a value printed in exponent form gave a wrong number (`conv(1e21, 16, 10)` returned `7713`, `conv(1e-7, 16, 10)` returned `30`); it now converts as the same number written as a string, and `nan` and `inf` throw `BAD_ARGUMENTS` instead of returning a number. [#132662](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132662) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `BSONEachRow` output writing a nested `Array`, `Tuple` or `Map` with the size of another nested document when their names join into the same dotted path (for example `Map` keys `a` and `a.0` with array values), which made the output unreadable. Closes [#128260](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128260). [#132673](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132673) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An integer number of seconds outside the `Time` range, read by the `Values` and JSON input formats or by a text cast such as `'[4294967297]'::Array(Time)`, is now saturated to `999:59:59` / `-999:59:59`, as `CAST` and `toTime` do by default, instead of wrapping around (`4294967297` was stored as `00:00:01`). [#132703](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132703) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `clickhouse-client` and `clickhouse-local` hide secrets (passwords, keys of `s3`, `remote`, `BACKUP ... TO S3(...)` and other table functions and engines) in the failed query they print after the error in non-interactive mode, as the server does in `system.query_log`, and leave out the inline data of a failed `INSERT`. Closes [#132436](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/132436). [#132705](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132705) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
