* Fix ORC filter push down with bloom filters skipping the rows with `0.0` for `WHERE x = -0.0` and the rows with `-0.0` for `WHERE x = 0.0`. [#132730](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132730) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fix a crash (double free) when an allocation failed, e.g. because of the memory limit, while an aggregate function used as a window function moved to a new partition or frame, or while an aggregate function with the `-ArgMin` / `-ArgMax` combinator reset its state for a new extreme value. [#132755](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132755) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fixed `pow` returning `-inf`, a wrong-signed or a finite garbage value instead of `inf` or `0` when the result is far out of the `Float64` range, for example `pow(10, 1e19)`. [#132795](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132795) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Fixed Arrow Flight `GetSchema` storing an empty result in the query cache for a query with `use_query_cache = 1`, after which the same query returned no rows until the entry expired. [#132822](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132822) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix resolving host names, including `localhost`, on hosts that have no network address other than loopback (for example `docker run --network none` or systemd `PrivateNetwork=yes`): every lookup failed with `Cannot resolve host`. [#132833](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132833) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Backported in [#134064](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/134064): The `CGroupMaxCPU` asynchronous metric now takes the effective cpuset of the cgroup into account, as the minimum of the CPU quota and the number of CPUs in the cpuset. Previously it was not reported for containers with exclusive CPUs and no CFS quota, such as Kubernetes pods with the `static` CPU manager policy (where `cpu.max` is `max` since Kubernetes 1.35), and `CGroupUserTimeNormalized` and `CGroupSystemTimeNormalized` were missing as well. [#132862](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132862) ([dilu-ch](https://github.com/dilu-ch)).
* Backported in [#133797](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133797): Fix high memory usage of `MATERIALIZE INDEX` for text indexes on `packed` or `compact` parts. [#132964](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132964) ([Elmi Ahmadov](https://github.com/ahmadov)).
* Backported in [#133593](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133593): Fixed wrong results of `GROUP BY`, `LIMIT BY` and `ORDER BY` after `GROUP BY` with an injective function and a NULL argument: with `concatAssumeInjective` or `partitionId` and a typed NULL constant or a `Nullable` column, rows where that argument is NULL were split into several groups instead of one; with `bitmaskToArray` or `bitPositionsToArray` of a `Nullable` column, NULL and `0` both map to `[]` but were treated as different keys. [#133115](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/133115) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Backported in [#133613](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133613): Fixed `EXISTS` returning `1` (and `NOT EXISTS` returning `0`) when its subquery returns no rows because an `arrayJoin` in it expands an empty array. [#133172](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/133172) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Backported in [#133693](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133693): Fixed `CREATE TABLE ... AS filesystem(...)` with an explicit column list not checking `READ ON FILE`, which let a user with `WRITE ON FILE` (or with no `FILE` grant at all, via `ON CLUSTER`) read any file under `user_files` through the new table. Such a table now needs `READ` and `WRITE` on `FILE`, the grants that imply `TABLE ENGINE ON File`. This also applies to `CREATE TABLE copy AS src`, short `ATTACH` and local `RESTORE` of such a table. [#133420](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/133420) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Backported in [#133548](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133548): Removed the background task that cleaned up unused directories in `store/`. With `async_load_databases` enabled, it could revoke all access rights from the data directory of an attached table (queries to the table failed with `Permission denied`) and delete the directory 30 days later. It could also delete the data of a table that was recoverable with `ATTACH TABLE ... UUID` after a power loss. Unused directories in `store/` are now left untouched. The server settings `database_catalog_unused_dir_hide_timeout_sec`, `database_catalog_unused_dir_rm_timeout_sec`, and `database_catalog_unused_dir_cleanup_period_sec` are obsolete and have no effect. [#133464](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/133464) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Backported in [#133543](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133543): Fixed JSON schema inference that, depending on memory reuse, could reject valid input with `TYPE_MISMATCH` (for example `{"x":0}`, `{"x":-1}`, `{"y":1}`, `{"y":18446744073709551615}` in `JSONEachRow`) or infer a wrong type such as `Array(Dynamic)` instead of `Array(Nullable(UInt64))`. It also infers `String` instead of failing for JSON strings that mix `Int64` and larger integers with non-numeric text (with `input_format_json_try_infer_numbers_from_strings = 1` and `input_format_json_read_numbers_as_strings = 0`). [#133506](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/133506) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Backported in [#133759](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133759): Fix a crash and incorrect results in the `CapnProto` format when the schema contains groups or named unions declared inline in a struct (`g :group { ... }`, `u :union { ... }`). [#133530](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/133530) ([Nikita Mikhaylov](https://github.com/nikitamikhaylov)).
* Backported in [#133854](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133854): `ATTACH MASKING POLICY` is no longer accepted as a query. It skipped the checks of `CREATE MASKING POLICY`. [#133748](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/133748) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* Backported in [#133885](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133885): The secret first key argument of "keyed" SipHash SQL functions is now masked in `system.query_log`. [#133828](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/133828) ([Robert Schulze](https://github.com/rschu1ze)).

#### Build/Testing/Packaging Improvement
* Build statically linked binaries with `musl` libc for `amd64` and `aarch64`. [#109239](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109239) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Fix `memcpy-bench` CMake so `-fno-tree-loop-distribute-patterns` is applied via `target_compile_options` instead of being treated as a directory-level flag. [#113366](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113366) ([darion-yaphet](https://github.com/darion-yaphet)).
* Release binaries are now packaged with `SYMBOL_SECTIONS=compact`, which replaces `.symtab`/`.strtab` in release binaries (~148 MiB on the official build) with a compact `.clickhouse.symbols` section (~6 MiB) that `SymbolIndex` queries lazily, keeping stack-trace symbolization working with lower memory usage than the full symbol table. [#115323](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115323) ([Andrew Grigorev](https://github.com/ei-grad)).
* Use `clang-23` in CI. [#117346](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117346) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Update `libpqxx` to 8.0.2. [#119327](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119327) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Build the `C` and `C++` code of the ARM binaries with `-mbranch-protection=pac-ret`, which signs the return address so that overwriting it on the stack cannot redirect control flow; the AArch64 `.S` sources that key off `__ARM_FEATURE_PAC_DEFAULT` opt into the same signing. Sanitizer builds are excluded, because the sanitizer runtimes do not strip the signature when they unwind. [#119685](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119685) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Add the `NightlyExtendedPerformance` workflow, which runs every performance test query without sampling, including the new `long` tests `calibration`, `tpch_sf100` and `tpcds_sf100`, on the latest master ARM build every night and compares it with the previous night's build. [#122231](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122231) ([Vitaly Pryakhin](https://github.com/naushniki)).
* Alpine-based `clickhouse-server` and `clickhouse-keeper` images now run `apk upgrade` at build time, so they pick up security fixes for packages that are already in the alpine base image, such as OpenSSL. [#124094](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124094) ([Joel Bandi](https://github.com/joelbandi)).
* Update `simdjson` from v4.6.11 to v5.0.8. [#124651](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124651) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Test reading the entire Arrow test corpus from https://github.com/apache/arrow-testing. [#125134](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125134) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The Stress test runs every `.sql` test over a random protocol: native, native with TLS, HTTP, HTTPS, MySQL, PostgreSQL, gRPC, Arrow Flight, SSH or the Web Terminal. [#125517](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125517) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix linker error on USE_OPENSSL_FIPS=1 builds. [#125548](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125548) ([Craig Ringer](https://github.com/ringerc)).
* Fix building ClickHouse on FreeBSD powerpc64le. [#125856](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125856) ([Piotr Kubaj](https://github.com/pkubaj)).
* The `-falign-loops=32` loop alignment now also applies to ThinLTO builds, including the official release builds. Before, it took effect only in builds without LTO. [#125972](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125972) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The distroless `clickhouse-server` and `clickhouse-keeper` images now open their configuration, data and log directories to other uids, as the Ubuntu and Alpine images do, so they start under a custom user (`docker run --user`). Closes [#125743](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125743). [#126149](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126149) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Enable the `modernize-use-nodiscard` clang-tidy check and add `[[nodiscard]]` to `const` member functions. [#126158](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126158) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The native Parquet reader and writer no longer depend on the Apache Arrow C++ library: a build with Parquet needs only the Thrift-generated Parquet structures. A new CMake option `ENABLE_ARROW_LIBRARY` controls the Arrow C++ library, which is still used by Hive, Arrow Flight, delta-kernel-rs, and the `ParquetMetadata` format. Parquet is now tested in the fast test. [#126477](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126477) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Use the resolved LLVM `objcopy` in the Rust symbol post-processing scripts instead of the `objcopy` detected by CMake. [#126478](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126478) ([oleg-milanov](https://github.com/oleg-milanov)).
* Halved the hash join code in the `clickhouse` binary (21 MiB of `.text` in a release build without LTO, 100 MiB with MemorySanitizer) and reduced the compile time of the hash join sources: the hash join probe loop is no longer instantiated for combinations of clause count, filtering and block layout that can never run, and the build loop is shared between join kinds. [#126852](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126852) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `addBatchSparseSinglePlace` is no longer instantiated for every aggregate function, which removes about 5.5 MiB of code from the MSan build and about 0.8 MiB from a release build. [#126860](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126860) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `NumericIndexedVector` functions take 14.6 MiB less code with MemorySanitizer and 3.3 MiB less in a release build without LTO, and compile faster: vectors with a 64-bit index, which `groupNumericIndexedVector` never accepts, are no longer instantiated, and the ten `numericIndexedVectorPointwise*` functions share one implementation. [#126968](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126968) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `range_hashed` and `complex_key_range_hashed` dictionary lookup is no longer instantiated for every attribute type, which removes about 47 MiB of code from the MSan build and about 6 MiB from a release build. [#126978](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126978) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The loops that handle a `Nullable` divisor are now compiled only into `divide`, `intDiv`, `modulo` and `positiveModulo`, and wide integer argument pairs that are executed through a common type share one copy of that code. This removes about 3.5 MiB of code from the release build. [#127006](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127006) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Printing of pointers in ClickHouse code is now a compile-time error, so memory addresses cannot leak into exception messages and logs. [#127088](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127088) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The bundled OpenSSL no longer runs `OPENSSL_cpuid_setup` from the `.init` section on x86_64; `OPENSSL_init_crypto` runs it instead. [#127216](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127216) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* ASan, TSan and MSan builds work with the statically linked musl toolchain: the sanitizer runtimes bind their interceptors to the libc at link time instead of through `dlsym`. [#127217](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127217) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Link the `musl` build of ClickHouse with packed relative relocations, which makes the binary 34 MB smaller and its cold start faster. [#127256](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127256) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Update `c-ares` from v1.34.8 to v1.34.9. [#127411](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127411) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `croaring` from v5.2.3 to v5.3.2. [#127412](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127412) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `openldap` from 2.6.15 to 2.6.16. [#127413](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127413) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `google-benchmark` from v1.9.5 to v1.10.0. [#127415](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127415) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Add a test for `LowCardinality` columns, including `metric_family`, in the metric families table of a `TimeSeries` table. [#127939](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127939) ([Fabio Vincenzi](https://github.com/fabiovincenzi)).
* Link the executables with `-z noexecstack` and `-z nodlopen`: the stack stays non-executable whichever linker is used, and other programs cannot load the `clickhouse` binary with `dlopen`. [#127981](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127981) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Build with `-fstrict-flex-arrays=3` (except on macOS and in UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer builds), so that only a trailing `[]` member is treated as a flexible array, and `-Warray-bounds` and `__builtin_object_size` know the bounds of every other array. [#128007](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128007) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The Linux `clickhouse` binaries no longer have a `DT_RUNPATH` pointing into the CI build directory (`/ClickHouse/ci/tmp/build/contrib/libllvmlibc-cmake`), so the dynamic loader no longer looks for `libc.so.6` and the other system libraries there first. [#128125](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128125) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Update `croaring` from v5.3.2 to v5.4.0. [#128289](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128289) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `mongo-c-driver` from 2.5.6 to 2.5.7. [#128309](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128309) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `snappy` from 1.2.2 to 1.3.1. [#128311](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128311) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `simdutf` from v9.1.1 to v9.3.3. [#128312](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128312) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `StringZilla` from v5.0.5 to v5.3.2. [#128313](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128313) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `libuv` from v1.52.1 to v1.53.0. [#128314](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128314) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Fix the native macOS build with the macOS 27 SDK. [#128398](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128398) ([Brian Deitte](https://github.com/bdeitte)).
* Fix a crash when collecting a stack trace on a `boost::context` fiber in musl builds: the `libucontext` trampoline now marks the outermost frame, so unwinding stops there. [#128638](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128638) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Build silk fibers and `librseq` in musl builds. [#128639](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128639) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Disable `-Wdisabled-macro-expansion` in musl builds, where the standard streams are self-referential macros. [#128665](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128665) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Use Rust toolchain `nightly-2026-11-02`. [#128815](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128815) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Update `wasmtime` to v50.0.1. [#128834](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128834) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Build the `amd_compat` (non-AVX2 x86-64) and `arm_v80compat` (ARMv8.0) binaries on release and backport branches, not only on master, so LTS releases provide compat binaries for older hardware and AVX2-less VMs. [#129467](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129467) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* Fix a ppc64le bug where the bundled libunwind's `unw_getcontext` left callee-saved vector registers byte-swapped, which could make `clickhouse-server` fail to start intermittently. [#129808](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129808) ([Shichao Jin](https://github.com/jsc0218)).
* Enable Rust and adjust default enabled third-party features when cross-compiling for s390x. [#130059](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130059) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Add boundary and vector-remainder coverage for forward and reverse symbol searches. [#130146](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130146) ([Ilya Yatsishin](https://github.com/qoega)).
* Building with clang/lld 21, the minimum supported compiler, no longer fails at the final `clickhouse` link: `cmake/localize_rust_c_symbols.sh` now removes the `.llvm_addrsig` sections that `objcopy` invalidates, as `cmake/strip_rust_symbols.sh` already does. [#130174](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130174) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A CMake re-run no longer rewrites unchanged generated files (`TimeZones.generated.cpp`, `StorageSystemLicenses.generated.cpp`, the LLVM `.def` headers and the musl `bits/*.h` headers), so it no longer recompiles the code that uses them, and it configures faster: `git status` skips the work trees of submodules and `list-licenses.sh` no longer forks per vendored Rust crate. [#130176](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130176) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Faster incremental builds after editing `Settings.cpp`, `MergeTreeSettings.cpp` or `FormatFactorySettings.h`. [#130179](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130179) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An incremental build with nothing changed no longer re-runs the symbol post-processing of the Rust libraries and no longer relinks `clickhouse`. [#130180](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130180) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Editing `ProtocolDefines.h`, `SettingsEnums.h`, `ActionsDAG.h` or `IQueryPlanStep.h` recompiles fewer translation units (Debug `clickhouse` and `unit_tests_dbms`: 319 -> 145, 2201 -> 1283, 1419 -> 1088 and 802 -> 449 objects), because headers included almost everywhere no longer include them for a few constants, one enum or a type used only by reference. [#130183](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130183) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An edit of `src/IO/ReadHelpers.h` recompiles 1,346 files instead of 2,010, and an edit of `src/DataTypes/Serializations/ISerialization.h` recompiles 2,293 instead of 3,245, because `IO/Operators.h` no longer includes `ReadHelpers.h` and `IDataType.h` no longer includes `ISerialization.h`. [#130218](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130218) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An edit of `src/Formats/FormatSettings.h` recompiles 753 instead of 3,295 translation units, because `ReadHelpers.h`, `WriteHelpers.h`, `SettingsEnums.h`, `Context.h`, `NativeWriter.h` and other widely included headers no longer include it. [#130229](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130229) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The standalone `clickhouse-keeper` binary no longer links the SQL functions, which it never uses: the packaged x86_64 binary is 30% smaller (321 MB instead of 461 MB), and its ThinLTO link in release builds needs a third less CPU time. [#130236](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130236) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add `utils/download-ci-toolchain.sh`, which downloads the Clang that CI compiles with (the same release as apt.llvm.org, optimized with PGO and BOLT) for local builds; it compiles ClickHouse about 20% faster on x86_64. [#130239](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130239) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* ThinLTO release builds link `clickhouse` and `clickhouse-keeper` faster (1.7x to 2.4x in local measurements): C++ translation units without external definitions are now split for ThinLTO instead of being optimized serially as one regular LTO module. [#130240](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130240) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The cargo workspace of the Rust libraries moved from `rust/workspace` to `contrib/rust-contrib-workspace`, and each wrapper crate now lives next to its CMake file in `contrib/<lib>-cmake`. [#130348](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130348) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Update `mongo-cxx-driver` from r4.6.0 to r4.6.2. [#130444](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130444) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Delta-kernel-rs and chdig are built as members of the Rust workspace in `contrib/rust-contrib-workspace`, with one `Cargo.lock`, one cargo target directory and features resolved once for all members, so the crates they share with the other Rust libraries are compiled once, in one parallel cargo run. [#130548](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130548) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* CMake now looks for `ld.lld`, `llvm-ar`, `llvm-objcopy` and the other LLVM tools in the compiler's directory before searching `PATH`, so a build with a self-contained LLVM toolchain uses that toolchain's linker. CI release builds now link with the PGO-optimized `lld` of the CI toolchain, which makes the ThinLTO link of `clickhouse` about 18% faster. [#130659](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130659) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `protoc` is now built with only its C++ generator, so the build compiles 30 instead of 196 `libprotoc` sources and building `protoc` takes about 39% less CPU time. Generated code is unchanged. [#130693](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130693) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The bundled LLVM build now compiles LLVM Support, the TableGen library and the basic TableGen backends once and shares them between `llvm-min-tblgen` and `llvm-tblgen`, so a clean x86 build runs 403 fewer compile jobs and building the TableGen outputs takes about 40% less CPU time. Builds that use only LLVM Support, such as MemorySanitizer builds, no longer build TableGen at all. Generated files are unchanged. [#130700](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130700) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* New CMake option `RUST_BUILD_CACHE_DIR`: when set, Cargo keeps the compiled Rust crates in that directory (one subdirectory per Rust configuration), so a new build directory reuses them instead of compiling all Rust crates again, which ccache cannot cache. Off by default. [#130768](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130768) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `clickhouse-keeper` package no longer contains a separate binary: it depends on `clickhouse-common-static` and installs `clickhouse-keeper` as a symlink to `clickhouse`, which runs Keeper the same way. The `clickhouse-keeper-dbg` package is no longer built; the debug symbols are in `clickhouse-common-static-dbg`. The `BUILD_STANDALONE_KEEPER` CMake option is deprecated. [#130819](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130819) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix the build with `ENABLE_LIBFIU=0`. [#130961](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130961) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The CI toolchain (`clang`, `lld` and the other LLVM tools built by the `OptimizeToolchain` workflow) is now linked with jemalloc, so building ClickHouse with it is about 4-6% faster. The built binaries are unchanged. [#131183](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131183) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Allow cross-compiling Linux binaries on a macOS host. [#131330](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131330) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fix a data race in the embedded LLVM when JIT compilation runs on several threads on AArch64. [#131685](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131685) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* The debug sections of `clickhouse.debug` and `clickhouse-keeper.debug` in the `-dbg` packages are now compressed with zstd (4.3 GB -> 1.3 GB; zlib if the build's `objcopy` lacks zstd), so the `clickhouse-common-static-dbg` rpm stays under the 4 GiB file size limit of the rpm packager and can be installed again. Reading zstd-compressed files with gdb requires gdb 13 or newer built with zstd support. [#131720](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131720) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The CI toolchain's `clang` and `lld` use jemalloc 5.4.0 built from source, with transparent huge pages for the heap and its metadata. [#131745](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131745) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An edit of `src/IO/ByteRangeSet.h` recompiles 9 files instead of 3,187, and an edit of `src/Databases/IDatabase.h` 159 instead of 728, because `ReadBuffer.h`, `IStorage.h`, `Set.h` and `ColumnString.h` no longer include `ByteRangeSet.h`, `IDatabase.h`, `SetVariants.h` and `WriteHelpers.h`. [#131759](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131759) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Build the `C` and `C++` code of the ARM binaries with `-mbranch-protection=pac-ret`, which signs the return address so that overwriting it on the stack cannot redirect control flow. Code compiled for ARMv8.3 and later (SimSIMD) is excluded, so the binary still runs on cores without pointer authentication. Sanitizer builds are excluded, because the sanitizer runtimes do not strip the signature when they unwind. [#131784](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131784) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Add performance coverage for short literal/match sequences and highly compressed strings with both `LZ4` decoders. [#131942](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131942) ([Ilya Yatsishin](https://github.com/qoega)).
* Sign return addresses on ARM also in `SimSIMD` and `compiler-rt`, which were previously excluded. The binary still runs on cores without pointer authentication, such as Graviton2. [#131996](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131996) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Update `miniselect` from 0.4.0 to 0.4.1. [#132023](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132023) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `zxc` from v1.0.0 to v1.0.1. [#132024](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132024) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Fix the illumos build with `ENABLE_LIBRARIES=OFF` by not assuming bzip2 is available to libarchive. [#132587](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132587) ([Kenji Noguchi](https://github.com/knoguchi)).
* Use PGO+BOLT optimized clang toolchain for CI builds. [#132865](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132865) ([clickhouse-gh[bot]](https://github.com/apps/clickhouse-gh)).
* Fix the musl include flags so CLion and other tools that probe compiler flags can load the CMake project. [#132981](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132981) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).

#### NOT FOR CHANGELOG / INSIGNIFICANT

* <!-- CI automatic block start :ci_links: -->. [#81944](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/81944) ([Amos Bird](https://github.com/amosbird)).
* Introducing a new fuzzer check in the ClickHouse CI for my dear friend. [#94148](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/94148) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* Add a regression test for a failed in-place set build with GLOBAL IN. [#102308](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/102308) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Declarative function signatures, continuation of [#3775](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/3775). [#104948](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/104948) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Improvements to keeper-utils analyze-snapshot. [#106270](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/106270) ([Michael Kolupaev](https://github.com/al13n321)).
* Reject quoted type names that reparse as bareword literals. [#109259](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109259) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* release_job: call create_release in-process instead of via subprocess. [#111739](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111739) ([Alexei Fedotov](https://github.com/leshikus)).
* Bumped `contrib/llvm-project` so MemorySanitizer reports keep their stack trace when an asynchronous signal is delivered while the report is being printed. Affects MSan builds only. [#112008](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/112008) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make `03673_columns_description_cache` and `04257_102926_columns_descriptions_cache_nested_eviction` robust to asynchronous part removal, applying the fix [#109816](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/109816) already made to their sibling `04510_shared_part_schema_metadata_eviction`. [#113599](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113599) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* <!-- CI automatic block start :ci_links: -->. [#114166](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114166) ([Alexei Fedotov](https://github.com/leshikus)).
* CI: run the full test suite only when review threads are resolved. [#114729](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114729) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Bump `silk` to the latest `clickhouse-public`. [#114768](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114768) ([clickhouse-gh[bot]](https://github.com/apps/clickhouse-gh)).
* Tag functional tests averaging over 60s under MSan with `no-msan`. [#115050](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115050) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Not required. [#115100](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115100) ([Ilya Yatsishin](https://github.com/qoega)).
* CI: do not fail a job when configuring log export fails. [#115892](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115892) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not for changelog: removes undefined behaviour (a dangling reference read) with no observable behaviour change. [#117010](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117010) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not for changelog. [#118805](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118805) ([cui fliter](https://github.com/cuishuang)).
* Do not count a `ReplicatedMergeTree` replica's read-only state as an error in `system.errors` and `system.error_log` when the asynchronous metrics thread reads its replication delay. Previously a dropped or restarted replica recorded `TABLE_IS_READ_ONLY`, and a replica on static storage recorded `TABLE_IS_PERMANENTLY_READ_ONLY` and logged an error once per `asynchronous_metrics_update_period_s`, with no query having failed. [#119448](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119448) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* AST fuzzer oracle: skip a mismatch only if the data changed between the reads. [#119465](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119465) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add aliases for `allow_experimental` settings, pt. II. [#119949](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119949) ([Robert Schulze](https://github.com/rschu1ze)).
* Add synth io lags to Iceberg perf CI. [#119976](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119976) ([Arsen Muk](https://github.com/arsenmuk)).
* Not applicable — test-only change. [#120210](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120210) ([ClickGap AI Bot](https://github.com/clickgapai)).
* Run the ParallelReplicas test jobs in autopr mode every second time. [#120438](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120438) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Prepare changelog for 26.10. [#120500](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120500) ([clickhouse-gh[bot]](https://github.com/apps/clickhouse-gh)).
* Refactor hash join to give each `ON` disjunct its own `HashJoinTable`. [#120515](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120515) ([Harikrishnan Prabakaran](https://github.com/harikrishnan94)).
* Keep lldb's JIT breakpoint out of the server in clickhouse-test. [#120675](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120675) ([filimonov](https://github.com/filimonov)).
* Not applicable — test-only change. [#120817](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120817) ([ClickGap AI Bot](https://github.com/clickgapai)).
* Not applicable — test-only change. [#120818](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120818) ([ClickGap AI Bot](https://github.com/clickgapai)).
* Added a limited `clang-tidy` check that analyzes only the files a pull request modifies, so that it can run in the merge queue and stop `clang-tidy` breakage from reaching `master`. [#120941](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120941) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).

* Do not finalize the client output format into a canceled buffer. [#126142](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126142) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Mark four slow tests as `no-msan`. [#126150](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126150) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `Broken pipe` in test `05345_robin_hood_large_hash_tables`. [#126151](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126151) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Add `clean-old-worktrees-global` skill. [#126153](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126153) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Joins with `full_sorting_merge` and `partial_merge` on null-safe (`<=>`) nullable keys skip non-matching rows by the key value, as they did before the key was split into `isNull` and value keys. [#126155](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126155) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Lazily replicated `ARRAY JOIN` passenger columns hold only the rows of their output block again, so materialized views, `max_result_bytes` and squashing size `ARRAY JOIN` output correctly. [#126160](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126160) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky `04625_hash_table_sizes_stats_table_expression_modifiers` under tiny `index_granularity`. [#126168](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126168) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Upgrade check: before the server is upgraded, drop the row policies left by the stress phase whose filter cannot be applied to a table they cover, so they can no longer break a background read on the upgraded server. The other row policies stay and are carried across the upgrade. [#126169](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126169) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#126174](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126174) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Keep the filesystem cache enabled in the AutoPR tests that scan `test.hits`. [#126175](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126175) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Do not correct the global memory tracker below jemalloc `stats.allocated`. [#126176](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126176) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Tolerate short network stalls in `test_aggregate_function_state_tuple_return_type`. [#126177](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126177) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The tests `03047_on_fly_mutations_multiple_updates`, `03047_on_fly_mutations_basics` and `03047_on_fly_mutations_basics_rmt` now sum `FileOpen`/`S3GetObject` over the initial query and the parallel replicas queries it starts, instead of reading only the initiator's `system.query_log` row. [#126181](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126181) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test 05332_autopr_min_bytes_per_replica_events. [#126189](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126189) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not applicable — test-only change. [#126193](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126193) ([ClickGap AI Bot](https://github.com/clickgapai)).
* Remove `enable_analyzer` and `allow_experimental_analyzer` from tests. [#126194](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126194) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* A praktika job that requires a glob artifact (`*.rpm`, `*.deb`, `*64.tgz*`, `*.profdata`, fuzzers) now downloads only the files of the provider's last upload. [#126197](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126197) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not applicable — test-only change. [#126198](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126198) ([ClickGap AI Bot](https://github.com/clickgapai)).
* Mark `05339_ast_fuzzer_oracle_distributed_plan_read_cap` as `no-msan`. [#126199](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126199) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `FunctionsStress.stress` frees the ASan fake-stack frames that exceptions leave behind in its worker threads, so `range` iterations no longer stall in the ASan build. [#126210](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126210) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* fix: Keep full_sorting_merge for ASOF joins when the runtime filter would force hash. [#126211](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126211) ([Antonio Álvarez Caballero](https://github.com/29antonioac)).
* Not required (CI fix). [#126212](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126212) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* CI: bound the queries to the CI Logs cluster in the log-export setup by wall clock. [#126217](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126217) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An HTTP response with a status above 511 no longer produces an out-of-range `HTTPStatus` value. [#126225](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126225) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Do not report LLVM coverage jobs as failed because of failed tests. [#126227](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126227) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* With `distributed_plan_join_runtime_filters`, a runtime filter that is no longer needed by its receiver no longer produces `EXCHANGE_PEER_DISCONNECTED` errors in `system.errors` and in the log. [#126228](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126228) ([Alexander Gololobov](https://github.com/davenger)).
* Avoid quadratic argument matching in declarative function signatures. [#126235](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126235) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Rename integer division setting to sql_compatible_division_for_integers. [#126245](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126245) ([Ilya Yatsishin](https://github.com/qoega)).
* Not required. [#126252](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126252) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Revert "SLRU filesystem cache: promote partially downloaded file segments". [#126265](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126265) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Fix the build of `unit_tests_dbms` after the change of `StackTrace::tryResolveAddress`. [#126269](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126269) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Extract parts of the `MergeTree` index analysis into reusable helpers. [#126283](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126283) ([Antonio Filipovic](https://github.com/antoniofilipovic)).
* Pin the old server image of `test_custom_s3_disk_remote_url_allow_hosts` to `26.8.26.12`. [#126289](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126289) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* CI: move build jobs from large (m-type) to medium-cpu (c-type) runners. [#126294](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126294) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* This patch concludes the refactoring chain of the window transform execution model. [#126295](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126295) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* Not required. [#126306](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126306) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Remove the circular dependency between `clickhouse/cctools` and `clickhouse/fasttest`. [#126309](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126309) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* The `bedrock-openai` provider of `praktika review` now waits up to 600 s for a Converse response and retries a failed request once instead of four times. [#126311](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126311) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Praktika: pluggable info_extractor for Result and native C/C++ build error extractor. [#126313](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126313) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* Decline automatic parallel replicas when the coordinated read gets another read's analysis. [#126318](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126318) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Not required. [#126345](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126345) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Do not build `prql`, `polyglot` and `skim` with MSan. [#126354](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126354) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Replace broken links in source code comments. [#126357](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126357) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Add a style check for broken links in the PR diff. [#126358](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126358) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Keep the `sliceContains` block loop independent of the first-vector test on AArch64. [#126371](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126371) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Follow-up to the per-part statistics cache: weight of composite fields and structure of cloned statistics. [#126389](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126389) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix flaky test `02789_filesystem_cache_alignment` under concurrency control. [#126391](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126391) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Rerun the tests `master` changed since the PR CI in the merge queue. [#126406](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126406) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* clickhouse-test: wake on test exit instead of polling. [#126488](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126488) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Not required. [#126492](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126492) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#126495](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126495) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `insert_statistics_uniq_v2` performance test now merges each `t_materialize_uniq_v2_*` table into one part before measuring `MATERIALIZE STATISTICS`, so both compared servers mutate the same layout. [#126505](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126505) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add a performance test for queries that run under a CPU workload. [#126515](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126515) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Apply `-WithMemoryTracking` containers to `src/Processors/Formats`. [#126530](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126530) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Skip `/proc/cpuinfo` lines that do not parse in `CPUFrequencyMHz` instead of throwing. [#126540](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126540) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The stateless flaky check and targeted check pass their selected tests to `clickhouse-test` through a file, so a PR that changes thousands of tests no longer fails these jobs with `Argument list too long`. [#126545](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126545) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The bound that `optimize_and_compare_chain` derives within one table (for example `indexHint(l.a < 1000)` from `l.a < l.b AND l.b < 1000`) prunes the primary key again in `RIGHT JOIN ... ON` and in a subquery nested in an outer `JOIN ... ON`. [#126551](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126551) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make the integration test `test_https_replication/test.py::test_replication_after_partition` wait for the replication queue with `SYSTEM SYNC REPLICA` instead of polling for a fixed time. [#126552](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126552) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Remove no-parallel tag for some tests. [#126555](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126555) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* clickhouse-test: keep the fatal logs out of the suite directory. [#126556](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126556) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* `SELECT ... FINAL` with a skip index and `use_skip_indexes_if_final_exact_mode` no longer spends time quadratic in the number of parts when the parts have disjoint primary key ranges. [#126575](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126575) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Keep 64-row granules in test 05316_top_k_primary_key_skip_query_condition_cache. [#126583](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126583) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Retry throttled AWS calls in the Jepsen job for up to 3 minutes. [#126585](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126585) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Keep the set of `IN` in memory in `02844_subquery_timeout_with_break`. [#126623](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126623) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#126626](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126626) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Use helpers to remove special column representations. [#126639](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126639) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* A query with `make_distributed_plan = 1` on a server that configures no stateless workers is refused with `SUPPORT_IS_DISABLED` again, instead of `NO_ELEMENTS_IN_CONFIG` with an empty cluster list. [#126660](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126660) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make the stateless test `05406_hash_table_stats_key_group_cap` faster: it reads at most 74 granules per scan and aggregates 6e5 groups instead of 1.2e6. [#126661](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126661) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make `test_s3_aws_sdk_has_slightly_unreliable_behaviour` independent of how the server binary symbolizes stack traces. [#126665](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126665) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Keep the set of `IN` in memory in `00673_subquery_prepared_set_performance`. [#126681](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126681) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required for this category. [#126693](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126693) ([zhao-xiaogang](https://github.com/zhao-xiaogang)).
* Stop the AST fuzzer oracles from reporting false mismatches on `JSONDynamicPaths`, `byteSize` and the other functions whose result depends on how a `JSON` or `Dynamic` value is stored. [#126703](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126703) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#126707](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126707) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Report and gate the relocation headroom of `Build (amd_msan)`. [#126736](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126736) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a spurious `TOO_MANY_ROWS` error with `parallel_replicas_plan_based`, where naming the plan fragment sent to a replica ran index analysis and enforced `max_rows_to_read` against a query that reads nothing, such as a `count()` answered from partition metadata. [#126740](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126740) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Remove object addresses from NATS, io_uring and HDFS messages. [#126741](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126741) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fix flaky `test_rabbitmq_reconnect_drops_stale_deliveries`: do not wait 300 s for a hung `rabbitmqctl` call. [#126746](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126746) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Remove object addresses from logical error messages. [#126748](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126748) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Pin the view assertions that read the shipped SQL. [#126750](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126750) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Build `prql`, `polyglot` and `skim` with MSan again at Rust `opt-level = 1`. [#126751](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126751) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the Docker image tests of a bug fix PR on the master-built `head` images. [#126755](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126755) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Drop benchmark alignment flags and enable function/data sections in sanitizer builds. [#126771](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126771) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A targeted stateless or integration job now retries the GitHub API reads of its test selection for up to 3 minutes and is then skipped, not errored, when GitHub keeps answering with a server error, a network error, a rate limit, or a diff that is still being generated. [#126783](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126783) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix dangling `string_view` keys in `RowInputMissingColumnsFiller`. [#126784](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126784) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Pin the blocks marshalling assertions that name the query-based reading step. [#126793](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126793) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Remove the stale `format_version` assertion from `IcebergMetadata::getHistory`. [#126801](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126801) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Disable the uncompressed and columns caches in the retries of `04328_reader_executor_kpi_async_metric`. [#126830](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126830) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Port cloud and distributed cache options of `clickhouse-test` from downstream. [#126842](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126842) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Compile x86-64 MSan builds with `-O2` instead of `-O3`. [#126863](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126863) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Handle part storage type changes in mutations. [#126865](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126865) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Port masking-policy gate and Keeper component propagation in `MergeTreeDataSelectExecutor`. [#126866](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126866) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Upgrade check: judge a row policy only on tables that can be read without it. [#126895](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126895) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The settings-changes history records the default `max_bytes_ratio_before_external_set = 0.5`, `max_bytes_for_external_set_bloom_filter` and `max_bytes_for_external_set_cache` under 26.10, the version that first ships them, so `compatibility = '26.10'` keeps the spilling of `IN` sets to disk and its optimizations enabled. [#126919](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126919) ([Nihal Z. Miaji](https://github.com/nihalzp)).
* Revert "Apply primary key pruning before read-time skip indexes on parallel replicas followers". [#126955](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126955) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Build the `silk` bump on the `master` tip fetched at job start. [#126956](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126956) ([Miсhael Stetsyuk](https://github.com/mstetsyuk)).

* Do not merge-sort the streams of an all-constant full sort fed by a fan-out. [#126992](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126992) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make 03560, 04277 and 04371 independent of the parallel replicas implementation. [#126997](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/126997) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Fix flaky tests `02317_distinct_in_order_optimization` and `02733_fix_distinct_in_order_bug_49622` under parallel replicas. [#127002](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127002) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add a test for `PREWHERE` steps computing two columns with one name. [#127016](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127016) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add a test for the row counts of a multi-statement PostgreSQL query. [#127027](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127027) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `02010_lc_native` test now keeps its table on the local disk and takes its socket timeout from the test timeout instead of a fixed 30 s. [#127034](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127034) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `Upgrade check` CI job now gives the upgraded server the same randomized Keeper feature flags as the previous-release server, instead of drawing them again. [#127041](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127041) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).

* Stop the AST fuzzer oracles from reporting false mismatches when a query compares a `Dynamic` column with a non-`Dynamic` one in a join. [#127078](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127078) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Publish stripped illumos binaries in GitHub releases. [#127080](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127080) ([Joshua Carp](https://github.com/jmcarp)).

* Not required. [#127158](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127158) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).

* Add a test for duplicate groups of a `LowCardinality` key after a parallel hash join. [#127171](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127171) ([Christoph Wurm](https://github.com/cwurm)).
* Upgrade check: do not report alias rows as new settings. [#127183](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127183) ([Smita Kulkarni](https://github.com/SmitaRKulkarni)).
* Test-only change, no changelog entry required. [#127186](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127186) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Judge `Storage memory check` release scenarios by the memory left live. [#127191](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127191) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Sync private. [#127194](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127194) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* IAST: Fix invalid frees by saving the allocation address. [#127199](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127199) ([bbrown-oai](https://github.com/bbrown-oai)).
* Fix flaky test `05407_map_buckets_zero_key_read_volume`. [#127222](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127222) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `UNKNOWN_POLICY` when restoring into an existing table. [#127244](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127244) ([Julia Kartseva](https://github.com/jkartseva)).
* Follow-up to radix partitioned `uniqExact`: spilling and fixes. [#127252](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127252) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* AST fuzzer oracle: skip `SEMI` joins like `ANY` joins. [#127260](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127260) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Do not let cancellation interrupt the rollback of a merge or mutation. [#127263](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127263) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix flaky `test_when_s3_timeout_at_listing`: write the setup objects without parallel formatting. [#127278](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127278) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Destroy window function states when the `WindowTransform` constructor throws. [#127279](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127279) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Iceberg REST Catalog: Improve location validation. [#127285](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127285) ([Asya Shneerson](https://github.com/asya-ch)).
* Fix flaky test 05407_merge_tree_max_rows_to_use_cache_max_value with parallel replicas. [#127290](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127290) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Mark `02372_analyzer_join` as `no-msan`. [#127297](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127297) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Attach the datasets of the performance tests to Atomic databases. [#127308](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127308) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Add tests for numeric conversion to `DateTime64` / `Time64` at range boundaries. [#127309](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127309) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix flaky test 03008_s3_plain_rewritable_fault. [#127320](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127320) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make 04666_run_query_in_background independent of dictionaries_lazy_load. [#127326](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127326) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required (test-only change). [#127333](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127333) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Stop the AST fuzzer oracles from reporting false mismatches for queries that read a `WITH TOTALS` result through a subquery, a CTE, a JOIN or a view. [#127335](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127335) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Perf CI: find the performance reference build past master commits that have no MasterCI run. [#127365](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127365) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Rotate apt mirrors in server and keeper image builds, including the release build. [#127376](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127376) ([Alexei Fedotov](https://github.com/leshikus)).
* Queries #5 and #9 of the `iceberg_suite_local_rw_features` and `iceberg_suite_s3_rw_features` performance tests no longer rewrite the same rows on every measured run, so their timings no longer drift upward within a run series. [#127380](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127380) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required: CI-only change. [#127387](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127387) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make `04413_documentation_no_indented_lines` linear in the number of lines. [#127390](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127390) ([Christoph Viebig](https://github.com/cv4g)).
* Pin the parallel replicas checks that only the query-based implementation passes. [#127396](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127396) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* CI: remove clickhousectl upload workflow. [#127404](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127404) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* Changed syntax for query argument in elasticsearch table engine/function. [#127428](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127428) ([Kirill Kopnev](https://github.com/Fgrtue)).
* Fix a heap-use-after-free when the filesystem cache reused the remote reader of a finished query that ran under a workload with an IO resource. [#127429](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127429) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Upgrade check: retry the GitHub releases page when it comes back short. [#127433](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127433) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix two regressions of the DPsub conflict detector: deferred conjunct placement and default-padded null rejection. [#127440](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127440) ([Fisnik Kastrati](https://github.com/fkastrati)).
* Check privileges of functions when they are built, not in their creators. [#127443](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127443) ([Nikolai Kochetov](https://github.com/KochetovNicolai)).
* praktika: fix "workaround" in a comment. [#127451](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127451) ([mikakot](https://github.com/mikakot)).
* The `SZ3` codec compresses a block losslessly when its absolute error bound is below the smallest normal `Float64`, instead of hitting an assertion in debug builds. [#127454](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127454) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Keep the sort of the `05331_parquet_prewhere_dictionary_values_once` fixtures in memory. [#127455](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127455) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Bump jemalloc to include ClickHouse/jemalloc#16 (consmetic). [#127476](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127476) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Move the Paimon integration tests from the private repository. [#127477](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127477) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Test-only change, no changelog entry required. [#127481](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127481) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* praktika: minor comment fixes in runner.py. [#127485](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127485) ([mikakot](https://github.com/mikakot)).
* The test `05401_autopr_set_transplant_unpaired_sets` now keeps the randomized `index_granularity` and `index_granularity_bytes` at their defaults or above, so it no longer times out when the runner draws tiny granules. [#127515](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127515) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Bugfix validation now downloads the master binary of the PR's own master parent chain, so it is never newer than the test configs it is started with. [#127524](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127524) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* [needs human] Add 7 tests for coverage gaps in 6 components. [#127528](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127528) ([ClickGap AI Bot](https://github.com/clickgapai)).
* Not required. [#127560](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127560) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky 05346_system_connections_http_keep_alive: another connection can have the same client port. [#127562](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127562) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add 26 `long` performance tests to `NightlyExtendedPerformance`. [#127564](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127564) ([Vitaly Pryakhin](https://github.com/naushniki)).
* The test `04505_json_object_hash_split_invariance` now checks the grace hash join spill over the initial query and the parallel replicas queries it starts, using `spilled_to_disk` of `system.query_log`, instead of the byte counters of the initiator's row only. [#127565](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127565) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Correct the global memory tracker from RSS on illumos. [#127616](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127616) ([Joshua Carp](https://github.com/jmcarp)).
* Write one part per INSERT in 03518_alter_logical_race and re-enable merges. [#127626](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127626) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make the `ANY LEFT JOIN` check in `05224_null_safe_join_fixed_keys` independent of the picked row. [#127631](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127631) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* darwin fast test: enable the job in PRs again and skip five tests failing on macOS. [#127646](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127646) ([Alexei Fedotov](https://github.com/leshikus)).
* [UpSync]: Sync critical files. [#127654](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127654) ([clickhouse-cloud-ci[bot]](https://github.com/apps/clickhouse-cloud-ci)).
* Index analysis names the cached results of monotonic key functions by a number assigned when the condition is built, instead of by the function address. [#127658](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127658) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test `05294_drop_table_cancels_vector_index_merge`. [#127662](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127662) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Do not allocate per-resource scheduling state for a query whose workload is defined for no resource, which removes a UBSan `alignment` report on UBSan-only builds. [#127664](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127664) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Inject the Keeper failure at commit in test_kafka_no_holes_when_write_suffix_failed. [#127666](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127666) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Stop the AST fuzzer oracles from reporting false mismatches when the fuzzer turns a reference to an alias into a `{name:Identifier}` query parameter. [#127668](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127668) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky integration test `test_merge_tree_s3/test.py::test_s3_disk_apply_new_settings` in the `db disk` flavour. [#127679](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127679) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Keep `use_query_condition_cache_for_time_conditions` experimental and disabled on master, as in 26.10. [#127687](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127687) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make the `parallel` case of `05238_workload_memory_spilling_distinct` independent of block scheduling. [#127691](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127691) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Query #0 of the `insert_sequential_and_background_merges` performance test no longer slows down with every measured run, because its table now removes the merged parts right away. [#127692](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127692) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `file_table_function` performance test writes to `/dev/null`, so its measured runs no longer depend on the data earlier runs appended to the same file. [#127693](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127693) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make `05355_top_k_dynamic_filter_single_sort_column_ties` non-parallel. [#127694](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127694) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `number_formatting_formats` performance test writes the output of queries #0-#11 to `/dev/null`, so these queries time the formatting instead of the runner's disk. [#127695](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127695) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `polymorphic_parts_s`, `polymorphic_parts_m` and `polymorphic_parts_l` performance tests set `max_insert_threads = 1`, so each `INSERT` writes its parts in one thread, as it did before 26.8. [#127696](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127696) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `aggregation_in_order_if_null_map` performance test runs its queries with `max_threads = 1`, so its timings no longer depend on how the threads of the multi-stream in-order aggregation interleave. [#127697](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127697) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make `05229_parquet_v3_dictionary_filter_after_bloom` and `05325_parquet_v3_dictionary_filter_after_bloom_disjunction` measure the Parquet reader even when the query condition cache would skip the file. [#127698](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127698) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `codecs_int_insert` performance test inserts 5M rows per query instead of 20M, so the whole test writes 4x less data and stays below the write volume after which its runs started to wait for the disk on CI. [#127700](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127700) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The forced-spill queries #1, #4 and #7 of the `external_distinct_header_constants` performance test use a 4 MiB `max_bytes_before_external_distinct` instead of 16 MiB, so every run spills the same way and their timings are no longer bimodal. [#127705](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127705) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test_keeper_https_control_standalone_cluster: wait for a leader and a follower. [#127712](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127712) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Stop the upgrade check from failing on the TTL merge that `05073_column_ttl_recompute_materialized` makes fail on purpose. [#127731](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127731) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix the flaky integration test `test_scheduler_cached_disk/test.py::test_takeover_after_query_end`. [#127739](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127739) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The test `test_azure_blob_storage_plain_rewritable` no longer leaves `__meta/snapshot.bin` behind: the second disk it creates on the location of `node` has `metadata_snapshot_enabled = 0`, and its generated disk config is named per xdist worker. [#127744](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127744) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Cover hive partition columns in the TopK query condition cache key of `file`. [#127745](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127745) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a SIGSEGV in UBSan builds when `match`, `extract`, `extractAll` or `replaceRegexpOne/All` call the JIT-compiled regexp matcher. Release builds are not affected. [#127750](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127750) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Case 6 of the `04633_async_insert_select_alter_race` test now keeps its tables on the local disk, so a filesystem cache can no longer push its view creation past the lock timeout. [#127751](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127751) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `SYSTEM SYNC MERGES`: report `TIMEOUT_EXCEEDED` when `CancellationChecker` stops it. [#127759](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127759) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix flaky test `02346_text_index_phrase_search_cursors`. [#127760](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127760) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky `05045_create_token` when a token deadline falls into the hour repeated at the end of DST. [#127762](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127762) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky `05315_plain_rewritable_part_removal_without_copies` in the hour repeated at the end of DST. [#127763](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127763) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The SQLancer++ check no longer loses a worker thread to a `ConcurrentModificationException` in its own feedback statistics, which made the `FUZZING` oracle fail with no finding. [#127773](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127773) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make test_parking_fires_on_idle_worker actually sleep. [#127780](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127780) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix the flaky test `05218_text_index_substreams_opened_lazily`, which failed with a randomized `use_columns_cache = 1` since the columns cache also serves compact parts. [#127781](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127781) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Do not log the size of a set that the in-place set-build failpoint abandons. [#127786](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127786) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky `test_writes_split_offsets_read_by_spark`. [#127787](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127787) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test `test_limited_replicated_fetches`. [#127794](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127794) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `Log`, `TinyLog` and `Buffer` tables now refuse a column with the experimental `Quantized` codec with `ILLEGAL_COLUMN`. [#127795](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127795) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test `05055_format_schema_source_query_background_access` when the session date is ahead of the server date. [#127797](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127797) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `05441_iceberg_more_manifests_than_read_ahead` no longer runs in the MSan stateless test jobs. [#127798](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127798) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add 9 tests for coverage gaps in 7 components. [#127806](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127806) ([ClickGap AI Bot](https://github.com/clickgapai)).
* Revert "Avoid building stream names and substreams cache keys for every block in MergeTreeReaderWide". [#127836](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127836) ([Pavel Kruglov](https://github.com/Avogar)).
* Raise the memory limit of `05449_radix_uniq_exact_spill_bounded_sets`. [#127837](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127837) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fix refreshes of refreshable materialized views whose inner table declares subcolumn codecs. [#127839](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127839) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* CI-only change, no changelog entry required. [#127861](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127861) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Test-only change, no changelog entry required. [#127865](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127865) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix the flaky test `03254_prewarm_mark_cache_rmt`, which failed with a randomized `use_columns_cache = 1` since the columns cache also serves compact parts. [#127866](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127866) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `LOGICAL_ERROR` reading `SHARED` paths of dynamic types of a `JSON` column with the columns cache. [#127867](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127867) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Raise the lower bound of the randomized `merge_max_block_size` in the `03209_json_type_horizontal_merges` and `03209_json_type_vertical_merges` tests to 100. [#127877](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127877) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Split the coverage queries of the test selection by file. [#127879](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127879) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `LOGICAL_ERROR` reading a `JSON` or `Dynamic` column with its subcolumn from a compact part with the columns cache. [#127881](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127881) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Remove the FIFO-backed SYSTEM RELOAD DICTIONARY case from 04666_run_query_in_background. [#127900](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127900) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
