* Fix stack-use-after-scope in `MergeTreeData::loadStatisticsOfPartsInParallel` when scheduling a loader job throws. [#127911](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127911) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix the flaky test `05304_json_shared_typed_paths_replication_backup`, which read replica `p2` before it had fetched the part inserted into `p1`. [#127914](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127914) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* [UpSync]: Sync critical files. [#127915](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127915) ([clickhouse-cloud-ci[bot]](https://github.com/apps/clickhouse-cloud-ci)).
* Fix rare failures of the tests `04105_system_pause_view` and `04319_system_stop_all_background` where the `SYSTEM STOP` or `SYSTEM CANCEL` of a refresh reached the server after that refresh had already finished. [#127917](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127917) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a crash in `INSERT ... RETURNING` when the `RETURNING` query reads the inserted table through the `merge` table function, a `Merge` table or `remote`. [#127919](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127919) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test `05227_mutate_part_ahead_of_table_metadata_version_mergetree`. [#127925](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127925) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run `04117_detach_database_kill_query` sequentially again. [#127935](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127935) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Add a test for auto-converted `url` into a `remote()` destination. [#127943](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127943) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Fix flaky `04249_min_allocation_size_to_throw_on_memory_limit`. [#127979](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127979) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Not required. [#127985](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127985) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Throttle the GLOBAL IN subquery of `02916_glogal_in_cancel` and run it on macOS again. [#127986](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127986) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a data race on the exit code of the embedded client in the SSH server. [#127990](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127990) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Overlap the slow CREATE AS SELECT cases of 04666_run_query_in_background and run it on macOS again. [#127994](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/127994) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test `test_admission_timeout_shared_budget_across_slot_and_memory`: time the waiter on the server. [#128010](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128010) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The test `05020_top_k_through_array_join_explain` now keeps the randomized `index_granularity` and `index_granularity_bytes` at 100 rows / 100000 bytes or above, so its single 100-row part always has one granule. [#128015](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128015) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Do not share the output buffer of a gRPC call with its fatal error callback. [#128018](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128018) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `clickhouse-local` and the embedded client no longer call the client's progress handler from pipeline threads. [#128019](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128019) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required: CI-only change. [#128022](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128022) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test `04813_gcd_codec_hash_signedness` with small `index_granularity_bytes`. [#128024](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128024) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Not required. [#128027](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128027) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix the flaky test `05227_system_tables_filter_by_in`, whose `replication_queue` check could read the queue of `t_rep` before and after a background thread removed its only entry. [#128054](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128054) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* CI: "Build profile diff" is green when the read-only CI logs cluster rejects the readiness probe for load. [#128067](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128067) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Rename `USE_MULTITARGET_CODE` to `USE_X86_64_MULTITARGET_CODE`. [#128073](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128073) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Do not let a derived `indexHint` keep `HAVING` above `GROUPING SETS`. [#128077](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128077) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Test-only change, no changelog entry required. [#128080](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128080) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make the remembered verdict of `05324_adaptive_aggregator_disable_thaw` independent of how `numbers_mt` splits rows. [#128084](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128084) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `05397_clickhouse_local_locked_path_untouched` is skipped on macOS, where the util-linux `flock` command it uses is not available. [#128088](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128088) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#128101](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128101) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* [needs human] Add 6 tests for coverage gaps in 6 components. [#128103](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128103) ([ClickGap AI Bot](https://github.com/clickgapai)).
* <!-- CI automatic block start :ci_links: -->. [#128118](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128118) ([Alexei Fedotov](https://github.com/leshikus)).
* Take `what()` as `std::string_view` in `LOG_IMPL` exception handlers. [#128119](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128119) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make `03354_pr_disable_for_multiple_tables_query` work with plan-based parallel replicas. [#128142](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128142) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Not required. [#128144](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128144) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Bound the `system.text_log` scans of `05161_blob_killer_on_cached_plain_disk`. [#128148](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128148) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Tag four `json_shared_typed_paths` tests with `no-msan`. [#128150](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128150) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Bring 04666_run_query_in_background under 10 s by overlapping its CREATE AS SELECT populates with the other cases. [#128161](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128161) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Exclude 23 tests that take more than 10 s from the fast test. [#128162](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128162) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Use a private home directory in tests that run `clickhouse` as `clickhouse-local`. [#128197](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128197) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The `05442_mergetree_part_write_cancellation` test now keeps its table on the local disk, so a filesystem cache lock can no longer delay its `KILL QUERY` past the time bound. [#128200](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128200) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `05449_radix_uniq_exact_spill_bounded_sets` is skipped when temporary data goes to the distributed cache. [#128201](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128201) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Iceberg REST Catalog: Add warehouse support (part 1). [#128210](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128210) ([Asya Shneerson](https://github.com/asya-ch)).
* I messed up Nullable and LowCardinality in MongoDB integration, plus replaying some settings. CI failure: https://s3.amazonaws.com/clickhouse-test-reports/praktika.html?PR=94148&sha=779fbaadfdb18e79f0fbb334c4e79402b4309a93&name_0=PR&name_1=La+Casa+Del+Dolor+%28arm_asan_ubsan%29. [#128212](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128212) ([Pedro Ferreira](https://github.com/PedroTadim)).
* Use a private home directory in test `02203_shebang`. [#128230](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128230) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Perf CI: end the dashboard wait before the job timeout. [#128236](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128236) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Only drain the rest of the result after a local format error in the client. [#128238](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128238) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Leave the row estimate of a filtered array join unknown in join reordering. [#128248](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128248) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required: CI-only change. [#128255](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128255) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the performance tests on Iceberg tables. [#128261](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128261) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Fix `StringValueFilter` turning itself off when several threads update its statistics at once. [#128277](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128277) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* ### Description. [#128284](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128284) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Build profile diff: celebrate a significantly smaller binary. [#128288](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128288) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Add upgrade and mixed-version tests for data lake objects. [#128294](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128294) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Add a differential fuzzer for data lake tables written by foreign tools. [#128295](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128295) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Add resource soak tests for data lake catalogs and Delta Lake writes. [#128296](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128296) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Add a corpus of Parquet files and Iceberg tables from many writers. [#128305](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128305) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Show hardware counters for changed queries in the perf report. [#128316](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128316) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Show the thread stack alignment of both servers in the perf report. [#128320](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128320) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* The integration test harness waits until nodes it stopped or killed are no longer listed as running, so a following `docker compose start` no longer skips one of them. [#128323](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128323) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Perf CI: without a dashboard verdict, do not fail a PR shard on a slowdown other PRs had as well. [#128353](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128353) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test `test_s3_disk_connections_soft_limit`. [#128355](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128355) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required: CI-only change. [#128362](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128362) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required: changes only the agent instructions in `.claude/`. [#128365](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128365) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Read the input of the `Variant` and `Dynamic` serialization fuzzers as untrusted `Native` data. [#128367](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128367) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `clickhouse_fuzzer` no longer reports a false timeout after printing the runner thread's stack while no input is running. [#128377](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128377) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky 03223_nested_json_in_shared_data_merges: order its SELECTs. [#128385](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128385) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `prewhere_subcolumns` performance test now gives every server the same part layout, so its `where n.null` query no longer reports a false regression. [#128387](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128387) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add CLAUDE.md guidance on attributing issues to the causing PR or affected versions. [#128390](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128390) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Symbolize the runner stack of `clickhouse_fuzzer` outside its signal handler. [#128407](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128407) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Disable per-input leak detection in `clickhouse_fuzzer`. [#128408](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128408) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Add a stateless tests run with plan-based parallel replicas. [#128413](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128413) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Refuse a table function on the right of a `RIGHT JOIN` for parallel replicas when `additional_table_filters` is set. [#128443](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128443) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Vortex reads no longer stall for minutes under ThreadSanitizer when many threads contend for a scan's channel. [#128451](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128451) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Praktika: take the changed files of a push from every commit it added, not only from its head. [#128453](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128453) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Restore the Parquet writer round trips dropped from 05478 as a separate test. [#128468](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128468) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Cut Iceberg data files when they are smaller than a Parquet row group. [#128471](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128471) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix flaky `00155_long_merges` when its inserts straddle the first day of a month. [#128477](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128477) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add 7 tests for coverage gaps in 6 components. [#128478](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128478) ([ClickGap AI Bot](https://github.com/clickgapai)).
* Re-approve fork PR workflows in the continue-pr skill. [#128479](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128479) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The `Storage memory check` scenarios insert a fixed time instead of `now()`, so the check no longer fails unrelated PRs at the start of every month. [#128485](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128485) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Perf CI: end the confirmation reruns before the job timeout. [#128491](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128491) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix the test `05474_parquet_corpus_writers` on master after [#128336](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/128336). [#128494](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128494) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* ### Description. [#128496](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128496) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run EXPLAIN checks w/o parallel replicas (query-based). [#128527](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128527) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Not required. [#128531](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128531) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Do not log all mark ranges in `MergeTreePrefetchedReadPool`. [#128540](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128540) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix the flaky test `05453_webterminal_close_during_insert` when it runs in parallel with other tests. [#128545](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128545) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Enable the data lake fuzzer checks of fixed issues. [#128557](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128557) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Add `can be tested` label to approved PRs and to PRs of automated agents. [#128559](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128559) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Tag 05339_parquet_top_k_string_statistics as no-parallel. [#128565](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128565) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Keep the single-allocation limit in `clickhouse_fuzzer` without leak detection. [#128569](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128569) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Block other stack-unwinding signals in the `clickhouse_fuzzer` `SIGUSR1` handler. [#128572](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128572) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Always back-quote the table of a path PUT upload so a table named `function` works. [#128581](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128581) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Add `ci-skip-new-tests-check` label to bypass the new tests check. [#128583](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128583) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Allow empty `AUTOGENERATED` regions (new page scaffolds) in the docs guard. [#128586](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128586) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Just simplification of interfaces. [#128601](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128601) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* Run EXPLAIN checks w/o parallel replicas (plan-based). [#128602](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128602) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Move the job into `JobWithPriority` instead of copying it. [#128603](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128603) ([Antonio Filipovic](https://github.com/antoniofilipovic)).
* Add --state option to label_external_contributors.py. [#128630](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128630) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* Cache the logger of `ThreadStatus` instead of looking it up for every thread. [#128649](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128649) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Register the `fmod` JIT symbol without a cast, so it is tidy-clean under musl. [#128650](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128650) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Compare `system.dns_cache` IPs order-insensitively in `test_dns_cache`. [#128652](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128652) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Implement some asynchronous metrics for MacOS (required for chdig). [#128654](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128654) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Prewarm macOS code signature verification of the clickhouse binary after linking. [#128658](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128658) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Fix `BACKUP` to tar archives and `strptime`-based parsing in musl builds, which hit the `wctomb` and `nl_langinfo` traps from inside the C library. [#128664](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128664) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Expose `USE_MUSL` in `system.build_options` and skip `test_jemalloc_percpu_arena` on musl. [#128666](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128666) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Make `test_cleanup_after_start` and `test_prometheus_protocols` independent of the libc. [#128667](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128667) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* This patch simplifies interfaces so that output blocks will be represented as an output chunk stream and will not be tied to sliding blocks, which can then be made fully const. [#128687](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128687) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* Not required. [#128704](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128704) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Revert "Fit the insert pipeline and merges into a small server's memory". [#128706](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128706) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Extended the BuzzHouse, AST fuzzer and `casa_del_dolor` fuzzers with more features: the `TimeSeries` table engine with its inner tables and the `timeSeries*` table functions, column and subcolumn codecs (`CODEC <path> (...)`, `MODIFY COLUMN ... CODEC`, `REMOVE CODEC`), `FirstApplicable` codecs (also for marks, primary key and TTL `RECOMPRESS`), the `GDeflate` codec, `SHUFFLE`, `INSERT ... RETURNING`, S3Queue and AzureQueue `after_processing` `move`/`tag` and the `exclusive` mode, the distributed cache with `SYSTEM DROP DISTRIBUTED CACHE`, Iceberg compaction settings, the `bucketed_metric_log` system log and more HTTP request headers for `url`. [#128707](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128707) ([Pedro Ferreira](https://github.com/PedroTadim)).
* Rename setting `allow_experimental_lateral_join` to `enable_lateral_join`. [#128713](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128713) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky tests 05318, 05345, 05227, 03742 seen in ClickHouse Cloud CI. [#128737](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128737) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Tag `05294_json_shared_typed_paths_subcolumns` with `no-msan`. [#128740](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128740) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Make tests pass with plan-based parallel replicas (counters, EXPLAIN, row order). [#128751](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128751) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Disable the adaptive aggregator for queries generated from PromQL. [#128764](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128764) ([Vitaly Baranov](https://github.com/vitlibar)).
* The second query of `05450_radix_uniq_exact_spill_rounded_size_hint` builds its spilled buckets in two threads, so it stays under its memory limit. [#128766](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128766) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The test `04011_predicate_statistics_log` now keeps the randomized `index_granularity_bytes` at its default or above, so the 8192-row granules its Q6 consistency check relies on are not shrunk by the runner. [#128769](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128769) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Plan-based parallel replicas: do not switch off filter fusion into `ARRAY JOIN`. [#128771](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128771) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Raise the time budget of the merge-queue targeted checks to 30 minutes. [#128772](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128772) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* La Casa Del Dolor: report startup failures, fix encrypted disk paths, avoid false shutdown failures. [#128774](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128774) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* ClickHouse Iceberg Catalog: Support stage-create. [#128786](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128786) ([Asya Shneerson](https://github.com/asya-ch)).
* Run six tests with parallel replicas and in a Replicated database again. [#128787](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128787) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Add missing standard includes for libc++ 23. [#128841](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128841) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Discard the result of `try_lock_for` explicitly in `ProfiledLocks`. [#128842](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128842) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Use `std::to_address` instead of `base` of vector iterators. [#128843](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128843) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Return `std::unique_lock` objects from `ReplicatedMergeTreeQueue::lockQueue`. [#128844](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128844) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Remove redundant `String` conversions and casts reported by clang-tidy 23. [#128845](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128845) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Use `std::min<size_t>` in `ReadBufferFromAzureBlobStorage`. [#128846](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128846) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Use `getAllOnesValue` for the initial value of JIT-compiled `groupBitAnd`. [#128847](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128847) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Relax the `FileStatus` cache weight bound in `test_metadata_cache_exact_size_tracking`. [#128850](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128850) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* La Casa Del Dolor: keep Azure strict and min upload part sizes within max_upload_part_size. [#128857](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128857) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix failures of the tests `04105_system_pause_view` and `04319_system_stop_all_background` when the database metadata is on object storage, where the check that the refresh is running landed before the refresh had read its first row. [#128859](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128859) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* ### Description. [#128881](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128881) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `05447_mysql_prepared_statement_metadata` test now takes its socket timeout from the test timeout instead of a fixed 30 s. [#128886](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128886) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reject UNIQUE KEY on disks that do not support writing with append. [#128898](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128898) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Honour the upstream `ci-relocation-headroom-ok` label in private sync PRs. [#128901](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128901) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Single node EXPLAIN PLAN tree = 1: draw plans as boxes. [#128911](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128911) ([Diego Gomes Tomé](https://github.com/diegomestre2)).
* Use `hash_join_layout` in performance tests and drop the largest `parallel_hash_join_threshold` special case. [#128912](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128912) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The two queries of the test `02302_defaults_in_columnar_formats` that cover the native Parquet reader now pin `input_format_parquet_use_native_reader_v3 = 1`, so release branches that still randomize this setting no longer run them on the legacy reader. [#128946](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128946) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Drop the Build Praktika for PyPI job. [#128965](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128965) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* Do not echo dictionary source file content in YAMLRegExpTree error. [#128978](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128978) ([Han Fei](https://github.com/hanfei1991)).
* Fix a hang of the test `05332_columns_cache_memory_owned_by_server` when an earlier query on the same server cached the hash table size of `numbers(10)`. [#128981](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128981) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* La Casa Del Dolor: give a restarted server 180 s to start. [#128984](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/128984) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add 5 tests for coverage gaps in `src/Client`, `src/Parsers`, `src/Storages`. [#129029](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129029) ([ClickGap AI Bot](https://github.com/clickgapai)).
* Reject a non-finite float before converting it to an integer. [#129034](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129034) ([Yakov Olkhovskiy](https://github.com/yakov-olkhovskiy)).
* La Casa Del Dolor: do not symbolize trace_log in-flush on sanitizer builds. [#129036](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129036) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Wait for all server ports at once in the stress test, add the missing ones. [#129046](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129046) ([Pedro Ferreira](https://github.com/PedroTadim)).
* Enforce storage-backed read buffer padding. [#129052](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129052) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Forbid using a lightweight snapshot as the base of an incremental backup. [#129055](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129055) ([Han Fei](https://github.com/hanfei1991)).
* The AST fuzzer job no longer reports `error` when a fuzzer pass finishes before the runner finds the client's PID. [#129073](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129073) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add tests with the golden Delta tables of delta-kernel-rs. [#129093](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129093) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Add Delta Lake tests with tables from delta-rs and duckdb-delta. [#129094](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129094) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Add Delta Lake tests from the delta-kernel-rs test tables. [#129095](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129095) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* <!-- CI automatic block start :ci_links: -->. [#129096](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129096) ([Alexei Fedotov](https://github.com/leshikus)).
* Add Delta Lake reader tests with tables from the Trino test suite. [#129097](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129097) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Add Delta Lake tables to the data lake corpus and tests of Delta Lake features. [#129100](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129100) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Add `can be tested` only to approved PRs of external contributors. [#129101](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129101) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix typo in file name: enciding -> encoding. [#129129](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129129) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Keep real format strings in log messages instead of `{}`. [#129136](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129136) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fix typo in Docker entrypoint comment. [#129142](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129142) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Test that a text index matches a startsWith/endsWith needle literally. [#129147](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129147) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Hold the query slot with a connected client in `test_read_temporary_tables_on_failure`. [#129151](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129151) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required: changes only the agent instructions in `.claude/`. [#129167](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129167) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* ClickHouse Iceberg Catalog: Improve connection handling. [#129179](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129179) ([Asya Shneerson](https://github.com/asya-ch)).
* A query over an object-storage `*Cluster` table function no longer fails with `UNKNOWN_FUNCTION` on the replicas when its filter contains a lambda whose body was JIT-compiled. [#129182](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129182) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* [UpSync]: Sync critical files. [#129187](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129187) ([clickhouse-cloud-ci[bot]](https://github.com/apps/clickhouse-cloud-ci)).
* The test `05043_crash_report_without_global_context` now sends its simulated fault to the parked main thread with `tgkill` instead of signalling the whole process, so the signal listener can no longer take it. [#129202](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129202) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#129206](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129206) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required (test-only change). [#129210](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129210) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#129212](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129212) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not for changelog. [#129217](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129217) ([Christoph Viebig](https://github.com/cv4g)).
* Plan-based parallel replicas: do not ship a read of a temporary table. [#129223](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129223) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Bound the wait for a sanitizer report in `stop_clickhouse`, use a monotonic clock in La Casa Del Dolor. [#129227](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129227) ([Pedro Ferreira](https://github.com/PedroTadim)).
* Extract `writeRange` / `readRange` helpers for `minmax_*.idx` files. [#129234](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129234) ([Den Kalantaevskii](https://github.com/Diskein)).
* The integration tests runner image now uses `pyarrow` 26.0.1, so a PyIceberg upsert no longer aborts the data lake fuzzer run. [#129243](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129243) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* This patch adds a window aggregate function interface execution strategy and implements `prefix` and `recalculate` strategies with dispatch based on the used frame type. [#129257](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129257) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* Fix SeaweedFS setup failing on a repeated local stateless run. [#129259](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129259) ([Arsen Muk](https://github.com/arsenmuk)).
* Make the perf result digest opt-in, disabled by default. [#129279](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129279) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Rearrange the WHATIF and hypothetical objects code. [#129281](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129281) ([Yaroslav Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Human description: I need to translate ActionDAG, etc to CUDA. There are 2 ways: 1) write CUDA code again 2) use our JIT infrastructure and convert it into NVPTX. Doc: https://llvm.org/docs/NVPTXUsage.html. [#129288](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129288) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* The test `05448_dictionary_clickhouse_source_update_field_time_zone` now runs in about 5 seconds instead of about 49. [#129298](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129298) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Remove performance test queries that are also measured in another file. [#129303](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129303) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Collect per-query performance coverage, balance its 16 shards using instrumented runtimes, and select performance tests from PR code changes. [#129327](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129327) ([egor romanov](https://github.com/egor-click)).
* Not required: CI-only change. [#129330](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129330) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `insert_statistics_countmin` performance test sets `async_insert = 0`, so its 100-row INSERTs no longer go through the asynchronous insert queue and every measured run writes its parts in the query itself. [#129333](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129333) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Clear libfiu's fast-path flag when the last fail point is disabled. [#129334](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129334) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* <!-- CI automatic block start :ci_links: -->. [#129345](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129345) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* The `read_in_order_through_spilling_join` performance test writes its tables in the same layout on every server, so both servers of a comparison do the same work. [#129359](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129359) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Pack the AddressSanitizer stack depot in `native_reader_fuzzer`. [#129365](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129365) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add a `disabled` attribute to performance test queries, and disable five verbatim duplicates. [#129366](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129366) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Require the complete organization member list in `autoassign_approvers.py`. [#129376](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129376) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The `numbers_mt` queries of the `hash_table_sizes_stats_small` performance test run with `max_threads = 4`, so from 300000 rows on they read, aggregate and output in one stream per thread. [#129379](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129379) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* La Casa Del Dolor: keep the Azure list_object_keys_size within a 32-bit int. [#129382](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129382) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `count_from_formats` performance test writes the files of the formats whose `count()` spends the largest share of its time in the kernel's page-cache copy (`TabSeparated*`, `BSONEachRow`, `TSKV`, `CSV*`, `Values`) first in its fill. The measured queries are unchanged. [#129384](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129384) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `dynamic_field_serialization_cache` performance test runs its query with 2 threads on 262144 rows, each thread reading half of the part, so its timing depends much less on which server process runs it. [#129387](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129387) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `bitmapHasAny` worst-case queries of the `bitmap_cardinality` performance test use operands of two bitset containers per side instead of 46, and 23x more rows, so the data each row scans fits the L1 cache. [#129388](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129388) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Praktika: update submodules in parallel when creating the submodule cache. [#129390](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129390) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* ClickHouse Iceberg Catalog: Add rename table endpoint. [#129391](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129391) ([Asya Shneerson](https://github.com/asya-ch)).
* Docs: add mikelewisCH to the docs reference regeneration reviewers. [#129394](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129394) ([Mike Lewis](https://github.com/mikelewisCH)).
* Run `xmllint` in the style check without a shell. [#129397](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129397) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Keep a compound function name parsable when one of its parts contains a dot. [#129405](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129405) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test `05494_iceberg_single_manifest_per_insert`: find the current snapshot's manifest list through the table metadata instead of by file modification time. [#129407](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129407) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Pull the replication log before `MOVE PART ... TO SHARD` checks the part (flaky `05495_part_moves_between_shards_step_retry`). [#129409](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129409) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* ClickHouse Iceberg catalog: multi-table commits. [#129414](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129414) ([Asya Shneerson](https://github.com/asya-ch)).
* Not required. [#129416](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129416) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add 7 tests for coverage gaps in 5 components. [#129425](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129425) ([ClickGap AI Bot](https://github.com/clickgapai)).
* Not required: CI-only change. [#129429](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129429) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#129437](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129437) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The fake exchange peer of the streaming exchange unit tests now resets a connection only after the source acknowledged every byte sent to it, so the reset can no longer cut the `SinkHello` short. [#129442](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129442) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* <!-- CI automatic block start :ci_links: -->. [#129455](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129455) ([Harikrishnan Prabakaran](https://github.com/harikrishnan94)).
* The test `05217_aggregation_in_order_fixed_keys` now keeps the randomized `merge_max_block_size` at or above the default 8192, so its `OPTIMIZE ... FINAL` no longer stalls on a draw near 1. [#129456](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129456) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* CI: drop the Copilot backend from the AI code-review job and move it to a dedicated runner. [#129463](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129463) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* Sync shared files changed by the private `AmberMap` PR. [#129464](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129464) ([Miсhael Stetsyuk](https://github.com/mstetsyuk)).
* More random disruptions in the stress test. [#129515](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129515) ([Pedro Ferreira](https://github.com/PedroTadim)).
* Extract the per-part `MergeTree` index analysis into `analyzeSinglePart`. [#129540](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129540) ([Antonio Filipovic](https://github.com/antoniofilipovic)).
* Remove HTTP debug log with a heap address. [#129559](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129559) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* La Casa Del Dolor: do not give an encrypted disk a path on a readonly disk. [#129576](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129576) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* CI only: `ClickHouseVersion.__hash__` now hashes the fields that `__eq__` compares, so equal versions hash equal. [#129594](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129594) ([Hafiar](https://github.com/0xABCD01)).
* CI only: `Utils.traverse_paths` now honors glob patterns in `exclude_paths` and forwards `not_exists_ok` for missing include paths. [#129595](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129595) ([Hafiar](https://github.com/0xABCD01)).
* Sync the named collections encryption key interface from the private repository. [#129601](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129601) ([Julia Kartseva](https://github.com/jkartseva)).
* Execute scalar subqueries in `EXPLAIN AST optimize = 1`. [#129606](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129606) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Bound the waits of the gRPC fatal error callback on the signal listener thread. [#129608](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129608) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#129610](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129610) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Apply `-WithMemoryTracking` containers to `src/Processors/QueryPlan`. [#129617](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129617) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Fix three `librdkafka` data races that abort `amd_tsan` Kafka tests. [#129623](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129623) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Run the single-threaded prefetch_in_aggregation performance queries on 20 million rows. [#129625](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129625) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the bfloat16 performance test queries on 300 million rows. [#129626](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129626) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Replace the window function performance queries above 1 s with smaller copies. [#129627](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129627) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the long sparse_grams performance queries on fewer rows. [#129628](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129628) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* SQLStorm: run original queries that master supports, drop needless rewrite rules. [#129639](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129639) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Close the insecure gRPC channel in `test_grpc_protocol_ssl` when it never becomes ready, so it no longer delays gRPC connections of later tests on the same worker. [#129651](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129651) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The Iceberg configuration of the performance tests no longer runs `prewhere_subcolumn_read_steps`, which has a `JSON` column, and `lazy_final_aggregation_in_order`, which asserts the number of MergeTree parts. [#129658](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129658) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add 8 tests for coverage gaps in `src/Functions`, `src/Parsers`, `src/Processors`, `src/Storages`. [#129662](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129662) ([ClickGap AI Bot](https://github.com/clickgapai)).
* Refactor: share the traversal of ActionsDAG nodes between its walks. [#129671](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129671) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Fix flaky test `05401_quota_on_profile_events_query_result_cache`: the reads of its quota user no longer use parallel replicas. [#129672](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129672) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `Build profile diff` reports a readiness probe that connected to the CI logs cluster and then timed out as a cluster outage (green, nothing compared) instead of failing the check. [#129675](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129675) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add tests for in-order optimizations and primary key analysis over a `CAST` into `Bool`. [#129683](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129683) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Point `LLVM_PROFILE_FILE` at `/dev/null` on the per-test coverage lanes. [#129684](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129684) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The test `05484_grpc_client_gone_before_final_result` no longer fails when `SYSTEM FLUSH LOGS text_log` takes more than 60 s on a loaded server. [#129685](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129685) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add 8 tests for coverage gaps in 6 components. [#129691](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129691) ([ClickGap AI Bot](https://github.com/clickgapai)).
* The test `05292_part_log_remove_part_on_retried_drop_table` now waits for its table to finish loading before it arms the failpoint, so the table load can no longer consume it. [#129699](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129699) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The Fast test now fails when a test added by the PR runs longer than 15 seconds, or 7.5 seconds if it is tagged `no-parallel`. [#129716](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129716) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Remove unused loadDataPartForRemovalIfExists, add helpers to remove a part by its storage. [#129752](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129752) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Encapsulate the selection of `FirstApplicable` in `CompressionCodecFirstApplicable`. [#129760](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129760) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* This patch removes `getDefaultFrame` and `checkWindowFrameType` from the window function interface and replaces them with the proper declaration of window function requirements. [#129783](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129783) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* Bump `silk` to the latest `clickhouse-public`. [#129800](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129800) ([clickhouse-gh[bot]](https://github.com/apps/clickhouse-gh)).
* Ship a `LIMIT` without `ORDER BY` to replicas in plan-based parallel replicas. [#129811](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129811) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Find nested worktrees in `clean-old-worktrees-global`. [#129824](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129824) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Stop the `Pretty` squash thread when its output format is cancelled. [#129825](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129825) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Do not take submodules of worktrees for nested worktrees in `clean-old-worktrees-global`. [#129854](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129854) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The performance comparison report is now built when no query ran on both servers, so the Iceberg jobs of a pull request that changes only performance tests no longer fail with `Errors while building the report.`. [#129874](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129874) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test `05457_dictionary_remote_clickhouse_invalidate_query`. [#129879](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129879) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Do not read a `Merge` table in order when its child plan runs distributed. [#129885](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129885) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* AST fuzzer oracle: record the data version of `Memory` and `Log`-family tables. [#129900](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129900) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The test `05292_regexp_local_cache_profile_events` now selects its three measured `system.query_log` rows by their exact `log_comment`, so a concurrent flush can no longer add the row of the test's own `SYSTEM FLUSH LOGS`. [#129905](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129905) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The data lake fuzzer no longer reports a false content mismatch for an empty string in a Hudi partition column. [#129922](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129922) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The data lake fuzzer records a Rust panic of a foreign tool (delta-rs, PyIceberg) as a rejected operation instead of ending the run. [#129924](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129924) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the non-sanitizer unit tests on the `amd_debug` build in pull requests, and let the targeted integration jobs select `test_ytsaurus`. [#129927](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129927) ([Alexey Bakharew](https://github.com/alexbakharew)).
* Fix flaky test 04653_packed_part_size_accounting_no_read_in_commit. [#129938](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129938) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* La Casa Del Dolor now configures the server with the in-network ports of nginx, Redis, MongoDB and Kafka, so the `web` disk and the `URL`, `Redis`, `MongoDB` and `Kafka` tables it generates are reachable. [#129956](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129956) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test 05508_quota_on_profile_events_exempt_outer_scalar_subquery. [#129987](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/129987) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add Iceberg tables from the Trino test suite as stateless tests. [#130014](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130014) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Clamp random `index_granularity` in test `00992_system_parts_race_condition_zookeeper_long`. [#130034](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130034) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test `05566_top_k_filter_moves_same_column_condition_to_prewhere`. [#130035](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130035) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add 7 tests for coverage gaps in 6 components. [#130036](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130036) ([ClickGap AI Bot](https://github.com/clickgapai)).
* Update README.md. [#130043](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130043) ([Zoe Steinkamp](https://github.com/zoesteinkamp)).
* Revert "Revert "Search long integer slices in 512-byte chunks on AArch64 in `hasAll` and `hasAny`"". [#130046](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130046) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Use `sipHash128` instead of `MD5` in the stateless test `05565_uniq_exact_variadic_chunked_keys`, so it also passes when OpenSSL FIPS mode is enabled. [#130141](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130141) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Do not consult the query condition cache under a masking policy. [#130148](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130148) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An `INNER JOIN` whose `ON` is split into several clauses and contains an `OR` without join keys, such as `ON (a.k = b.k OR a.k2 = b.k2) AND (a.x = 1 OR b.y = 2)`, works again with `join_algorithm = 'auto'`. Closes [#130122](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/130122). [#130150](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130150) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Do not run `00673_subquery_prepared_set_performance` with parallel replicas. [#130153](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130153) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix Iceberg manifest-list pruning skipping NULL partitions for `ifNull`/`coalesce` filters. [#130158](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130158) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky `test_load_balancing_default`. [#130175](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130175) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Upgrade check: download previous-release packages from packages.clickhouse.com when GitHub cannot serve them. [#130181](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130181) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Do not read a disk's space twice when a `least_used` reservation on a `JBOD` volume fails on it. [#130198](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130198) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Perf CI: give the dashboard gate the uploads' window when the job is late. [#130199](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130199) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* check_ci.py: ask groeneai to investigate failures instead of offering to create issues. [#130217](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130217) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Not required: test-only change. [#130220](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130220) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The regression is not in a release yet. [#130223](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130223) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Set TSan `memory_limit_mb` for the stress test server. [#130226](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130226) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Raise the docker-library readiness default to 10 probes. [#130243](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130243) ([Alexei Fedotov](https://github.com/leshikus)).
* Fix the flaky test `05022_client_slash_command_hints`. [#130253](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130253) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* La Casa Del Dolor: ignore a forced end-of-run stop on sanitizer builds. [#130304](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130304) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add `actueleai` to `AGENT_AUTHORS` in `autoassign_approvers.py`. [#130324](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130324) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Do cancellation checks periodically in recalculated window aggregate functions. [#130328](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130328) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* Filter merge-queue jobs by changed files. [#130347](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130347) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* Fix ORC `TIMESTAMP` values before the first time zone transition. [#130354](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130354) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Ship the preliminary `DISTINCT` to replicas in plan-based parallel replicas. [#130355](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130355) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Not required: fuzzer-only change. [#130358](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130358) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Apply `-WithMemoryTracking` containers to the rest of `src/Processors/QueryPlan`. [#130369](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130369) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Add `ExtractColumnsStep::clone` and "Not-ready Set" regression tests. [#130373](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130373) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Let subplans extracted by plan optimizations inherit limits and resources. [#130378](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130378) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Remove `chcache`. [#130379](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130379) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Update grpc to 1.85.1. [#130389](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130389) ([Vitaly Baranov](https://github.com/vitlibar)).
* Perf CI: read the 13-column `changed-perf.tsv` in `read_slower_queries`. [#130412](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130412) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Review skill: check what `deserialize` assumes about its input. [#130433](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130433) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Destroy pooled HTTP connections outside the HTTP pool locks. [#130446](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130446) ([Nikolai Ovchinnikov](https://github.com/kolsha)).
* Filter `system.text_log` by `event_time` in tests. [#130490](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130490) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Not required. [#130493](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130493) ([Pedro Ferreira](https://github.com/PedroTadim)).
* Tests: split the filesystem cache dashboards out of 03540_system_dashboards. [#130498](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130498) ([Kseniia Sumarokova](https://github.com/kssenii)).
* Check the class of source columns in merges and squashing instead of corrupting memory. [#130522](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130522) ([Pavel Kruglov](https://github.com/Avogar)).
* Not for changelog. [#130523](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130523) ([Ilya Yatsishin](https://github.com/qoega)).
* An `INSERT` into Google Cloud Storage with `s3_create_new_file_on_insert = 1` no longer fails when the file is larger than `s3_max_single_part_upload_size`. [#130525](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130525) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Revert "Make a table read by a scalar subquery in a CONSTRAINT a loading dependency". [#130531](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130531) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Install `pigz` in the `clickhouse/binary-builder` image, so the release build jobs compress their `.tgz` packages in parallel. [#130545](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130545) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Record the lld link time trace at 50 ms granularity. [#130583](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130583) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Forbid ATTACH TABLE full definition in cloud mode. [#130601](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130601) ([Yaroslav Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Raise the script read timeout in three executable tests flaky on the macOS Fast test. [#130639](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130639) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix timeout of `05617_filesystem_cache_system_dashboards` under TSan with S3 storage. [#130647](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130647) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Raise ZooKeeper session timeout of the kerberized Kafka broker in integration tests. [#130648](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130648) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Stop the AST fuzzer oracles from reporting false mismatches for a query with `LIMIT` in a subquery when `make_distributed_plan` is enabled. [#130656](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130656) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#130670](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130670) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Debug and sanitizer builds no longer abort with `IAggregateFunction::merge called with the same source and destination state` when a window function copies a zero-size aggregate state. [#130678](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130678) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The data lake fuzzer no longer reports a false `filter` mismatch for PyIceberg tables partitioned by `bucket` over a decimal column. [#130684](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130684) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Validate the retry strategy of an S3 client clone. [#130686](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130686) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Print only the profile event totals in `05141_parquet_time_stats_pruning` and `05361_parquet_geometry_logical_type_interop`. [#130688](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130688) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Log a timed-out cleanup status poll of a distributed plan task at Warning. [#130697](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130697) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Retry transient S3 errors in praktika for up to 3 minutes. [#130710](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130710) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix the flaky resource soak tests of `test_database_iceberg` on ThreadSanitizer builds. [#130714](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130714) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The praktika repo-state notes printed before and after each CI job no longer check the work trees of submodules. In CI that check took 42-76 s at the end of many build jobs, while the test jobs that need those builds were waiting. [#130715](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130715) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Praktika now compresses artifacts with `zstd -T0` (all cores) instead of one worker: compressing `unit_tests_dbms`, about 20 s in each debug and sanitizer build job, took about 3 s in a local measurement with 32 workers. [#130722](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130722) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#130728](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130728) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Carry the packed `String` join key settings in the serialized query plan. [#130750](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130750) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Replace the arm-unstable `arrayReduce('avg')` query of the `vectorize_aggregation_combinators` performance test. [#130757](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130757) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Upgrade check: ignore the expected checksum mismatch of uncompressed part files after the upgrade. [#130760](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130760) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* LLVM coverage: exclude brace-only lines, throwing statements and fuzzer/benchmark tools. [#130761](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130761) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Add a TPC-DS performance test over Iceberg. [#130763](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130763) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Mark `05494_join_hashed_keys` as `no-msan`. [#130766](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130766) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Cache resolved streams in wide part writer. [#130784](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130784) ([Kirill Shokhin](https://github.com/kirillshokhin)).
* introduce foundation for merge checkpoints. [#130796](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130796) ([Den Kalantaevskii](https://github.com/Diskein)).
* Build `clickhouse-examples` without ThinLTO, in the `arm_binary` build. [#130824](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130824) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Split the packed `String` key join tests so that they finish under MSan. [#130856](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130856) ([Harikrishnan Prabakaran](https://github.com/harikrishnan94)).
* Run only the fast test in draft pull requests. [#130857](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130857) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Pin `max_bytes_before_external_group_by` off in `04909_group_by_top_k_freeze_after_eviction_burst` and `05241_low_cardinality_serialized_group_by`, whose freeze assertions fail when CI randomizes a small spill threshold. [#130860](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130860) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An aggregation over an object-storage `*Cluster` table function no longer loses groups when `HAVING`, or an outer `WHERE` over the aggregation, filters a `GROUP BY` key. [#130861](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130861) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Pack the AddressSanitizer stack depot in `serialization_dynamic_fuzzer`. [#130875](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130875) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix flaky test `05315_blob_storage_log_copy_event`. [#130876](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130876) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Not required. [#130877](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130877) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Pack the AddressSanitizer stack depot in `serialization_dynamic_fuzzer`. [#130880](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130880) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Link two fuzzers at a time in Build (arm_fuzzers). [#130902](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130902) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Bump `silk` to the latest `clickhouse-public`. [#130912](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130912) ([clickhouse-gh[bot]](https://github.com/apps/clickhouse-gh)).
* Remove `ppa:ubuntu-toolchain-r/test` from `binary-builder` image. [#130938](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130938) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Do not erase a foreign entry of the logger map in `LoggerDeleter`. [#130954](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130954) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Do not let exceptions unwind through `dl_iterate_phdr` in `SymbolIndex`. [#130955](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130955) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Do not cache the format schema directory in a static variable. [#130956](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130956) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Reset `NamedCollectionFactory` on shutdown. [#130957](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130957) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Restart the process-wide `TextLog` queue for a new `TextLog`. [#130958](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130958) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Shut down every system log even if one of them throws. [#130959](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130959) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Move `REGISTER_FUNCTION` out of anonymous namespaces. [#130962](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130962) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Run the full performance suite when a PR changes too many files for selection. [#130967](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130967) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Skip an unreadable `/etc/ld.so.preload` like the dynamic loader does. [#130981](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130981) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Insert into the `Join` tables of `05494_join_hashed_keys` without parallel replicas. [#130995](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/130995) ([Harikrishnan Prabakaran](https://github.com/harikrishnan94)).
* The adaptive aggregator (`enable_adaptive_aggregator`, enabled by default) is faster for `GROUP BY` with many distinct keys at low thread counts: it stages records in larger chunks when few threads aggregate, and its merge requests every next chunk of records ahead. On ClickBench Q32 and Q33 and a variant of Q32 with 16-byte keys, the geometric mean speedup is 1.06x at 4 threads and 1.04x at 8 threads, up to 1.08x, with peak memory within 2%; from 16 threads on, speed and memory stay the same. [#131003](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131003) ([Nihal Z. Miaji](https://github.com/nihalzp)).
* Cache resolved streams in wide part reader. [#131011](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131011) ([Kirill Shokhin](https://github.com/kirillshokhin)).
* Revert "Revert "Sync shared files changed by the private AmberMap PR"". [#131033](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131033) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Fix TOO_SLOW in external aggregation overflow any tests. [#131034](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131034) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Pin index granularity in `05571` and `05572` tests. [#131040](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131040) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Review skill: walk the input of a fixed bug through the touched functions. [#131044](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131044) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Revert CI regressions on medium-confidence verdicts too. [#131047](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131047) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Move single-server Iceberg integration tests to stateless tests. [#131052](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131052) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Fix SIGSEGV (in <26.10) when format_schema query reads a MongoDB table. [#131055](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131055) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Authenticate coverage-based performance test selection with a read-only GitHub job token. [#131070](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131070) ([egor romanov](https://github.com/egor-click)).
* The PREWHERE effectiveness profile events (`GranulesReadByPrewhereReaders`, `GranulesSkippedByPrewhereReaders`, `RowsSkippedByPrewhereReaders`) no longer count the internal dynamic filters of `ORDER BY ... LIMIT` (top-K) and join runtime filters; a query without a `PREWHERE` now reports zero for them. [#131075](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131075) ([olegkkruglov](https://github.com/olegkkruglov)).
* Parse the HTTP response header with an incremental parser. [#131086](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131086) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Sync the backup writer's native-copy interface. [#131137](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131137) ([Julia Kartseva](https://github.com/jkartseva)).
* Use `sipHash128` instead of `MD5` in test `05627_prewhere_guard_sibling_subcolumn`. [#131147](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131147) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Make the `rand` performance test call the functions it measures. [#131157](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131157) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Update the descriptions of the packed `String` join key settings. [#131171](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131171) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Write release reports, CIDB rows and the changelog only in ClickHouse/ClickHouse. [#131179](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131179) ([Alexei Fedotov](https://github.com/leshikus)).
* Run the join_lazy_indexing and three hash_join_with_row_store performance queries on smaller tables. [#131196](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131196) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* [UpSync]: Sync critical files. [#131199](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131199) ([clickhouse-cloud-ci[bot]](https://github.com/apps/clickhouse-cloud-ci)).
* Make test `05320_queries_quota_interval_rollover` sleep until the end of its quota interval instead of a fixed time, so slow queries do not push it out of the next interval. [#131217](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131217) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#131220](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131220) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#131241](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131241) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Raise the script read timeout in `05618_executable_check_exit_code_limit`, flaky on the macOS Fast test. [#131250](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131250) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the ascii performance test queries with format Null. [#131252](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131252) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Remove dead `ENABLE_ORC` and `ORC_MAGIC_BYTES` leftovers. [#131253](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131253) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Give both servers one part in the `low_cardinality_argument` performance test, and drop its tables after it. [#131258](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131258) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Remove two redundant server log waits from `test_streaming_engines_definer_interserver_secret::test_rabbitmq` that timed out when the RabbitMQ setup line scrolled out of their look-behind window. [#131260](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131260) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Re-enable the adaptive aggregator for queries generated from PromQL. [#131270](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131270) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Refresh the GCP OAuth bearer token under `FiberMutex` in Silk thread mode. [#131271](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131271) ([Nikolai Ovchinnikov](https://github.com/kolsha)).
* Keep `blob_storage_log` connection info per fiber. [#131273](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131273) ([Nikolai Ovchinnikov](https://github.com/kolsha)).
* Revert [#129096](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/129096): hardcode the CI DB read URL again. [#131283](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131283) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* AI review: check preconditions, warn on stopped sessions. [#131284](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131284) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Choose the HTTP transport per S3 request and reject cooperative HTTPS through tunneling proxies. [#131303](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131303) ([Nikolai Ovchinnikov](https://github.com/kolsha)).
* Keep performance coverage usable after individual test failures, relax coverage timeouts, and refresh shard weights. [#131309](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131309) ([egor romanov](https://github.com/egor-click)).
* Data lake fuzzer: do not partition a Delta table by all columns. [#131312](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131312) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Run only ASan and tidy builds plus one ASan test suite while review threads are unresolved. [#131319](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131319) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Sync private. [#131324](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131324) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* Read sources for performance test selection from git, not the GitHub API. [#131332](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131332) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Add tests for an early error response while an HTTP request body is sent. [#131333](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131333) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Restore `current_thread` when the `ThreadStatus` constructor throws. [#131334](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131334) ([Nikolai Ovchinnikov](https://github.com/kolsha)).
* Pin `enable_parallel_single_level_merge` in `03634_autopr_output_bytes_estimation`. [#131358](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131358) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Not required. [#131362](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131362) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Let backport PR builds from this repository write to the shared sccache. [#131392](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131392) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
