* Fix a typo in a comment in `HTTPServerResponse`. [#131394](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131394) ([Alexei Fedotov](https://github.com/leshikus)).
* Revert "Forbid renaming the source of incremental refreshable views". [#131424](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131424) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Not required. [#131426](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131426) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add a nightly performance test: TPC-DS SF100 over Iceberg. [#131427](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131427) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Review skill: data read back, sizes from counts, names used as paths, two readings of one input. [#131432](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131432) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a data race on the active connection count of remote query connections. [#131436](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131436) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Pin the subquery query result cache tests to query-based parallel replicas. [#131464](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131464) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* La Casa Del Dolor: keep S3 upload parts at least 5 MiB. [#131475](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131475) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* SQLancer++: do not compare results cut short by a server error. [#131478](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131478) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Split dynamic PREWHERE filters off user predicates on the same column. [#131511](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131511) ([olegkkruglov](https://github.com/olegkkruglov)).
* Fix the race between `KILL QUERY` and the memory limit in `05315_freeform_structure_search_query_limits`. [#131525](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131525) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* ClickHouse Iceberg Catalog: Add warehouse support (part 2). [#131544](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131544) ([Asya Shneerson](https://github.com/asya-ch)).
* Revert "Build the symbol index in the background at server startup". [#131562](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131562) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* La Casa Del Dolor: run its S3 server on SeaweedFS instead of MinIO. [#131564](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131564) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Move `ScopedNonBlocking` into its own files. [#131566](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131566) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the short and variable-length string joins of hash_join_with_row_store_strings on smaller tables. [#131570](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131570) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Faster and cheaper PR CI: earlier stage 2, shorter tail, cross-compiled builds in the merge queue. [#131576](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131576) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Test-only change, no changelog entry required. [#131581](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131581) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Loop `CherryPick` for 6 hours instead of 3. [#131582](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131582) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Retry transient S3 errors when performance test selection reads a coverage manifest. [#131597](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131597) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The .NET clients of `test_postgresql_protocol::test_dotnet_client` and `test_mysql_protocol::test_mysql_dotnet_client` are now built in their Docker image, so the tests run a published binary instead of `dotnet run`. [#131603](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131603) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the codecs_t64_select performance queries on a quarter of the rows. [#131605](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131605) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `(NOT x) | f` inside `CODEC`, `STATISTICS` and `EXPLAIN SYNTAX` output is formatted as `(not(x)) | f`, so it parses back to the same query. [#131606](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131606) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* ### Description. [#131639](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131639) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make `test_select_racing_drop` deterministic with a failpoint instead of a sleep. [#131652](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131652) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run two kway_merge_expensive_comparator sorts on a quarter of the rows. [#131660](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131660) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Do not run 02810 with parallel replicas. [#131666](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131666) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Load the primary indexes of parts in parallel when deciding whether to load statistics. [#131667](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131667) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run four adaptive_aggregation_top_k_threshold queries on hits_10m_single. [#131677](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131677) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* TimeSeries: use 256 KiB granules and ALP, ZSTD(4) for the values of the samples table by default. [#131678](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131678) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Disable the query condition cache in `03634_autopr_input_bytes_estimation`. [#131690](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131690) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add `fix-ci` skill: triage all master failures and fix or find fixes. [#131692](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131692) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* When the connection thread of a query fails while the query runs, the query now reports that error instead of an error a processor raised because of the resulting cancellation. [#131695](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131695) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#131696](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131696) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `Relocation headroom` check of `Build (amd_msan)` no longer compares a pull request with an older master commit while the master parent's own build is still running. [#131700](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131700) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Plan-based parallel replicas: keep reads through `mergeTreeProjection` local. [#131701](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131701) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Fix flaky `05038_query_plan_limits_hedged_sql_fallback`: read the SQL fallback from the replica's `query_log` row, not `text_log`. [#131708](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131708) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Remove the unit tests that need the Apache Arrow C++ library. [#131712](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131712) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Not required. [#131723](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131723) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix UBSan report on unknown Parquet encodings in the `ParquetMetadata` format. [#131724](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131724) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Measure the SYSTEM CANCEL wake latency in server time in test_cancel_wakes_flush_wait_promptly. [#131725](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131725) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `05565_filesystem_isolation_dev_log` in the LLVM coverage build. [#131727](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131727) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix the per-test coverage build crashing on `SYSTEM SET COVERAGE TEST` after the switch to `clang-23`. [#131751](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131751) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Wait for the materialized view drop before `DROP USER` in test_kafka_no_holes_when_write_suffix_failed. [#131760](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131760) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Revert "Enable `-mbranch-protection=pac-ret` on ARM". [#131765](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131765) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* TimeSeries selector: filter out empty sample slices only when the time range is shorter than a bucket. [#131766](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131766) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Export the Delta Lake commit metadata without the Apache Arrow C++ library. [#131768](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131768) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Do not select performance tests from a coverage collection that covered no test. [#131772](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131772) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#131776](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131776) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Keep the rest of `/etc/ssl` in test `05637_filesystem_isolation_system_ca_directory_link`. [#131777](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131777) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the uniq_multi_place performance query for up to 1024 groups. [#131785](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131785) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A server that logs at `test` level no longer waits the whole `shutdown_wait_unfinished` for idle native protocol connections when it stops. [#131790](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131790) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Backported in [#133868](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133868): Keep the cloud credentials visible with `filesystem_isolation`. [#131811](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131811) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Accept only MergeTree sources for an incremental refresh. [#131814](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131814) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Test resuming a streaming read of ReplicatedMergeTree on another replica. [#131817](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131817) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Run a smaller copy of the external_set performance query 13. [#131824](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131824) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky `05768_statistics_probe_parallel_index_load` when a concurrent query clears the primary index cache. [#131827](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131827) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The build profile post hook skips a build that did not succeed, instead of reporting it as a broken profile producer or uploading its profile. [#131830](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131830) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Pin two more subquery query result cache tests to query-based parallel replicas. [#131852](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131852) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Run the text_index_postprocessor_filter performance inserts on 500k rows. [#131857](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131857) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the group_by_dynamic_keys performance queries on 400k rows. [#131871](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131871) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix the flaky resource soak tests of `test_database_iceberg` on MemorySanitizer builds. [#131873](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131873) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Submit a GitHub approval when the AI review approves the PR. [#131876](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131876) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* A server with `filesystem_isolation` no longer fails to start when something has a mount of its new root open for writing at the moment that mount is made read-only: the remount waits up to three seconds for the writer. [#131877](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131877) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make the test `05768_statistics_probe_parallel_index_load` pass when a concurrent query clears the primary index cache while the test loads the indexes. [#131886](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131886) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the polymorphic_parts_l Buffer insert on 20 parts. [#131888](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131888) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Perf CI: compare executable bases modulo the largest segment alignment. [#131889](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131889) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix clang-tidy on master: mark `ParserTransactionControl::getDocumentation` `[[nodiscard]]`. [#131891](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131891) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Tag `04692_insert_dedup_retry_sparse_tuple` as `no-parallel-replicas`. [#131894](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131894) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the polymorphic_parts_m performance inserts on 20 parts. [#131896](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131896) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the uniq_states_read_merge performance queries above 1 s on a quarter of the states. [#131912](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131912) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test `test_backup_restore_on_cluster/test.py::test_async_backups_to_same_destination`. [#131921](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131921) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Bound the read batch of `keeper-bench --storage`. [#131926](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131926) ([Shaohua Wang](https://github.com/tiandiwonder)).
* Run the insert_values_with_expressions performance query above 1 s on the first 10000 rows. [#131933](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131933) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the settings-history style check on backport CI. [#131954](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131954) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* This patch moves `add*Step` functions and `QueryAnalysisResult` into separate files so they can be used in the streaming queries planner. [#131971](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/131971) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* The commented-out `hide sensitive HTTP query-string parameters` example of `query_masking_rules` in `config.xml` now works when uncommented. [#132002](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132002) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* AI review: submit the GitHub approval without a body. [#132030](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132030) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix JIT miscompile of `0 / x` after the update to LLVM 23. [#132041](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132041) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a crash of `unit_tests_dbms` in the `SchedulerWorkloadResourceManager` tests caused by two different global `ResourceTest` fixtures. [#132058](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132058) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `RENAME COLUMN` when the key uses a subcolumn of an independent dotted column. [#132070](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132070) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Add an AI review check for builds that weaken security hardening. [#132090](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132090) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Keep the identity of a set in the cache key of an expression. [#132104](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132104) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* The near-limit warning of the `Relocation headroom` check of `Build (amd_msan)` now says how to switch x86-64 MSan builds to the large code model. [#132105](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132105) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The data lake fuzzer's Hudi writer no longer has its writes refused for arrays with a NULL element. [#132108](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132108) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Declare the `backup_from_snapshot_pause_before_collecting_snapshot_file` failpoint. [#132124](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132124) ([Nikolai Ovchinnikov](https://github.com/kolsha)).
* Fix flaky test 05409_quota_on_profile_events_remote_insert_mv_dedup. [#132139](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132139) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#132149](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132149) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* This patch introduces a planner for streaming queries. This allows us not to mix implementations of batch (normal) queries and streaming semantics (mostly). [#132154](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132154) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* Not required. [#132156](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132156) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the Int128 and UInt256 array_sort_numeric performance queries on a quarter of their rows. [#132174](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132174) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#132181](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132181) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test 05507_autopr_direct_read_from_text_index. [#132192](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132192) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Read `system.query_log` in default-size blocks in `05620_read_in_order_virtual_row_fixed_key`. [#132195](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132195) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Make the `lazy_columns_replication` `arrayJoin` query measure lazy replication again. [#132196](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132196) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky `04659_connection_pool_zero_wait_blocks`: count the pool wait lines from the waiter's own server logs, not `text_log`. [#132221](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132221) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the all-unique-pattern queries of `re2_regex_caching` on a quarter of their rows. [#132233](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132233) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add long String columns with and without statistics to the hash join row store perf test. [#132249](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132249) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Run the `JSON(max_dynamic_paths = 4)` and `Dynamic` queries of `generate_random_json` on half and a third of their rows. [#132277](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132277) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add a performance test for hash joins on a single String key. [#132288](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132288) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Clone the output options of query ASTs that dropped them. [#132292](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132292) ([Pedro Ferreira](https://github.com/PedroTadim)).
* Run the `constant_column_search` queries above 1 s on `hits_10m_single`. [#132300](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132300) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Not required. [#132319](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132319) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Extract lld link time traces named `<output>.time-trace.json`. [#132334](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132334) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Do not build the PGO+BOLT toolchain in `ci-force-all` PRs without `ci-toolchain`. [#132347](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132347) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Run the `JSONEachRow` and `Avro` queries of `formats_columns_nullable` on a quarter of the rows. [#132357](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132357) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* AI review: do not approve when a session was stopped as a cybersecurity risk. [#132371](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132371) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Do not apply jemalloc profiling settings of a query to fibers. [#132408](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132408) ([Nikolai Ovchinnikov](https://github.com/kolsha)).
* Keep the sets of `IN` in memory in `03173_set_transformed_partition_pruning`. [#132435](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132435) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Set `local_filesystem_read_method` explicitly in `02910_prefetch_unexpceted_exception`. [#132437](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132437) ([Lazizbek Ergashev](https://github.com/lazerg)).
* Keep `QueryCancellationBlocker` state per fiber. [#132442](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132442) ([Nikolai Ovchinnikov](https://github.com/kolsha)).
* Gpu aggregation library. [#132443](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132443) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* Query `#1` of the `hashing_serialized_values` performance test (`sipHash64` over a `JSON` column) is disabled, because its time depends on the code layout of each build rather than on the change being tested. [#132468](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132468) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix wrong result of a join reordered by DPsub with `query_plan_join_order_use_interesting_orders` when a join key class mixed column types such as `Date` and `DateTime` in different time zones. [#132507](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132507) ([Fisnik Kastrati](https://github.com/fkastrati)).
* Build only the needed parts of the CI toolchain. [#132530](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132530) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Not required. [#132539](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132539) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `UUID` dictionary leaves and union children that the Arrow IPC reader declared with the wrong type. [#132550](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132550) ([Yakov Olkhovskiy](https://github.com/yakov-olkhovskiy)).
* Pin the compression codec in 05257_autopr_rejected_by_cost_model. [#132570](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132570) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Backported in [#134039](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/134039): Pack `String` join keys when the hash table outgrows the L2 cache. [#132572](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132572) ([Harikrishnan Prabakaran](https://github.com/harikrishnan94)).
* Remove resolved items from the Parquet reader TODO list. [#132581](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132581) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Add a performance test reading 1 KB strings from Parquet and MergeTree. [#132602](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132602) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Mark `log_query_plans` as experimental. [#132608](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132608) ([Matheus Nerone](https://github.com/Manerone)).
* Do not start up and shut down the wrapped metadata storage twice. [#132612](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132612) ([Den Kalantaevskii](https://github.com/Diskein)).
* Randomize the Parquet reader and writer settings in stateless tests. [#132712](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132712) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Share one helper for cutting a URL at its query string or fragment. [#132714](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132714) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* ### Description. [#132740](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132740) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test 05048_query_plan_json_join_statistics under parallel replicas. [#132794](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132794) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Name the innermost frame of threads blocked in a cancellable syscall in the static build. [#132810](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132810) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Link the toolchain's PGO profile collection build with the distro lld. [#132814](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132814) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Not required. [#132816](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132816) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Detect MemorySanitizer and UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer reports in integration tests. [#132826](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132826) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Do not hide setup and teardown errors of xfail integration tests. [#132827](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132827) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Let gdb pass the `system.stack_trace` signal of the musl server in fuzzer jobs. [#132857](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132857) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Pin 05829 to query-based parallel replicas. [#132867](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132867) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Pin blocks marshalling for the EXPLAIN checks of `05137_join_fixed_string_zero_bytes_runtime_filter` and blacklist it for plan-based parallel replicas. [#132869](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132869) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Pin 04161 and 05493 to query-based parallel replicas. [#132872](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132872) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Pin the Top-K limit in 05100_autopr_gate_lazy_read. [#132873](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132873) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `Sign macOS binary` retries a failed Apple notary submission request and waits up to 50 minutes for notarization instead of 30. [#132875](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132875) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Pin 05628 and 05043 to query-based parallel replicas. [#132876](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132876) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* [UpSync]: Sync critical files. [#132894](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132894) ([clickhouse-cloud-ci[bot]](https://github.com/apps/clickhouse-cloud-ci)).
* Randomise `enable_adaptive_codec_selection` in stateless and stress tests. [#132937](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132937) ([Raufs Dunamalijevs](https://github.com/rienath)).
* AI review: require an exact verdict line and parse it leniently. [#132945](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132945) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Drop unneeded no-parallel tags from seven stateless tests. [#132949](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/132949) ([Kirill Kopienko](https://github.com/smthwentroong)).
* Add PromQL tests flaky with plan-based parallel replicas to the blacklist. [#133000](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/133000) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Sync `src/Core/ServerSettings.cpp` from private repository. [#133004](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/133004) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* Fix nightly changelog job hanging in changelog.py tag fetch. [#133065](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/133065) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Pin `enable_adaptive_codec_selection` in `05293_autopr_statistics_plan_based`. [#133069](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/133069) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Backported in [#133123](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/133123): [UpSync]: Sync critical files. [#133100](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/133100) ([clickhouse-cloud-ci[bot]](https://github.com/apps/clickhouse-cloud-ci)).
